osition = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2)
	numberBox.ZIndex = 9
	numberBox.Parent = numberFrame
	numberBox.Visible = true

	local function formatValue(value)
		local text = string.format("%.2f", tonumber(value) or 0.2)
		return text:gsub("0+$", ""):gsub("%.$", "")
	end
	local function refreshNumber(value) numberBox.Text = formatValue(value) end
	refreshNumber(current)
	local function syncFromGameSettings()
		local readOK, latest = pcall(function() return GameSettings[property] end)
		if readOK and type(latest) == "number" then
			refreshNumber(latest)
			slider:SetValue(sensitivityToSlider(latest))
		end
	end
	if slider then slider.ValueChanged:Connect(syncFromGameSettings) end
	numberBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		local value = tonumber(numberBox.Text)
		if not value then
			syncFromGameSettings()
			return
		end
		value = math.clamp(value, 0.2, 4)
		local writeOK = pcall(function() GameSettings[property] = value end)
		if writeOK then
			if slider then slider:SetValue(sensitivityToSlider(value)) end
			refreshNumber(value)
		else
			syncFromGameSettings()
		end
	end)
	pcall(function()
		GameSettings:GetPropertyChangedSignal(property):Connect(syncFromGameSettings)
	end)

	if property == "MouseSensitivity" then
		this.MouseSensitivityNumberFrame, this.MouseSensitivityNumber = numberFrame, numberBox
	else
		this.GamepadSensitivityNumberFrame, this.GamepadSensitivityNumber = numberFrame, numberBox
	end
	return row, label, slider
end
local isDesktopMouse = UserInputService.MouseEnabled and UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled
if isDesktopMouse then
	this.MouseSensitivityFrame, this.MouseSensitivityLabel, this.MouseSensitivitySlider =
		addSensitivityRow("MouseSensitivity", "Camera Sensitivity")
elseif UserInputService.GamepadEnabled then
	this.GamepadSensitivityFrame, this.GamepadSensitivityLabel, this.GamepadSensitivitySlider =
		addSensitivityRow("GamepadCameraSensitivity", "Camera Sensitivity (Gamepad)")
else
	connectSignal(UserInputService.GamepadConnected, function()
		if not this.GamepadSensitivitySlider and not isDesktopMouse then
			this.GamepadSensitivityFrame, this.GamepadSensitivityLabel, this.GamepadSensitivitySlider =
				addSensitivityRow("GamepadCameraSensitivity", "Camera Sensitivity (Gamepad)")
		end
	end)
end
-- Camera and movement modes. These registered mode APIs are CoreScript-specific on some builds;
-- keep the row hidden rather than breaking the whole page if unavailable.
local PlayerScripts = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
if PlayerScripts then
	local function addRegisteredModeRow(title, isCamera)
		local getter = isCamera and (UserInputService.TouchEnabled and "GetRegisteredTouchCameraMovementModes" or "GetRegisteredComputerCameraMovementModes")
			or (UserInputService.TouchEnabled and "GetRegisteredTouchMovementModes" or "GetRegisteredComputerMovementModes")
		local ok, modes = pcall(function() return PlayerScripts[getter](PlayerScripts) end)
		if not ok or type(modes) ~= "table" or #modes == 0 then return end
		local labels, map = {}, {}
		for _, mode in ipairs(modes) do
			local display = mode.Name
			if display == "Default" then display = isCamera and (UserInputService.TouchEnabled and "Default (Follow)" or "Default (Classic)") or (UserInputService.TouchEnabled and "Default (Thumbstick)" or "Default (Keyboard)") end
			if display == "KeyboardMouse" then display = "Keyboard + Mouse" end
			if display == "ClickToMove" then display = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and "Tap to Move" or "Click to Move" end
			if display == "DynamicThumbstick" then display = "Dynamic Thumbstick" end
			table.insert(labels, display); map[display] = mode
		end
		local property = isCamera and (UserInputService.TouchEnabled and "TouchCameraMovementMode" or "ComputerCameraMovementMode")
			or (UserInputService.TouchEnabled and "TouchMovementMode" or "ComputerMovementMode")
		local current = 1
		pcall(function()
			local value = GameSettings[property].Value
			for index, mode in ipairs(modes) do if mode.Value == value then current = index; break end end
		end)
		local row, label, selector = addRow(this, title, "Selector", labels, current)
		if row then row.LayoutOrder = isCamera and 2 or 3 end
		if selector then selector.IndexChanged:Connect(function(index) local mode = map[labels[index]]; if mode then pcall(function() GameSettings[property] = mode end) end end) end
		return row, label, selector
	end
	this.CameraModeFrame, this.CameraModeLabel, this.CameraMode = addRegisteredModeRow("Camera Mode", true)
	this.MovementModeFrame, this.MovementModeLabel, this.MovementMode = addRegisteredModeRow("Movement Mode", false)
	if this.CameraModeFrame then settingsDisabledInVR[this.CameraModeFrame] = true end
	if this.MovementModeFrame then settingsDisabledInVR[this.MovementModeFrame] = true end
end

-- Shift lock: only display if the setting is writable and the device has mouse/keyboard.
if UserInputService.MouseEnabled and UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled and not isTenFootInterface then
	local controlOk, controlMode = pcall(function() return GameSettings.ControlMode end)
	if controlOk then
		this.ShiftLockFrame, this.ShiftLockLabel, this.ShiftLockMode = addRow(this, "Shift Lock Switch", "Selector", {"On", "Off"}, controlMode == Enum.ControlMode.MouseLockSwitch and 1 or 2)
		if this.ShiftLockFrame then this.ShiftLockFrame.LayoutOrder = 1; settingsDisabledInVR[this.ShiftLockFrame] = true end
		if this.ShiftLockMode then this.ShiftLockMode.IndexChanged:Connect(function(index) pcall(function() GameSettings.ControlMode = index == 1 and Enum.ControlMode.MouseLockSwitch or Enum.ControlMode.Classic end) end) end
	end
end

-- MicroProfiler setting, where supported.
if useMicroProfiler then
	local property = isMobile and "MicroProfilerWebServerEnabled" or "OnScreenProfilerEnabled"
	local ok, enabled = pcall(function() return GameSettings[property] end)
	if ok and enabled ~= nil then
		this.MicroProfilerFrame, this.MicroProfilerLabel, this.MicroProfilerMode = addRow(this, "Micro Profiler", "Selector", {"On", "Off"}, enabled and 1 or 2)
		if this.MicroProfilerFrame then this.MicroProfilerFrame.LayoutOrder = 10 end
		if this.MicroProfilerMode then this.MicroProfilerMode.IndexChanged:Connect(function(index) pcall(function() GameSettings[property] = index == 1 end) end) end
	end
end

-- VR is retained as a preference row if this client exposes the setting.
local vrOK, vrSetting = pcall(function() return GameSettings.VREnabled end)
if vrOK and vrSetting ~= nil then
	this.VREnabledFrame, this.VREnabledLabel, this.VREnabledSelector = addRow(this, "VR", "Selector", {"On", "Off"}, vrSetting and 1 or 2)
	if this.VREnabledFrame then this.VREnabledFrame.LayoutOrder = 12 end
	if this.VREnabledSelector then this.VREnabledSelector.IndexChanged:Connect(function(index) pcall(function() GameSettings.VREnabled = index == 1 end) end) end
end

-- Owner-only developer console option. Optional APIs are required lazily.
local function showDeveloperConsoleButton()
	local button, label = utility:MakeStyledButton("DevConsoleButton", "Open", UDim2.new(0,300,1,-20), function()
		local names = enableNewDevConsole and {"DevConsoleMaster"} or {"DeveloperConsoleModule"}
		local module = safeRequire(Modules, names[1]) or safeRequire(Modules, names[2])
		if module then
			pcall(function() if module.SetVisibility then module:SetVisibility(true) elseif module.ToggleVisibility then module:ToggleVisibility() end end)
		end
		local hubModule = safeRequire(SettingsModules, "SettingsHub")
		if hubModule then pcall(function() hubModule:SetVisibility(false, true) end) end
	end, this)
	if not button then return end
	if label then label.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans end
	button.Position = UDim2.new(1,-400,0,12)
	local row = utility:AddNewRowObject(this, "Developer Console", button)
	if row then row.LayoutOrder = 13 end
end
if LocalPlayer then
	-- Defer owner-only UI creation until the current ModuleScript require has
	-- completed. task.defer may begin inside SettingsHub's require chain and trigger a circular dependency.
	task.defer(function()
		local canUse = false
		if game.CreatorType == Enum.CreatorType.User then
			canUse = LocalPlayer.UserId == game.CreatorId
		elseif game.CreatorType == Enum.CreatorType.Group then
			local ok, rank = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:GetRankInGroup(game.CreatorId) end)
			canUse = ok and rank == 255
		end
		if canUse then pcall(showDeveloperConsoleButton) end
	end)
end

-- Optional console overscan UI depends on Roact/console modules; don't make the whole page fail.
if isTenFootInterface then
	pcall(function()
		local button, label = utility:MakeStyledButton("AdjustButton", "Adjust", UDim2.new(0,300,1,-20), function()
			local shell = Modules:FindFirstChild("Shell")
			if not shell then return end
			local settingsHub = safeRequire(SettingsModules, "SettingsHub")
			local overscanModule = shell:FindFirstChild("Components")
			if settingsHub and overscanModule then settingsHub:SetVisibility(false, true) end
		end, this)
		if not button then return end
		if label then label.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans end
		button.Position = UDim2.new(1,-400,0,12)
		local row = utility:AddNewRowObject(this, "Safe Zone", button)
		if row then row.LayoutOrder = 14 end
	end)
end

this.TabHeader.Name = "GameSettingsTab"
this.TabHeader.Icon.Image = isTenFootInterface and "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarIcons/GameSettingsTab@2x.png" or "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarIcons/GameSettingsTab.png"
if useNotificationsLocalization and this.TabHeader:FindFirstChild("Title") then
	this.TabHeader.Title.Text = "Settings"
elseif this.TabHeader.Icon:FindFirstChild("Title") then
	this.TabHeader.Icon.Title.Text = "Settings"
end
this.Page.ZIndex = 5
if this.PageListLayout then this.PageListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0,0) end
createdRows = true
refreshVRVisibility()

return this

                                        <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                        <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: ResetCharacter.lua
    Full-source viewport/no-scroll repair for Yeku Legacy CoreGui

    Fixes:
        - Never makes the confirmation page taller than the visible viewport.
        - Reports the visible viewport size to SettingsHub, preventing an
          oversized PageView.CanvasSize and its unwanted scrollbar.
        - Keeps the page and its content container at 100% viewport height.
        - Keeps the confirmation text above the buttons and fits short viewports.
        - Keeps Reset highlighted by default.
        - Preserves Utility-generated buttons and labels.
        - Activates the selected choice with Enter or numpad Enter.
        - Preserves ResetButtonCallback support and ButtonB cancellation.
        - Retains the page-factory and SettingsHub contracts.
]]

local RESET_CHARACTER_GAME_ACTION = "ResetCharacterAction"
local RESET_ENTER_ACTION = "ResetCharacterConfirmEnterAction"

local PRIMARY_HOVER_IMAGE =
	"rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarAssets/MenuButtonSelected.png"

local ENTER_ACTION_PRIORITY = 10000
local ENTER_DEBOUNCE_SECONDS = 0.25

local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local ContextActionService = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local PlayersService = game:GetService("Players")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")

local RobloxGui = script and script:FindFirstAncestor("NewRobloxGui")

if not (RobloxGui and RobloxGui:IsA("ScreenGui")) then
	RobloxGui = CoreGui:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 15)
end

assert(
	RobloxGui and RobloxGui:IsA("ScreenGui"),
	"[ResetCharacter] CoreGui.NewRobloxGui is missing."
)

local Modules = RobloxGui:WaitForChild("Modules", 15)
assert(Modules, "[ResetCharacter] NewRobloxGui.Modules is missing.")

local SettingsFolder = Modules:WaitForChild("Settings", 15)
assert(SettingsFolder, "[ResetCharacter] NewRobloxGui.Modules.Settings is missing.")

local utility = require(SettingsFolder:WaitForChild("Utility", 15))

local PageInstance
local LastEnterActivation = -math.huge
local ViewportSizeConnection
local CameraSizeConnection
local SavedHubScrollState = nil

-- ============================================================
-- TEN-FOOT MODE
-- ============================================================

local IsTenFootInterface = false

do
	local module = Modules:FindFirstChild("TenFootInterface")
		or Modules:FindFirstChild("TenFootInterface.lua")

	if module and module:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		local ok, api = pcall(require, module)

		if ok and type(api) == "table"
			and type(api.IsEnabled) == "function"
		then
			local enabledOK, enabled = pcall(function()
				return api:IsEnabled()
			end)

			IsTenFootInterface = enabledOK and enabled == true
		end
	else
		local value = Lighting:FindFirstChild("TenFootInterface")
		IsTenFootInterface = value ~= nil and value.Value == true
	end
end

-- ============================================================
-- VIEWPORT HELPERS
-- ============================================================

local function GetViewportObject(this)
	local hub = this and this.HubRef

	if not hub then
		return nil
	end

	-- Measure PageView first because SettingsHub compares CanvasSize against
	-- PageView.AbsoluteSize. Matching this exact size prevents even a tiny
	-- fractional overflow from enabling the vertical scrollbar.
	for _, name in ipairs({
		"PageView",
		"PageViewClipper",
		"PageClipper",
		}) do
		local ok, object = pcall(function()
			return hub[name]
		end)

		if ok
			and typeof(object) == "Instance"
			and object:IsA("GuiObject")
			and object.AbsoluteSize.X &gt; 0
			and object.AbsoluteSize.Y &gt; 0
		then
			return object
		end
	end

	return nil
end

local function GetViewportSize(this)
	local viewportObject = GetViewportObject(this)

	if viewportObject then
		local size = viewportObject.AbsoluteSize
		if size.X &gt; 0 and size.Y &gt; 0 then
			return size
		end
	end

	local page = this and this.Page
	if page then
		local size = page.AbsoluteSize
		if size.X &gt; 0 and size.Y &gt; 0 then
			return size
		end
	end

	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if camera then
		local size = camera.ViewportSize
		if size.X &gt; 0 and size.Y &gt; 0 then
			return Vector2.new(size.X, math.max(1, size.Y - 100))
		end
	end

	return Vector2.new(800, 500)
end

local function IsPageActive(this)
	if not this or not this.Page then
		return false
	end

	local page = this.Page

	if not page.Visible or not page.Parent then
		return false
	end

	if not page:IsDescendantOf(game) then
		return false
	end

	local hub = this.HubRef

	if hub then
		if hub.Visible == false then
			return false
		end

		local currentPage = hub.Pages and hub.Pages.CurrentPage
		if currentPage and currentPage ~= this then
			return false
		end
	end

	return true
end

-- SettingsHub owns one shared ScrollingFrame for every settings page.
-- Its CanvasSize is recalculated after Page:Display(), so changing GetSize()
-- alone cannot reliably remove the bar. Disable scrolling on that shared
-- viewport while this fixed-layout confirmation page is active, and restore
-- its original settings when the page hides.
local function SetHubScrollingDisabled(this, disabled)
	local hub = this and this.HubRef
	local pageView = hub and hub.PageView

	if not (pageView and pageView:IsA("ScrollingFrame")) then
		return
	end

	if disabled then
		if not SavedHubScrollState then
			local state = {}
			pcall(function() state.ScrollingEnabled = pageView.ScrollingEnabled end)
			pcall(function() state.ScrollBarThickness = pageView.ScrollBarThickness end)
			pcall(function() state.ScrollBarImageTransparency = pageView.ScrollBarImageTransparency end)
			SavedHubScrollState = state
		end

		pcall(function() pageView.ScrollingEnabled = false end)
		pcall(function() pageView.ScrollBarThickness = 0 end)
		pcall(function() pageView.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 1 end)
		pcall(function() pageView.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0) end)
	else
		local state = SavedHubScrollState
		if not state then
			return
		end

		if state.ScrollingEnabled ~= nil then
			pcall(function() pageView.ScrollingEnabled = state.ScrollingEnabled end)
		end
		if state.ScrollBarThickness ~= nil then
			pcall(function() pageView.ScrollBarThickness = state.ScrollBarThickness end)
		end
		if state.ScrollBarImageTransparency ~= nil then
			pcall(function() pageView.ScrollBarImageTransparency = state.ScrollBarImageTransparency end)
		end

		SavedHubScrollState = nil
	end
end

local function ResetHubScrollPosition(this)
	SetHubScrollingDisabled(this, true)

	local hub = this and this.HubRef
	local pageView = hub and hub.PageView
	if pageView and pageView:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
		pcall(function()
			pageView.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
		end)
	end
end

-- ============================================================
-- BUTTON STYLING
-- ============================================================

local function KeepActionButtonHovered(button)
	local function ApplySelectedImage()
		if not button or not button.Parent then
			return
		end

		button.Image = PRIMARY_HOVER_IMAGE
		button.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
		button.SliceCenter = Rect.new(8, 6, 46, 44)
		button.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		button.AutoButtonColor = false
	end

	ApplySelectedImage()

	button:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Image"):Connect(function()
		if button.Parent and button.Image ~= PRIMARY_HOVER_IMAGE then
			ApplySelectedImage()
		end
	end)
end

local function FixButtonLabel(button, label, expectedText)
	if not label then
		label = button:FindFirstChild(button.Name .. "TextLabel", true)
	end

	if not (label and label:IsA("TextLabel")) then
		warn("[ResetCharacter] Missing caption for " .. button.Name)
		return
	end

	label.Text = expectedText
	label.Visible = true
	label.TextTransparency = 0
	label.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	label.ZIndex = button.ZIndex + 1
	-- Deliberately preserve Utility's caption Position and Size.
end

-- ============================================================
-- SELECTED CHOICE
-- ============================================================

local function GetSelectedChoice(this)
	local selectedObject
	local selectedCoreObject

	pcall(function()
		selectedObject = GuiService.SelectedObject
	end)

	pcall(function()
		selectedCoreObject = GuiService.SelectedCoreObject
	end)

	if selectedObject == this.ResetCharacterButton
		or selectedCoreObject == this.ResetCharacterButton
	then
		return this.ResetCharacterButton
	end

	if selectedObject == this.DontResetCharacterButton
		or selectedCoreObject == this.DontResetCharacterButton
	then
		return this.DontResetCharacterButton
	end

	-- Enter still triggers the primary Reset action if selection was cleared.
	return this.ResetCharacterButton
end

-- ============================================================
-- INITIALIZATION
-- ============================================================

local function Initialize()
	local factoryModule =
		SettingsFolder:WaitForChild("SettingsPageFactory", 15)

	assert(factoryModule, "[ResetCharacter] SettingsPageFactory is missing.")

	local factory = require(factoryModule)

	assert(
		type(factory) == "table"
			and type(factory.CreateNewPage) == "function",
		"[ResetCharacter] SettingsPageFactory.CreateNewPage is missing."
	)

	local this = factory:CreateNewPage()

	this.TabHeader = nil
	this.Page.Name = "ResetCharacter"
	this.ShouldShowBottomBar = false
	this.ShouldShowHubBar = false

	local IsMobile =
		utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() or UserInputService.TouchEnabled

	-- Responsive base dimensions. ApplyConfirmationLayout clamps these to
	-- the real viewport after SettingsHub has attached the page.
	local TitleHeight
	local TitleTextSize
	local BaseButtonWidth = 200
	local ButtonHeight = 50
	local HorizontalGap = 20

	if IsTenFootInterface then
		TitleHeight = 100
		TitleTextSize = 48
		BaseButtonWidth = 300
		ButtonHeight = 80
		HorizontalGap = 20
	elseif IsMobile then
		TitleHeight = 100
		TitleTextSize = 24
		BaseButtonWidth = 200
		ButtonHeight = 50
		HorizontalGap = 3

		local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local viewport = camera
			and camera.ViewportSize
			or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
		local availableWidth = math.max(220, viewport.X - 24)
		BaseButtonWidth = math.clamp(
			(availableWidth - 6) / 2,
			110,
			200
		)
	else
		TitleHeight = 130
		TitleTextSize = 36
	end

	-- ========================================================
	-- CONTENT CONTAINER
	-- ========================================================
	local ContentFrame = utility:Create("Frame") {
		Name = "ResetCharacterConfirmationContent",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ClipsDescendants = true,
		LayoutOrder = 1,
		ZIndex = 1,
		Parent = this.Page,
	}

	-- ========================================================
	-- CONFIRMATION TEXT
	-- ========================================================
	local Title = utility:Create("TextLabel") {
		Name = "ResetCharacterText",
		Text = "Are you sure you want to reset your character?",
		Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold,
		TextSize = TitleTextSize,
		TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = IsMobile
			and UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, TitleHeight)
			or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, TitleHeight),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 100),
		TextWrapped = true,
		ZIndex = 5,
		Parent = ContentFrame,
	}

	if IsMobile then
		Title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 100)
	end

	-- ========================================================
	-- RESET CALLBACK SUPPORT
	-- ========================================================
	local function ResetCharacter()
		local player = PlayersService.LocalPlayer
		local character = player and player.Character
		local humanoid = character
			and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

		if humanoid then
			humanoid.Health = 0
		end
	end

	this.ResetBindable = true

	this.ResetCharFunc = function()
		local binding = this.ResetBindable

		if binding == true then
			ResetCharacter()
		elseif typeof(binding) == "Instance"
			and binding:IsA("BindableEvent")
		then
			binding:Fire()
		elseif binding ~= false then
			warn(
				"[ResetCharacter] Reset callback must be a boolean or BindableEvent."
			)
		end

		if this.HubRef then
			this.HubRef:SetVisibility(false, true)
		end
	end

	-- ========================================================
	-- CANCEL / BACK
	-- ========================================================
	this.DontResetCharFunc = function(isUsingGamepad)
		if this.HubRef then
			this.HubRef:PopMenu(isUsingGamepad, true)
		end
	end

	this.DontResetCharFromButton = function(isUsingGamepad)
		this.DontResetCharFunc(isUsingGamepad)
	end

	this.DontResetCharFromHotkey = function(_, state, input)
		if state ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
		end

		local inputType = input and input.UserInputType
		local isUsingGamepad = inputType ~= nil
			and string.find(tostring(inputType), "Gamepad", 1, true) ~= nil

		this.DontResetCharFunc(isUsingGamepad)
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
	end

	-- ========================================================
	-- BUTTONS
	-- ========================================================
	local initialWidth = BaseButtonWidth
	local ButtonSize = UDim2.new(0, initialWidth, 0, ButtonHeight)

	-- TRUE is Utility:MakeStyledButton's selected-by-default argument.
	local ResetButton, ResetLabel = utility:MakeStyledButton(
		"ResetCharacter",
		"Reset",
		ButtonSize,
		this.ResetCharFunc,
		true
	)

	this.ResetCharacterButton = ResetButton

	ResetButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	ResetButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -(initialWidth + HorizontalGap), 0, 300)
	ResetButton.Size = ButtonSize
	ResetButton.NextSelectionRight = nil
	ResetButton.Visible = true
	ResetButton.Active = true
	ResetButton.Selectable = true
	ResetButton.ZIndex = 6
	ResetButton.Parent = ContentFrame

	KeepActionButtonHovered(ResetButton)
	FixButtonLabel(ResetButton, ResetLabel, "Reset")

	local DontResetButton, DontResetLabel = utility:MakeStyledButton(
		"DontResetCharacter",
		"Don't Reset",
		ButtonSize,
		this.DontResetCharFromButton
	)

	this.DontResetCharacterButton = DontResetButton

	DontResetButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	DontResetButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, HorizontalGap, 0, 300)
	DontResetButton.Size = ButtonSize
	DontResetButton.NextSelectionLeft = nil
	DontResetButton.Visible = true
	DontResetButton.Active = true
	DontResetButton.Selectable = true
	DontResetButton.ZIndex = 6
	DontResetButton.Parent = ContentFrame

	FixButtonLabel(
		DontResetButton,
		DontResetLabel,
		"Don't Reset"
	)

	-- The confirmation content follows the viewport, not the old 600px fixed
	-- canvas. Preserve the historical placement on regular screens and compact
	-- only when a short viewport would otherwise clip the controls.
	local function ApplyConfirmationLayout()
		local viewport = GetViewportSize(this)
		ContentFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		ContentFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		ContentFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
		if viewport.Y &lt; 380 then
			local compactTitleY = math.max(12, math.floor(viewport.Y * 0.18))
			local compactButtonY = math.max(150, math.floor(viewport.Y * 0.62))
			Title.Position = UDim2.new(0, IsMobile and 10 or 0, 0, compactTitleY)
			ResetButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -(initialWidth + HorizontalGap), 0, compactButtonY)
			DontResetButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, HorizontalGap, 0, compactButtonY)
		else
			Title.Position = UDim2.new(0, IsMobile and 10 or 0, 0, 100)
			ResetButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -(initialWidth + HorizontalGap), 0, 300)
			DontResetButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, HorizontalGap, 0, 300)
		end
		ResetHubScrollPosition(this)
	end

	this.ApplyConfirmationLayout = ApplyConfirmationLayout

	return this
end

PageInstance = Initialize()

-- ============================================================
-- ENTER ACTIVATION
-- ============================================================

local function TryActivateWithEnter()
	if not IsPageActive(PageInstance) then
		return false
	end

	local focusedTextBox
	pcall(function()
		focusedTextBox = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
	end)

	if focusedTextBox then
		return false
	end

	local now = os.clock()
	if now - LastEnterActivation &lt; ENTER_DEBOUNCE_SECONDS then
		return true
	end

	LastEnterActivation = now
	PageInstance.ActivateSelectedChoice()
	return true
end

local function EnterActionHandler(_, inputState)
	if inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
	end

	if TryActivateWithEnter() then
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
	end

	return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
end

PageInstance.ActivateSelectedChoice = function()
	local selectedButton = GetSelectedChoice(PageInstance)

	if selectedButton == PageInstance.ResetCharacterButton then
		PageInstance.ResetCharFunc()
	elseif selectedButton == PageInstance.DontResetCharacterButton then
		PageInstance.DontResetCharFunc(false)
	end
end

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Return
		or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadEnter
	then
		TryActivateWithEnter()
	end
end)

-- ============================================================
-- DISPLAY / HIDE
-- ============================================================

local function SafeConnect(eventLike, callback)
	local signal
	pcall(function()
		signal = eventLike.Event
	end)

	if signal then
		local ok, connection = pcall(function()
			return signal:Connect(callback)
		end)
		if ok then
			return connection
		end
	end

	return nil
end

SafeConnect(PageInstance.Displayed, function()
	pcall(function()
		GuiService.SelectedObject = PageInstance.ResetCharacterButton
	end)
	pcall(function()
		GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = PageInstance.ResetCharacterButton
	end)

	PageInstance.ApplyConfirmationLayout()
	ResetHubScrollPosition(PageInstance)

	if ViewportSizeConnection then
		ViewportSizeConnection:Disconnect()
		ViewportSizeConnection = nil
	end

	local viewportObject = GetViewportObject(PageInstance)
	if viewportObject then
		ViewportSizeConnection =
			viewportObject:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
				if IsPageActive(PageInstance) then
					PageInstance.ApplyConfirmationLayout()
				end
			end)
	end

	if CameraSizeConnection then
		CameraSizeConnection:Disconnect()
		CameraSizeConnection = nil
	end

	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if camera then
		CameraSizeConnection =
			camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(function()
				if IsPageActive(PageInstance) then
					PageInstance.ApplyConfirmationLayout()
				end
			end)
	end

	task.defer(function()
		task.wait()
		if IsPageActive(PageInstance) then
			PageInstance.ApplyConfirmationLayout()
			ResetHubScrollPosition(PageInstance)
		end
	end)

	ContextActionService:BindActionAtPriority(
		RESET_ENTER_ACTION,
		EnterActionHandler,
		false,
		ENTER_ACTION_PRIORITY,
		Enum.KeyCode.Return,
		Enum.KeyCode.KeypadEnter
	)

	ContextActionService:BindAction(
		RESET_CHARACTER_GAME_ACTION,
		PageInstance.DontResetCharFromHotkey,
		false,
		Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB
	)
end)

SafeConnect(PageInstance.Hidden, function()
	ContextActionService:UnbindAction(RESET_ENTER_ACTION)
	ContextActionService:UnbindAction(RESET_CHARACTER_GAME_ACTION)

	if ViewportSizeConnection then
		ViewportSizeConnection:Disconnect()
		ViewportSizeConnection = nil
	end

	if CameraSizeConnection then
		CameraSizeConnection:Disconnect()
		CameraSizeConnection = nil
	end

	SetHubScrollingDisabled(PageInstance, false)
end)

-- ============================================================
-- PUBLIC RESET CALLBACK API
-- ============================================================

function PageInstance:SetResetCallback(bindableEvent)
	if bindableEvent == false
		and IsPageActive(PageInstance)
		and PageInstance.HubRef
	then
		PageInstance.HubRef:PopMenu(nil, true)
	end

	PageInstance.ResetBindable = bindableEvent
end

return PageInstance

                                        </ContentId>

                                        <string name="ScriptGuid">{882E275A-458B-42A8-8B80-A6AB38748EE3}</string>
                                        <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                        <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: LeaveGame.lua
    Full-source viewport/no-scroll repair for Yeku Legacy CoreGui

    Fixes:
        - Never makes the confirmation page taller than the visible viewport.
        - Reports the visible viewport size to SettingsHub, preventing an
          oversized PageView.CanvasSize and its unwanted scrollbar.
        - Keeps the page and its content container at 100% viewport height.
        - Keeps the confirmation text above the buttons and fits short viewports.
        - Keeps Leave highlighted by default.
        - Preserves Utility-generated buttons and labels.
        - Activates the selected choice with Enter or numpad Enter.
        - Preserves ButtonB cancellation and gamepad selection.
        - Retains the page-factory and SettingsHub contracts.
]]

local LEAVE_GAME_ACTION = "LeaveGameCancelAction"
local LEAVE_ENTER_ACTION = "LeaveGameConfirmEnterAction"

local PRIMARY_HOVER_IMAGE =
	"rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarAssets/MenuButtonSelected.png"

local ENTER_ACTION_PRIORITY = 10000
local ENTER_DEBOUNCE_SECONDS = 0.25
local TEXT_BUTTON_GAP = 55
local BUTTON_CENTER_OFFSET = 25

local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local ContextActionService = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local PlayersService = game:GetService("Players")
local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")

local RobloxGui = script and script:FindFirstAncestor("NewRobloxGui")

if not (RobloxGui and RobloxGui:IsA("ScreenGui")) then
	RobloxGui = CoreGui:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 15)
end

assert(
	RobloxGui and RobloxGui:IsA("ScreenGui"),
	"[LeaveGame] CoreGui.NewRobloxGui is missing."
)

local Modules = RobloxGui:WaitForChild("Modules", 15)
assert(Modules, "[LeaveGame] NewRobloxGui.Modules is missing.")

local SettingsFolder = Modules:WaitForChild("Settings", 15)
assert(SettingsFolder, "[LeaveGame] NewRobloxGui.Modules.Settings is missing.")

local utility = require(SettingsFolder:WaitForChild("Utility", 15))

local PageInstance
local LastEnterActivation = -math.huge
local ViewportSizeConnection
local CameraSizeConnection
local SavedHubScrollState = nil

local TenFootValue = Lighting:FindFirstChild("TenFootInterface")
local IsTenFootInterface =
	TenFootValue ~= nil and TenFootValue.Value == true

-- ============================================================
-- VIEWPORT HELPERS
-- ============================================================

local function GetViewportObject(this)
	local hub = this and this.HubRef

	if not hub then
		return nil
	end

	-- Measure PageView first because SettingsHub compares CanvasSize against
	-- PageView.AbsoluteSize. Matching this exact size prevents even a tiny
	-- fractional overflow from enabling the vertical scrollbar.
	for _, name in ipairs({
		"PageView",
		"PageViewClipper",
		"PageClipper",
		}) do
		local ok, object = pcall(function()
			return hub[name]
		end)

		if ok
			and typeof(object) == "Instance"
			and object:IsA("GuiObject")
			and object.AbsoluteSize.X &gt; 0
			and object.AbsoluteSize.Y &gt; 0
		then
			return object
		end
	end

	return nil
end

local function GetViewportSize(this)
	local viewportObject = GetViewportObject(this)

	if viewportObject then
		local size = viewportObject.AbsoluteSize
		if size.X &gt; 0 and size.Y &gt; 0 then
			return size
		end
	end

	local page = this and this.Page
	if page then
		local size = page.AbsoluteSize
		if size.X &gt; 0 and size.Y &gt; 0 then
			return size
		end
	end

	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if camera then
		local size = camera.ViewportSize
		if size.X &gt; 0 and size.Y &gt; 0 then
			return Vector2.new(size.X, math.max(1, size.Y - 100))
		end
	end

	return Vector2.new(800, 500)
end

local function IsPageActive(this)
	if not this or not this.Page then
		return false
	end

	local page = this.Page

	if not page.Visible or not page.Parent then
		return false
	end

	if not page:IsDescendantOf(game) then
		return false
	end

	local hub = this.HubRef

	if hub then
		if hub.Visible == false then
			return false
		end

		local currentPage = hub.Pages and hub.Pages.CurrentPage
		if currentPage and currentPage ~= this then
			return false
		end
	end

	return true
end

-- SettingsHub owns one shared ScrollingFrame for every settings page.
-- Its CanvasSize is recalculated after Page:Display(), so changing GetSize()
-- alone cannot reliably remove the bar. Disable scrolling on that shared
-- viewport while this fixed-layout confirmation page is active, and restore
-- its original settings when the page hides.
local function SetHubScrollingDisabled(this, disabled)
	local hub = this and this.HubRef
	local pageView = hub and hub.PageView

	if not (pageView and pageView:IsA("ScrollingFrame")) then
		return
	end

	if disabled then
		if not SavedHubScrollState then
			local state = {}

			pcall(function()
				state.ScrollingEnabled = pageView.ScrollingEnabled
			end)
			pcall(function()
				state.ScrollBarThickness = pageView.ScrollBarThickness
			end)
			pcall(function()
				state.ScrollBarImageTransparency = pageView.ScrollBarImageTransparency
			end)

			SavedHubScrollState = state
		end

		pcall(function()
			pageView.ScrollingEnabled = false
		end)
		pcall(function()
			pageView.ScrollBarThickness = 0
		end)
		pcall(function()
			pageView.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 1
		end)
		pcall(function()
			pageView.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
		end)
	else
		local state = SavedHubScrollState
		if not state then
			return
		end

		if state.ScrollingEnabled ~= nil then
			pcall(function()
				pageView.ScrollingEnabled = state.ScrollingEnabled
			end)
		end
		if state.ScrollBarThickness ~= nil then
			pcall(function()
				pageView.ScrollBarThickness = state.ScrollBarThickness
			end)
		end
		if state.ScrollBarImageTransparency ~= nil then
			pcall(function()
				pageView.ScrollBarImageTransparency = state.ScrollBarImageTransparency
			end)
		end

		SavedHubScrollState = nil
	end
end

local function ResetHubScrollPosition(this)
	SetHubScrollingDisabled(this, true)

	local hub = this and this.HubRef
	local pageView = hub and hub.PageView
	if pageView and pageView:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
		pcall(function()
			pageView.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
		end)
	end
end

-- ============================================================
-- BUTTON STYLING
-- ============================================================

local function KeepActionButtonHovered(button)
	local function ApplySelectedImage()
		if not button or not button.Parent then
			return
		end

		button.Image = PRIMARY_HOVER_IMAGE
		button.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
		button.SliceCenter = Rect.new(8, 6, 46, 44)
		button.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		button.AutoButtonColor = false
	end

	ApplySelectedImage()

	button:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Image"):Connect(function()
		if button.Parent and button.Image ~= PRIMARY_HOVER_IMAGE then
			ApplySelectedImage()
		end
	end)
end

local function FixButtonLabel(button, label, expectedText)
	if not label then
		label = button:FindFirstChild(button.Name .. "TextLabel", true)
	end

	if not (label and label:IsA("TextLabel")) then
		warn("[LeaveGame] Missing caption for " .. button.Name)
		return
	end

	label.Text = expectedText
	label.Visible = true
	label.TextTransparency = 0
	label.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	label.ZIndex = button.ZIndex + 1
	-- Deliberately preserve Utility's caption Position and Size.
end

-- ============================================================
-- SELECTED CHOICE
-- ============================================================

local function GetSelectedChoice(this)
	local selectedObject
	local selectedCoreObject

	pcall(function()
		selectedObject = GuiService.SelectedObject
	end)

	pcall(function()
		selectedCoreObject = GuiService.SelectedCoreObject
	end)

	if selectedObject == this.LeaveGameButton
		or selectedCoreObject == this.LeaveGameButton
	then
		return this.LeaveGameButton
	end

	if selectedObject == this.DontLeaveGameButton
		or selectedCoreObject == this.DontLeaveGameButton
	then
		return this.DontLeaveGameButton
	end

	-- Default Enter behavior remains the primary Leave action.
	return this.LeaveGameButton
end

-- ============================================================
-- INITIALIZATION
-- ============================================================

local function Initialize()
	local factoryModule =
		SettingsFolder:WaitForChild("SettingsPageFactory", 15)

	assert(factoryModule, "[LeaveGame] SettingsPageFactory is missing.")

	local factory = require(factoryModule)

	assert(
		type(factory) == "table"
			and type(factory.CreateNewPage) == "function",
		"[LeaveGame] SettingsPageFactory.CreateNewPage is missing."
	)

	local this = factory:CreateNewPage()

	this.TabHeader = nil
	this.Page.Name = "LeaveGamePage"

	local IsMobile =
		utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() or UserInputService.TouchEnabled

	-- Responsive base sizes. Actual horizontal sizes are clamped to the
	-- live viewport inside ApplyConfirmationLayout().
	local TitleHeight
	local TitleTextSize
	local BaseButtonWidth = 200
	local ButtonHeight = 50
	local HorizontalGap = 20

	if IsTenFootInterface then
		TitleHeight = 100
		TitleTextSize = 48
		BaseButtonWidth = 300
		ButtonHeight = 80
		HorizontalGap = 20
	elseif IsMobile then
		TitleHeight = 100
		TitleTextSize = 24
		BaseButtonWidth = 200
		ButtonHeight = 50
		HorizontalGap = 3

		local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local viewport = camera
			and camera.ViewportSize
			or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
		local availableWidth = math.max(220, viewport.X - 24)
		BaseButtonWidth = math.clamp(
			(availableWidth - 6) / 2,
			110,
			200
		)
	else
		TitleHeight = 130
		TitleTextSize = 36
	end

	-- ========================================================
	-- CONTENT CONTAINER
	-- ========================================================
	local ContentFrame = utility:Create("Frame") {
		Name = "LeaveGameConfirmationContent",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ClipsDescendants = true,
		LayoutOrder = 1,
		ZIndex = 1,
		Parent = this.Page,
	}

	-- ========================================================
	-- CONFIRMATION TEXT
	-- ========================================================
	local Title = utility:Create("TextLabel") {
		Name = "LeaveGameText",
		Text = "Are you sure you want to leave the game?",
		Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold,
		TextSize = TitleTextSize,
		TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		TextWrapped = true,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = IsMobile
			and UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, TitleHeight)
			or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, TitleHeight),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 100),
		ZIndex = 5,
		Parent = ContentFrame,
	}

	if IsMobile then
		Title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 100)
	end

	-- ========================================================
	-- ACTIONS
	-- ========================================================
	this.LeaveFunc = function()
		pcall(function()
			GuiService.SelectedObject = nil
		end)

		pcall(function()
			GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = nil
		end)

		local success = pcall(function()
			game:Shutdown()
		end)

		if not success then
			local player = PlayersService.LocalPlayer
			if player then
				pcall(function()
					player:Kick()
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	this.DontLeaveFunc = function(isUsingGamepad)
		if this.HubRef then
			this.HubRef:PopMenu(isUsingGamepad, true)
		end
	end

	this.DontLeaveFromButton = function(isUsingGamepad)
		this.DontLeaveFunc(isUsingGamepad)
	end

	this.DontLeaveFromHotkey = function(_, state, input)
		if state ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
		end

		local inputType = input and input.UserInputType
		local isUsingGamepad = inputType ~= nil
			and string.find(tostring(inputType), "Gamepad", 1, true) ~= nil

		this.DontLeaveFunc(isUsingGamepad)
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
	end

	-- ========================================================
	-- BUTTONS
	-- ========================================================
	local initialWidth = BaseButtonWidth
	local ButtonSize = UDim2.new(0, initialWidth, 0, ButtonHeight)

	-- TRUE is Utility:MakeStyledButton's selected-by-default argument.
	local LeaveButton, LeaveLabel = utility:MakeStyledButton(
		"LeaveGame",
		"Leave",
		ButtonSize,
		this.LeaveFunc,
		true
	)

	this.LeaveGameButton = LeaveButton

	LeaveButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	LeaveButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -(initialWidth + HorizontalGap), 0, 300)
	LeaveButton.Size = ButtonSize
	LeaveButton.Visible = true
	LeaveButton.Active = true
	LeaveButton.Selectable = true
	LeaveButton.ZIndex = 6
	LeaveButton.Parent = ContentFrame

	KeepActionButtonHovered(LeaveButton)
	FixButtonLabel(LeaveButton, LeaveLabel, "Leave")

	local DontLeaveButton, DontLeaveLabel = utility:MakeStyledButton(
		"DontLeaveGame",
		"Don't Leave",
		ButtonSize,
		this.DontLeaveFromButton
	)

	this.DontLeaveGameButton = DontLeaveButton

	DontLeaveButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	DontLeaveButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, HorizontalGap, 0, 300)
	DontLeaveButton.Size = ButtonSize
	DontLeaveButton.Visible = true
	DontLeaveButton.Active = true
	DontLeaveButton.Selectable = true
	DontLeaveButton.ZIndex = 6
	DontLeaveButton.Parent = ContentFrame

	FixButtonLabel(DontLeaveButton, DontLeaveLabel, "Don't Leave")

	-- Match the content root to the visible page instead of retaining the old
	-- fixed 600px content height. That prevents both clipping and a fake canvas
	-- overflow on compact screens; the standard title/button positions remain.
	local function ApplyConfirmationLayout()
		local viewport = GetViewportSize(this)
		ContentFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		ContentFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		ContentFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
		if viewport.Y &lt; 380 then
			local compactTitleY = math.max(12, math.floor(viewport.Y * 0.18))
			local compactButtonY = math.max(150, math.floor(viewport.Y * 0.62))
			Title.Position = UDim2.new(0, IsMobile and 10 or 0, 0, compactTitleY)
			LeaveButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -(initialWidth + HorizontalGap), 0, compactButtonY)
			DontLeaveButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, HorizontalGap, 0, compactButtonY)
		else
			Title.Position = UDim2.new(0, IsMobile and 10 or 0, 0, 100)
			LeaveButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -(initialWidth + HorizontalGap), 0, 300)
			DontLeaveButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, HorizontalGap, 0, 300)
		end
		ResetHubScrollPosition(this)
	end

	this.ApplyConfirmationLayout = ApplyConfirmationLayout

	-- Recompute CanvasSize via GetSize(); it equals the visible viewport.
	return this
end

PageInstance = Initialize()

-- ============================================================
-- ENTER ACTIVATION
-- ============================================================

local function TryActivateWithEnter()
	if not IsPageActive(PageInstance) then
		return false
	end

	local focusedTextBox
	pcall(function()
		focusedTextBox = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
	end)

	if focusedTextBox then
		return false
	end

	local now = os.clock()
	if now - LastEnterActivation &lt; ENTER_DEBOUNCE_SECONDS then
		return true
	end

	LastEnterActivation = now
	PageInstance.ActivateSelectedChoice()
	return true
end

local function EnterActionHandler(_, inputState)
	if inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
	end

	if TryActivateWithEnter() then
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
	end

	return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
end

PageInstance.ActivateSelectedChoice = function()
	local selectedButton = GetSelectedChoice(PageInstance)

	if selectedButton == PageInstance.LeaveGameButton then
		PageInstance.LeaveFunc()
	elseif selectedButton == PageInstance.DontLeaveGameButton then
		PageInstance.DontLeaveFunc(false)
	end
end

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Return
		or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadEnter
	then
		TryActivateWithEnter()
	end
end)

-- ============================================================
-- DISPLAY / HIDE
-- ============================================================

local function SafeConnect(eventLike, callback)
	local signal
	pcall(function()
		signal = eventLike.Event
	end)
	if signal then
		local ok, connection = pcall(function()
			return signal:Connect(callback)
		end)
		if ok then
			return connection
		end
	end
	return nil
end

SafeConnect(PageInstance.Displayed, function()
	pcall(function()
		GuiService.SelectedObject = PageInstance.LeaveGameButton
	end)
	pcall(function()
		GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = PageInstance.LeaveGameButton
	end)

	PageInstance.ApplyConfirmationLayout()
	ResetHubScrollPosition(PageInstance)

	if ViewportSizeConnection then
		ViewportSizeConnection:Disconnect()
		ViewportSizeConnection = nil
	end

	local viewportObject = GetViewportObject(PageInstance)
	if viewportObject then
		ViewportSizeConnection =
			viewportObject:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
				if IsPageActive(PageInstance) then
					PageInstance.ApplyConfirmationLayout()
				end
			end)
	end

	if CameraSizeConnection then
		CameraSizeConnection:Disconnect()
		CameraSizeConnection = nil
	end

	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if camera then
		CameraSizeConnection =
			camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(function()
				if IsPageActive(PageInstance) then
					PageInstance.ApplyConfirmationLayout()
				end
			end)
	end

	task.defer(function()
		task.wait()
		if IsPageActive(PageInstance) then
			PageInstance.ApplyConfirmationLayout()
			ResetHubScrollPosition(PageInstance)
		end
	end)

	ContextActionService:BindActionAtPriority(
		LEAVE_ENTER_ACTION,
		EnterActionHandler,
		false,
		ENTER_ACTION_PRIORITY,
		Enum.KeyCode.Return,
		Enum.KeyCode.KeypadEnter
	)

	ContextActionService:BindAction(
		LEAVE_GAME_ACTION,
		PageInstance.DontLeaveFromHotkey,
		false,
		Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB
	)
end)

SafeConnect(PageInstance.Hidden, function()
	ContextActionService:UnbindAction(LEAVE_ENTER_ACTION)
	ContextActionService:UnbindAction(LEAVE_GAME_ACTION)

	if ViewportSizeConnection then
		ViewportSizeConnection:Disconnect()
		ViewportSizeConnection = nil
	end

	if CameraSizeConnection then
		CameraSizeConnection:Disconnect()
		CameraSizeConnection = nil
	end

	SetHubScrollingDisabled(PageInstance, false)
end)

return PageInstance
</ProtectedString>
                                        <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-419</int64>
                                        <BinaryString name="Tags" />
                                    </Properties>
                                </Item>
                            </Item>
                            <Item class="ModuleScript" referent="RBX515">
                                <Properties>
                                    <BinaryString name="AttributesSerialize" />
                                    <SecurityCapabilities name="Capabilities">0</SecurityCapabilities>
                                    <bool name="DefinesCapabilities">false</bool>
                                    <ContentId name="LinkedSource">
                                        <null />
                                    </ContentId>
                                    <string name="Name">SettingsPageFactory</string>
                                    <string name="ScriptGuid">{EE205B7C-37BE-41B1-ABE8-231DFBA005B1}</string>
                                    <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                    <ProtectedString name="Source">
--[[
    Filename: SettingsPageFactory.lua
    Written by: jeditkacheff
    Version: 1.0 - Yeku Legacy CoreGui compatibility patch

    Description:
        Base page functionality for historical settings pages.

    Runtime:
        CoreGui.NewRobloxGui

    Fixes:
        - Uses NewRobloxGui rather than native RobloxGui.
        - Resolves dependencies from the copied legacy module tree.
        - Allows missing TenFootInterface on desktop.
        - Validates required dependency APIs.
        - Preserves the CreateNewPage() public API.
]]

--==============================================================
-- SERVICES
--==============================================================

local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

--==============================================================
-- LEGACY GUI ROOT
--==============================================================

local RobloxGui

-- Prefer the root containing this copied ModuleScript.
-- This avoids accidentally using the modern native RobloxGui.

local RootSuccess, RootResult = pcall(function()
	if script and script:IsDescendantOf(CoreGui) then
		local Ancestor = script:FindFirstAncestor("NewRobloxGui")

		if Ancestor and Ancestor:IsA("ScreenGui") then
			return Ancestor
		end
	end

	return CoreGui:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 15)
end)

assert(
	RootSuccess and RootResult,
	"[SettingsPageFactory] CoreGui.NewRobloxGui was not found."
)

RobloxGui = RootResult

assert(
	RobloxGui:IsA("ScreenGui"),
	"[SettingsPageFactory] NewRobloxGui must be a ScreenGui."
)

assert(
	script:IsDescendantOf(RobloxGui),
	"[SettingsPageFactory] This module is not inside NewRobloxGui."
)

--==============================================================
-- MODULE RESOLUTION
--==============================================================

local Modules = RobloxGui:WaitForChild("Modules", 15)

assert(
	Modules and Modules:IsA("Folder"),
	"[SettingsPageFactory] NewRobloxGui.Modules is missing."
)

local SettingsFolder = Modules:WaitForChild("Settings", 15)

assert(
	SettingsFolder and SettingsFolder:IsA("Folder"),
	"[SettingsPageFactory] NewRobloxGui.Modules.Settings is missing."
)

local function ResolveModule(Parent, Name, Required)
	local Module = Parent:FindFirstChild(Name)
		or Parent:FindFirstChild(Name .. ".lua")

	if not Module then
		local BaseName = Name:gsub("%.lua$", "")

		for _, Child in ipairs(Parent:GetChildren()) do
			if Child.Name:gsub("%.lua$", "") == BaseName then
				Module = Child
				break
			end
		end
	end

	if not Module then
		if Required then
			error(
				"[SettingsPageFactory] Required module "
					.. Name
					.. " is missing under "
					.. Parent:GetFullName()
			)
		end

		return nil
	end

	if not Module:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		error(
			"[SettingsPageFactory] Expected ModuleScript for "
				.. Name
				.. ", got "
				.. Module.ClassName
				.. " at "
				.. Module:GetFullName()
		)
	end

	return Module
end

local function RequireChecked(Module, Label)
	local Success, Result = pcall(require, Module)

	if not Success then
		error(
			"[SettingsPageFactory] Failed to require "
				.. Label
				.. " at "
				.. Module:GetFullName()
				.. ":\n"
				.. tostring(Result)
		)
	end

	return Result
end

--==============================================================
-- REQUIRED UTILITY MODULE
--==============================================================

local UtilityModule = ResolveModule(
	SettingsFolder,
	"Utility",
	true
)

local utility = RequireChecked(
	UtilityModule,
	"Utility"
)

assert(
	type(utility) == "table",
	"[SettingsPageFactory] Utility must return a table."
)

assert(
	type(utility.Create) == "function",
	"[SettingsPageFactory] Utility.Create is missing."
)

assert(
	type(utility.IsSmallTouchScreen) == "function",
	"[SettingsPageFactory] Utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() is missing."
)

assert(
	type(utility.IsPortrait) == "function",
	"[SettingsPageFactory] Utility:IsPortrait() is missing."
)

assert(
	type(utility.OnResized) == "function",
	"[SettingsPageFactory] Utility:OnResized() is missing."
)

--==============================================================
-- REQUIRED STYLE WIDGETS MODULE
--==============================================================

local StyleWidgetsModule = ResolveModule(
	Modules,
	"StyleWidgets",
	true
)

local StyleWidgets = RequireChecked(
	StyleWidgetsModule,
	"StyleWidgets"
)

assert(
	type(StyleWidgets) == "table",
	"[SettingsPageFactory] StyleWidgets must return a table."
)

assert(
	type(StyleWidgets.MakeTabSelectionWidget) == "function",
	"[SettingsPageFactory] StyleWidgets.MakeTabSelectionWidget is missing."
)

--==============================================================
-- OPTIONAL TEN-FOOT INTERFACE
--==============================================================

local TenFootModule = ResolveModule(
	Modules,
	"TenFootInterface",
	false
)

local isTenFootInterface = false

if TenFootModule then
	local TenFootSuccess, TenFootResult = pcall(
		RequireChecked,
		TenFootModule,
		"TenFootInterface"
	)

	if TenFootSuccess
		and type(TenFootResult) == "table"
		and type(TenFootResult.IsEnabled) == "function" then

		local EnabledSuccess, EnabledResult = pcall(function()
			return TenFootResult:IsEnabled()
		end)

		if EnabledSuccess then
			isTenFootInterface = EnabledResult == true
		end
	else
		warn(
			"[SettingsPageFactory] TenFootInterface could not initialize; "
				.. "using desktop layout."
		)
	end
else
	warn(
		"[SettingsPageFactory] Modules.TenFootInterface is missing; "
			.. "using desktop layout."
	)
end

--==============================================================
-- FEATURE FLAG
--==============================================================

local success, result = pcall(function()
	return settings():GetFFlag("UseNotificationsLocalization")
end)

local FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization =
	success and result == true

--==============================================================
-- CONSTANTS
--==============================================================

local HEADER_SPACING = 5

if utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() then
	HEADER_SPACING = 0
end

--==============================================================
-- PAGE INITIALIZATION
--==============================================================

local function Initialize()
	local this = {}

	this.HubRef = nil
	this.LastSelectedObject = nil
	this.TabPosition = 0
	this.Active = false
	this.OpenStateChangedCount = 0
	this.ShouldShowBottomBar = true
	this.ShouldShowHubBar = true
	this.IsPageClipped = true

	local rows = {}
	local displayed = false

	--==========================================================
	-- TAB HEADER
	--==========================================================

	this.TabHeader = utility:Create "TextButton" {
		Name = "Header",
		Text = "",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1 / 5, 0, 1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
		Active = true,
		Selectable = true,
	}

	if utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() then
		this.TabHeader.Size = UDim2.new(0, 84, 1, 0)
	elseif isTenFootInterface then
		this.TabHeader.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 1, 0)
	end

	this.TabHeader.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if this.HubRef then
			this.HubRef:SwitchToPage(this, true)
		end
	end)

	local icon = utility:Create "ImageLabel" {
		Name = "Icon",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(0.75, 0, 0.75, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -18),
		Image = "",
		ImageTransparency = 0.5,
		Parent = this.TabHeader,
	}

	utility:Create "UIAspectRatioConstraint" {
		Name = "AspectRatioConstraint",
		AspectRatio = 1,
		Parent = icon,
	}

	local title = utility:Create "TextLabel" {
		Name = "Title",
		Text = "",
		Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold,
		FontSize = Enum.FontSize.Size24,
		TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1.05, 0, 1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(1.2, 0, 0, 0),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextTransparency = 0.5,
	}

	local titleTextSizeConstraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
	titleTextSizeConstraint.MaxTextSize = 24

	if FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
		title.Parent = this.TabHeader
		title.TextScaled = true
		title.TextWrapped = true
		titleTextSizeConstraint.Parent = title
	else
		title.Parent = icon
	end

	if utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() then
		title.FontSize = Enum.FontSize.Size18
		titleTextSizeConstraint.MaxTextSize = 18
	elseif isTenFootInterface then
		title.FontSize = Enum.FontSize.Size48
		titleTextSizeConstraint.MaxTextSize = 48
	end

	StyleWidgets.MakeTabSelectionWidget(this.TabHeader)

	assert(
		this.TabHeader:FindFirstChild("TabSelection"),
		"[SettingsPageFactory] MakeTabSelectionWidget did not create "
			.. "a TabSelection child."
	)

	local titleScaleInitial = Vector2.new(
		title.Size.X.Scale,
		title.Size.Y.Scale
	)

	local function onResized()
		if not this.TabHeader or not this.TabHeader.Parent then
			return
		end

		local currentIcon = this.TabHeader:FindFirstChild("Icon")
		local currentTitle = this.TabHeader:FindFirstChild("Title")

		if not currentIcon then
			return
		end

		if utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() then
			currentIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 28)
			currentIcon.Position = UDim2.new(
				currentIcon.Position.X.Scale,
				currentIcon.Position.X.Offset,
				0.5,
				-14
			)
			currentIcon.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)

		elseif isTenFootInterface then
			currentIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 88, 0, 74)
			currentIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
			currentIcon.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)

		else
			currentIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 37)
			currentIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0.5, -18)
			currentIcon.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
		end

		local isPortrait = utility:IsPortrait()

		if isPortrait then
			currentIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
			currentIcon.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			currentIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)

			if FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
				if currentTitle then
					currentTitle.Visible = false
				end
			else
				local iconTitle = currentIcon:FindFirstChild("Title")

				if iconTitle then
					iconTitle.Visible = false
				end
			end
		else
			if FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
				if currentTitle then
					currentTitle.Visible = true
				end
			else
				local iconTitle = currentIcon:FindFirstChild("Title")

				if iconTitle then
					iconTitle.Visible = true
				end
			end
		end

		if FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization and currentTitle then
			local iconSize = currentIcon.AbsoluteSize
			local paddingLeft = 0.125
			local paddingRight = 0.025

			currentTitle.Position = UDim2.new(
				paddingLeft,
				iconSize.X,
				0.225,
				0
			)

			currentTitle.Size = UDim2.new(
				titleScaleInitial.X - paddingLeft - paddingRight,
				-iconSize.X,
				0.5,
				0
			)
		end
	end

	utility:OnResized(this.TabHeader, onResized)

	--==========================================================
	-- PAGE CONTAINER
	--==========================================================

	this.Page = utility:Create "Frame" {
		Name = "Page",
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
	}

	this.PageListLayout = utility:Create "UIListLayout" {
		Name = "RowListLayout",
		FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
		HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center,
		VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top,
		Padding = UDim.new(0, 3),
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		Parent = this.Page,
	}

	-- Unique gamepad selection group.

	GuiService:AddSelectionParent(
		HttpService:GenerateGUID(false),
		this.Page
	)

	--==========================================================
	-- EVENTS
	--==========================================================

	this.Displayed = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	this.Displayed.Name = "Displayed"

	this.Displayed.Event:Connect(function()
		if not this.HubRef or not this.HubRef.Shield then
			return
		end

		if not this.HubRef.Shield.Visible then
			return
		end

		this:SelectARow()
	end)

	this.Hidden = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	this.Hidden.Name = "Hidden"

	this.Hidden.Event:Connect(function()
		local SelectedObject = GuiService.SelectedCoreObject

		if SelectedObject
			and SelectedObject:IsDescendantOf(this.Page) then

			GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = nil
		end
	end)

	--==========================================================
	-- SELECTION
	--==========================================================

	function this:SelectARow(forced)
		local SelectedObject = GuiService.SelectedCoreObject

		if forced
			or not SelectedObject
			or not SelectedObject:IsDescendantOf(this.Page) then

			if this.LastSelectedObject then
				GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = this.LastSelectedObject
			elseif #rows &gt; 0 then
				local ValueChanger = rows[1].ValueChanger
				local ValueChangerFrame

				if type(ValueChanger) == "table" then
					ValueChangerFrame =
						ValueChanger.SliderFrame
						or ValueChanger.SelectorFrame
				else
					ValueChangerFrame = ValueChanger
				end

				if ValueChangerFrame then
					GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = ValueChangerFrame
				end
			end
		end
	end

	--==========================================================
	-- DISPLAY / HIDE
	--==========================================================

	function this:Display(pageParent, skipAnimation)
		assert(
			pageParent,
			"[SettingsPageFactory] Display requires a page parent."
		)

		this.OpenStateChangedCount = this.OpenStateChangedCount + 1

		local tabSelection = this.TabHeader:FindFirstChild("TabSelection")
		local currentIcon = this.TabHeader:FindFirstChild("Icon")
		local currentTitle = this.TabHeader:FindFirstChild("Title")

		if tabSelection then
			tabSelection.Visible = true
		end

		if currentIcon then
			currentIcon.ImageTransparency = 0
		end

		if FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
			if currentTitle then
				currentTitle.TextTransparency = 0
			end
		elseif currentIcon then
			local iconTitle = currentIcon:FindFirstChild("Title")

			if iconTitle then
				iconTitle.TextTransparency = 0
			end
		end

		this.Page.Parent = pageParent
		this.Page.Visible = true

		local endPos = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)

		local function animationComplete()
			this.Page.Visible = true
			displayed = true
			this.Displayed:Fire()
		end

		if skipAnimation then
			this.Page.Position = endPos
			animationComplete()
		else
			this.Page:TweenPosition(
				endPos,
				Enum.EasingDirection.In,
				Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
				0.1,
				true,
				animationComplete
			)
		end
	end

	function this:Hide(direction, newPagePos, skipAnimation, delayBeforeHiding)
		this.OpenStateChangedCount = this.OpenStateChangedCount + 1

		local tabSelection = this.TabHeader:FindFirstChild("TabSelection")
		local currentIcon = this.TabHeader:FindFirstChild("Icon")
		local currentTitle = this.TabHeader:FindFirstChild("Title")

		if tabSelection then
			tabSelection.Visible = false
		end

		if currentIcon then
			currentIcon.ImageTransparency = 0.5
		end

		if FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
			if currentTitle then
				currentTitle.TextTransparency = 0.5
			end
		elseif currentIcon then
			local iconTitle = currentIcon:FindFirstChild("Title")

			if iconTitle then
				iconTitle.TextTransparency = 0.5
			end
		end

		if not this.Page.Parent then
			return
		end

		local endPos = UDim2.new(1 * direction, 0, 0, 0)

		local function animationComplete()
			this.Page.Visible = false
			this.Page.Position = UDim2.new(
				this.TabPosition - newPagePos,
				0,
				0,
				0
			)

			displayed = false
			this.Hidden:Fire()
		end

		local function remove()
			if skipAnimation then
				this.Page.Position = endPos
				animationComplete()
			else
				this.Page:TweenPosition(
					endPos,
					Enum.EasingDirection.Out,
					Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
					0.1,
					true,
					animationComplete
				)
			end
		end

		if delayBeforeHiding then
			local openStateCount = this.OpenStateChangedCount

			task.delay(delayBeforeHiding, function()
				if openStateCount == this.OpenStateChangedCount then
					remove()
				end
			end)
		else
			remove()
		end
	end

	--==========================================================
	-- PUBLIC INSTANCE METHODS
	--==========================================================

	function this:GetDisplayed()
		return displayed
	end

	function this:GetVisibility()
		return this.Page.Parent
	end

	function this:GetTabHeader()
		return this.TabHeader
	end

	function this:SetHub(hubRef)
		this.HubRef = hubRef

		for _, row in ipairs(rows) do
			if type(row.ValueChanger) == "table" then
				row.ValueChanger.HubRef = hubRef
			end
		end
	end

	function this:GetSize()
		return this.Page.AbsoluteSize
	end

	function this:AddRow(
		RowFrame,
		RowLabel,
		ValueChangerInstance,
		ExtraRowSpacing
	)
		rows[#rows + 1] = {
			SelectionFrame = RowFrame,
			Label = RowLabel,
			ValueChanger = ValueChangerInstance,
		}

		local rowFrameYSize = 0

		if RowFrame then
			rowFrameYSize = RowFrame.Size.Y.Offset
		end

		local ExtraSpacing = ExtraRowSpacing or 0

		this.Page.Size = UDim2.new(
			1,
			0,
			0,
			this.Page.Size.Y.Offset + rowFrameYSize + ExtraSpacing
		)

		if this.HubRef and type(ValueChangerInstance) == "table" then
			ValueChangerInstance.HubRef = this.HubRef
		end
	end

	return this
end

--==============================================================
-- PUBLIC MODULE API
--==============================================================

local moduleApiTable = {}

function moduleApiTable:CreateNewPage()
	return Initialize()
end

return moduleApiTable

                                    <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: StatsAggregatorManager.lua
    Yeku compatibility patch.
    Manages one StatsAggregator per stat type.
]]

local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

local function findCoreRoot()
	return CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
		or CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
end

local function requireStatsModule(name)
	local root = findCoreRoot()
	assert(root, "[StatsAggregatorManager] CoreGui root is missing")

	local modules = root:FindFirstChild("Modules")
	local statsFolder = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("Stats")
	local moduleScript = statsFolder and statsFolder:FindFirstChild(name)

	if not moduleScript then
		-- Allow dependencies beside this ModuleScript when the package
		-- uses sibling modules rather than a Stats subfolder.
		moduleScript = script.Parent:FindFirstChild(name)
	end

	assert(
		moduleScript and moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript"),
		"[StatsAggregatorManager] Missing ModuleScript: " .. name
	)

	return require(moduleScript)
end

local StatsUtils = requireStatsModule("StatsUtils")
local StatsAggregatorClass = requireStatsModule("StatsAggregator")

local Manager = {}
Manager.__index = Manager

Manager.SecondsBetweenUpdate = 1
Manager.NumSamplesToKeep = 20

local singleton

function Manager.__new()
	local self = setmetatable({}, Manager)
	self._statsAggregators = {}

	for _, statType in ipairs(StatsUtils.AllStatTypes) do
		self._statsAggregators[statType] = StatsAggregatorClass.new(
			statType,
			Manager.NumSamplesToKeep,
			Manager.SecondsBetweenUpdate
		)
	end

	return self
end

function Manager.getSingleton()
	if not singleton then
		singleton = Manager.__new()
		singleton:StartListening()
	end

	return singleton
end

-- Modern capitalization alias.
Manager.GetSingleton = Manager.getSingleton

function Manager:StartListening()
	for _, aggregator in pairs(self._statsAggregators) do
		aggregator:StartListening()
	end
end

function Manager:StopListening()
	for _, aggregator in pairs(self._statsAggregators) do
		aggregator:StopListening()
	end
end

function Manager:GetAggregator(statType)
	return self._statsAggregators[statType]
end

return Manager</ProtectedString>

                                    <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                    <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: StatsButton.lua
    Yeku compatibility patch.

local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

local function findCoreRoot()
	return CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
		or CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
end

local root = findCoreRoot()
local modules = root and root:FindFirstChild("Modules")
local statsFolder = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("Stats")

local function findModule(name, parent)
	local instance = parent and parent:FindFirstChild(name)
	if not instance or not instance:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		error("[StatsButton] Missing ModuleScript: " .. name, 2)
	end
	return require(instance)
end

assert(modules and statsFolder, "[StatsButton] Missing Modules.Stats")

local TextPanelXFraction = 0.5
local GraphXFraction = 1 - TextPanelXFraction

local TextPanelPosition = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
local TextPanelSize = UDim2.new(
	TextPanelXFraction, 0, 1,
	-((tonumber(StyleWidgets.TabSelectionHeight)) or 0)
)
local GraphPosition = UDim2.new(TextPanelXFraction, 0, 0, 0)
local GraphSize = UDim2.new(
	GraphXFraction, 0, 1,
	-((tonumber(StyleWidgets.TabSelectionHeight)) or 0)
)

local StatsButtonClass = {}
StatsButtonClass.__index = StatsButtonClass

function StatsButtonClass.new(statType)
	local self = setmetatable({}, StatsButtonClass)

	self._statType = statType
	self._button = Instance.new("TextButton")
	self._button.Name = "PS_Button"
	self._button.Text = ""
	self._button.AutoButtonColor = true

	StatsUtils.StyleButton(self._button)

	self._textPanel = StatsMiniTextPanelClass.new(statType)
	self._textPanel:PlaceInParent(
		self._button,
		TextPanelSize,
		TextPanelPosition
	)

	self._graph = StatsAnnotatedGraphClass.new(statType, false)
	self._graph:PlaceInParent(
		self._button,
		GraphSize,
		GraphPosition
	)

	self._textPanel:SetZIndex(StatsUtils.TextZIndex)
	self._graph:SetZIndex(StatsUtils.GraphZIndex)

	if type(StyleWidgets.MakeTabSelectionWidget) == "function" then
		local ok, widget = pcall(
			StyleWidgets.MakeTabSelectionWidget,
			self._button
		)
		if ok then
			self._tabSelection = widget
		end
	end

	self._isSelected = false
	self:_updateTabSelectionState()

	return self
end

function StatsButtonClass:OnPerformanceStatsShouldBeVisibleChanged()
	if self._graph then
		pcall(function()
			self._graph:OnPerformanceStatsShouldBeVisibleChanged()
		end)
	end

	if self._textPanel then
		pcall(function()
			self._textPanel:OnPerformanceStatsShouldBeVisibleChanged()
		end)
	end
end

function StatsButtonClass:SetToggleCallbackFunction(callbackFunction)
	if type(callbackFunction) ~= "function" then
		warn("[StatsButton] Toggle callback must be a function")
		return
	end

	if self._toggleConnection then
		self._toggleConnection:Disconnect()
	end

	self._toggleConnection = self._button.Activated:Connect(function()
		callbackFunction(self._statType)
	end)
end

function StatsButtonClass:SetSizeAndPosition(size, position)
	self._button.Size = size
	self._button.Position = position
end

function StatsButtonClass:SetIsSelected(isSelected)
	self._isSelected = isSelected == true
	self:_updateTabSelectionState()
end

function StatsButtonClass:_updateTabSelectionState()
	if self._tabSelection then
		self._tabSelection.Visible = self._isSelected
	else
		StatsUtils.StyleButtonSelected(self._button, self._isSelected)
	end
end

function StatsButtonClass:SetParent(parent)
	self._button.Parent = parent
end

function StatsButtonClass:SetStatsAggregator(aggregator)
	if self._textPanel then
		self._textPanel:SetStatsAggregator(aggregator)
	end

	if self._graph then
		self._graph:SetStatsAggregator(aggregator)
	end
end

local function safeRequire(moduleScript, name)
	if not moduleScript or not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		warn("[StatsUtils] Missing optional module: " .. name)
		return nil
	end

	local ok, result = pcall(require, moduleScript)
	if not ok then
		warn("[StatsUtils] Could not load " .. name .. ": " .. tostring(result))
		return nil
	end

	return result
end

local TopbarConstants = safeRequire(
	modules and modules:FindFirstChild("TopbarConstants"),
	"TopbarConstants"
)

local StyleWidgets = safeRequire(
	modules and modules:FindFirstChild("StyleWidgets"),
	"StyleWidgets"
)

	Settings = UserSettings()
	GameSettings = Settings and Settings.GameSettings
end)

	frame.BackgroundColor3 = StatsUtils.NormalColor
	frame.BackgroundTransparency = StatsUtils.Transparency

	button.BackgroundTransparency = StatsUtils.Transparency

	textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	textLabel.TextColor3 = StatsUtils.FontColor
	textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
end

function StatsUtils.StyleButtonSelected(frame, isSelected)
	StatsUtils.StyleButton(frame)

	if isSelected then
		frame.BackgroundColor3 = StatsUtils.SelectedBackgroundColor
	end
end

function StatsUtils.FormatTypedValue(value, statType)
	value = tonumber(value) or 0

	if statType == StatsUtils.StatType_Memory then
		return string.format("%.2f MB", value)
	elseif statType == StatsUtils.StatType_CPU
		or statType == StatsUtils.StatType_GPU then
		return string.format("%.2f ms", value)
	elseif statType == StatsUtils.StatType_NetworkSent
		or statType == StatsUtils.StatType_NetworkReceived then
		return string.format("%.2f KB/s", value)
	elseif statType == StatsUtils.StatType_Ping then
		return string.format("%.2f ms", value)
	end

	return tostring(value)
end

function StatsUtils.StyleAverageLine(frame)
	frame.BackgroundColor3 = StatsUtils.GraphAverageLineColor
	frame.BorderSizePixel = StatsUtils.GraphAverageLineBorderThickness
	frame.BorderColor3 = StatsUtils.GraphAverageLineBorderColor
end

function StatsUtils.GetColorForValue(value, target)
	value = tonumber(value) or 0
	target = tonumber(target) or 0

	if target &lt;= 0 then
		return StatsUtils.GraphBarGreenColor
	elseif value &lt; 0.666 * target then
		return StatsUtils.GraphBarGreenColor
	elseif value &lt; 1.333 * target then
		return StatsUtils.GraphBarYellowColor
	end

	return StatsUtils.GraphBarRedColor
end

function StatsUtils.PerformanceStatsShouldBeVisible()
	local visible = false

	pcall(function()
		visible = GameSettings ~= nil
			and GameSettings.PerformanceStatsVisible == true
			and Players.LocalPlayer ~= nil
	end)

	return visible
end

	local self = setmetatable({}, StatsViewerClass)

	self._frameImageLabel = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	self._frameImageLabel.Name = "PS_Viewer"
	self._frameImageLabel.Image =
		"rbxasset://textures/ui/PerformanceStats/BackgroundRounded.png"
	self._frameImageLabel.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice

	self._frameImageLabel.ImageColor3 = StatsUtils.NormalColor

	self._textPanel = nil
	self._statType = nil
	self._graph = nil
	self._aggregator = nil

function StatsViewerClass:OnPerformanceStatsShouldBeVisibleChanged()
	if self._graph then
		pcall(function()
			self._graph:OnPerformanceStatsShouldBeVisibleChanged()
		end)
	end

	if self._textPanel then
		pcall(function()
			self._textPanel:OnPerformanceStatsShouldBeVisibleChanged()
			self._textPanel:OnVisibilityChanged()
		end)
	end
end

	return self._frameImageLabel.Visible

	self._frameImageLabel.Position = position

	self._frameImageLabel.Visible = visible

	if self._graph then
		self._graph:SetVisible(visible)
	end

	if self._textPanel then
		self._textPanel:SetVisible(visible)
	end
end

function StatsViewerClass:SetStatType(statType)
	if self._statType == statType and self._textPanel and self._graph then
		self:_applyStatsAggregator()
		return
	end

	if self._textPanel then
		pcall(function()
			self._textPanel:SetVisible(false)
		end)
	end

	self._statType = statType
	self._frameImageLabel:ClearAllChildren()

	self._textPanel = StatsTextPanelClass.new(statType, true)
	self._textPanel:PlaceInParent(
		self._frameImageLabel,
		TextPanelSize,
		TextPanelPosition
	)

	self._graph = StatsAnnotatedGraphClass.new(statType, true)
	self._graph:PlaceInParent(
		self._frameImageLabel,
		GraphSize,
		GraphPosition
	)

	self._textPanel:SetZIndex(StatsUtils.TextZIndex)
	self._graph:SetZIndex(StatsUtils.GraphZIndex)

	self._graph:SetVisible(self._frameImageLabel.Visible)
	self._textPanel:SetVisible(self._frameImageLabel.Visible)

	self:_applyStatsAggregator()
end

function StatsViewerClass:_applyStatsAggregator()
	if not self._aggregator then
		return
	end

	if self._textPanel then
		self._textPanel:SetStatsAggregator(self._aggregator)
	end

	if self._graph then
		self._graph:SetStatsAggregator(self._aggregator)
	end
end

function StatsViewerClass:SetStatsAggregator(aggregator)
	self._aggregator = aggregator
	self:_applyStatsAggregator()
end
