                                    <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: StatsAggregator.lua
    Yeku compatibility patch.
    Gathers performance statistics and maintains a circular sample buffer.
]]

local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

local function findCoreRoot()
	return CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
		or CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
end

local root = findCoreRoot()
local modules = root and root:FindFirstChild("Modules")
local statsFolder = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("Stats")
local statsUtilsModule = statsFolder and statsFolder:FindFirstChild("StatsUtils")
	or script.Parent:FindFirstChild("StatsUtils")

assert(statsUtilsModule and statsUtilsModule:IsA("ModuleScript"),
	"[StatsAggregator] Missing StatsUtils ModuleScript")

local StatsUtils = require(statsUtilsModule)

local StatsAggregatorClass = {}
StatsAggregatorClass.__index = StatsAggregatorClass

function StatsAggregatorClass.new(statType, numSamples, pauseBetweenSamples)
	assert(type(numSamples) == "number" and numSamples &gt;= 1,
		"numSamples must be at least 1")

	numSamples = math.max(1, math.floor(numSamples))

	local self = setmetatable({}, StatsAggregatorClass)

	self._statType = statType
	self._numSamples = numSamples
	self._pauseBetweenSamples = math.max(0.03, tonumber(pauseBetweenSamples) or 0.25)

	self._statName = StatsUtils.StatNames[statType]
	self._statMaxName = StatsUtils.StatMaxNames[statType]

	self._samples = {}
	for i = 0, numSamples - 1 do
		self._samples[i] = 0
	end

	self._oldestIndex = 0
	self._listeners = {}
	self._nextListenerId = 1001
	self._listening = false

	return self
end

function StatsAggregatorClass:AddListener(callbackFunction)
	assert(type(callbackFunction) == "function",
		"AddListener expects a callback function")

	local id = self._nextListenerId
	self._nextListenerId += 1
	self._listeners[id] = callbackFunction

	return id
end

function StatsAggregatorClass:RemoveListener(listenerId)
	self._listeners[listenerId] = nil
end

function StatsAggregatorClass:_notifyAllListeners()
	for listenerId, callback in pairs(self._listeners) do
		if type(callback) == "function" then
			local ok, err = pcall(callback)
			if not ok then
				warn("[StatsAggregator] Listener " .. tostring(listenerId)
					.. " failed: " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end
	end
end

function StatsAggregatorClass:StartListening()
	if self._listening then
		return
	end

	-- Set this before spawning so repeated calls cannot start
	-- multiple sampling loops.
	self._listening = true

	task.spawn(function()
		while self._listening do
			local ok, value = pcall(function()
				return self:_getStatValue()
			end)

			if not ok then
				warn("[StatsAggregator] Sampling failed: " .. tostring(value))
				value = 0
			end

			self:_storeStatValue(tonumber(value) or 0)
			self:_notifyAllListeners()
			task.wait(self._pauseBetweenSamples)
		end
	end)
end

function StatsAggregatorClass:StopListening()
	self._listening = false
end

function StatsAggregatorClass:GetValues()
	local result = {}

	for i = 0, self._numSamples - 1 do
		local index = (self._oldestIndex + i) % self._numSamples
		result[i + 1] = self._samples[index] or 0
	end

	return result
end

function StatsAggregatorClass:GetAverage()
	local sum = 0

	for i = 0, self._numSamples - 1 do
		sum += tonumber(self._samples[i]) or 0
	end

	return sum / self._numSamples
end

function StatsAggregatorClass:GetLatestValue()
	local index = (self._oldestIndex + self._numSamples - 1)
		% self._numSamples

	return self._samples[index] or 0
end

function StatsAggregatorClass:_storeStatValue(value)
	self._samples[self._oldestIndex] = tonumber(value) or 0
	self._oldestIndex = (self._oldestIndex + 1) % self._numSamples
end

local function readStatsItem(item)
	if not item then
		return 0
	end

	local ok, value = pcall(function()
		return item:GetValue()
	end)

	if ok and type(value) == "number" then
		return value
	end

	ok, value = pcall(function()
		return item.Value
	end)

	if ok and type(value) == "number" then
		return value
	end

	return 0
end

function StatsAggregatorClass:_getStatValue()
	if not self._statName then
		return 0
	end

	local statsService = game:GetService("Stats")
	local performanceStats = statsService:FindFirstChild("PerformanceStats")
	local item = performanceStats
		and performanceStats:FindFirstChild(self._statName)

	return readStatsItem(item)
end

function StatsAggregatorClass:GetTarget()
	if not self._statMaxName then
		return 0
	end

	local statsService = game:GetService("Stats")
	local performanceStats = statsService:FindFirstChild("PerformanceStats")
	local item = performanceStats
		and performanceStats:FindFirstChild(self._statMaxName)

	return readStatsItem(item)
end

return StatsAggregatorClass</ProtectedString>
                                    <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-425</int64>
                                    <BinaryString name="Tags" />
                                </Properties>
                            </Item>
                            <Item class="ModuleScript" referent="RBX522">
                                <Properties>
                                    <BinaryString name="AttributesSerialize" />
                                    <SecurityCapabilities name="Capabilities">0</SecurityCapabilities>
                                    <bool name="DefinesCapabilities">false</bool>
                                    <ContentId name="LinkedSource">
                                        <null />
                                    </ContentId>
                                    <string name="Name">StatsTextPanel</string>
                                    <string name="ScriptGuid">{5A9B6AA4-B2B9-40F3-99C9-09BD11F90982}</string>
                                    <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                    <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: StatsTextPanel.lua

local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

		or CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")

local function requireStatsModule(name)
	local root = findCoreRoot()
	assert(root, "[StatsTextPanel] CoreGui root is missing")

	local modules = root:FindFirstChild("Modules")
	local statsFolder = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("Stats")
	local moduleScript = statsFolder and statsFolder:FindFirstChild(name)

	if not moduleScript then
		moduleScript = script.Parent:FindFirstChild(name)
	end

	assert(
		moduleScript and moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript"),
		"[StatsTextPanel] Missing ModuleScript: " .. name
	)

	return require(moduleScript)
end

local StatsTextPanelClass = {}
StatsTextPanelClass.__index = StatsTextPanelClass

function StatsTextPanelClass.new(statType)
	local self = setmetatable({}, StatsTextPanelClass)

	self._statType = statType
	self._aggregator = nil
	self._listenerId = nil

	self._frame = Instance.new("Frame")
	self._frame.Name = "PS_TextPanel"
	self._frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	self._frame.ZIndex = StatsUtils.TextZIndex or 5

	self._titleLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	self._titleLabel.Name = "Title"
	self._titleLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	self._titleLabel.TextColor3 = StatsUtils.FontColor
	self._titleLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	self._titleLabel.TextSize = 24
	self._titleLabel.Text = self:_getTitle()
	self._titleLabel.Size = TitleSize
	self._titleLabel.Position = TitlePosition
	self._titleLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	self._titleLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
	self._titleLabel.Parent = self._frame

	self:_addCurrentValueWidget()
	self:_addTargetValueWidget()
	self:_addAverageValueWidget()

	return self
end

function StatsTextPanelClass:_getTitle()
	return StatsUtils.TypeToName[self._statType]
		or tostring(self._statType)
end

function StatsTextPanelClass:_addCurrentValueWidget()
	self._currentValueWidget = DecoratedValueLabelClass.new(
		self._statType,
		"Current"
	)

	self._currentValueWidget:PlaceInParent(
		self._frame,
		LegendItemValueSize,
		CurrentValuePosition
	)

	local decorationFrame = self._currentValueWidget:GetDecorationFrame()
	local decoration = Instance.new("ImageLabel")

	decoration.Name = "CurrentValueDecoration"
	decoration.Position = UDim2.new(
		0.5, -StatsUtils.OvalKeySize / 2,
		0.5, -StatsUtils.OvalKeySize / 2
	)
	decoration.Size = UDim2.new(
		0, StatsUtils.OvalKeySize,
		0, StatsUtils.OvalKeySize
	)
	decoration.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	decoration.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/PerformanceStats/OvalKey.png"
	decoration.BorderSizePixel = 0
	decoration.Parent = decorationFrame

	self._currentValueDecoration = decoration
end

function StatsTextPanelClass:_addTargetValueWidget()
	self._targetValueWidget = DecoratedValueLabelClass.new(
		self._statType,
		"Target"
	)

	self._targetValueWidget:PlaceInParent(
		self._frame,
		LegendItemValueSize,
		TargetValuePosition
	)

	local decorationFrame = self._targetValueWidget:GetDecorationFrame()
	local decoration = Instance.new("ImageLabel")

	decoration.Name = "TargetValueDecoration"
	decoration.Position = UDim2.new(
		0.5, -StatsUtils.TargetKeyWidth / 2,
		0.5, -StatsUtils.TargetKeyHeight / 2
	)
	decoration.Size = UDim2.new(
		0, StatsUtils.TargetKeyWidth,
		0, StatsUtils.TargetKeyHeight
	)
	decoration.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	decoration.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/PerformanceStats/TargetKey.png"
	decoration.BorderSizePixel = 0
	decoration.Parent = decorationFrame

	self._targetValueDecoration = decoration
end

function StatsTextPanelClass:_addAverageValueWidget()
	self._averageValueWidget = DecoratedValueLabelClass.new(
		self._statType,
		"Average"
	)

	self._averageValueWidget:PlaceInParent(
		self._frame,
		LegendItemValueSize,
		AverageValuePosition
	)

	local decorationFrame = self._averageValueWidget:GetDecorationFrame()
	local decoration = Instance.new("Frame")

	decoration.Name = "AverageValueDecoration"
	decoration.Position = UDim2.new(
		0, 0, 0.5, -StatsUtils.GraphAverageLineTotalThickness / 2
	)
	decoration.Size = UDim2.new(
		1, 0, 0, StatsUtils.GraphAverageLineInnerThickness
	)
	decoration.Parent = decorationFrame

	StatsUtils.StyleAverageLine(decoration)
	self._averageValueDecoration = decoration
end

function StatsTextPanelClass:PlaceInParent(parent, size, position)
	self._frame.Size = size
	self._frame.Position = position
	self._frame.Parent = parent
end

function StatsTextPanelClass:SetStatsAggregator(aggregator)
	if self._aggregator and self._listenerId then
		self._aggregator:RemoveListener(self._listenerId)
	end

	self._listenerId = nil
	self._aggregator = aggregator

	self:_refreshVisibility()
end

function StatsTextPanelClass:SetVisible(visible)
	self._frame.Visible = visible == true
	self:_refreshVisibility()
end

function StatsTextPanelClass:_shouldBeVisible()
	return self._frame.Visible
		and StatsUtils.PerformanceStatsShouldBeVisible()
end

function StatsTextPanelClass:_stopListening()
	if self._aggregator and self._listenerId then
		self._aggregator:RemoveListener(self._listenerId)
	end

	self._listenerId = nil
end

function StatsTextPanelClass:_startListening()
	if not self._aggregator or self._listenerId then
		return
	end

	self._listenerId = self._aggregator:AddListener(function()
		self:_updateFromAggregator()
	end)
end

function StatsTextPanelClass:_refreshVisibility()
	if self:_shouldBeVisible() then
		self:_startListening()
		self:_updateFromAggregator()
	else
		self:_stopListening()
	end
end

-- Used by settings/performance-stat visibility handlers.
function StatsTextPanelClass:OnPerformanceStatsShouldBeVisibleChanged()
	self:_refreshVisibility()
end

function StatsTextPanelClass:OnVisibilityChanged()
	self:_refreshVisibility()
end

function StatsTextPanelClass:_updateFromAggregator()
	local value = 0
	local average = 0
	local target = 0

	if self._aggregator then
		value = self._aggregator:GetLatestValue() or 0
		average = self._aggregator:GetAverage() or 0
		target = self._aggregator:GetTarget() or 0
	end

	self._currentValueWidget:SetValue(value)
	self._targetValueWidget:SetValue(target)
	self._averageValueWidget:SetValue(average)

	self._currentValueDecoration.ImageColor3 =
		StatsUtils.GetColorForValue(value, target)
end

function StatsTextPanelClass:SetZIndex(zIndex)
	self._frame.ZIndex = zIndex
	self._titleLabel.ZIndex = zIndex

	self._currentValueWidget:SetZIndex(zIndex)
	self._targetValueWidget:SetZIndex(zIndex)
	self._averageValueWidget:SetZIndex(zIndex)
end

local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

local function findCoreRoot()
	return CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
		or CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")

local function requireStatsModule(name)
	local root = findCoreRoot()
	assert(root, "[StatsMiniTextPanel] CoreGui root is missing")

	local modules = root:FindFirstChild("Modules")
	local statsFolder = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("Stats")
	local moduleScript = statsFolder and statsFolder:FindFirstChild(name)

	if not moduleScript then
		moduleScript = script.Parent:FindFirstChild(name)
	end

	assert(
		moduleScript and moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript"),
		"[StatsMiniTextPanel] Missing ModuleScript: " .. name
	)

	return require(moduleScript)
end

local StatsUtils = requireStatsModule("StatsUtils")
local BarGraphClass = requireStatsModule("BarGraph")

local MARGIN = 10
local LABEL_WIDTH = 30

local StatsAnnotatedGraphClass = {}
StatsAnnotatedGraphClass.__index = StatsAnnotatedGraphClass

function StatsAnnotatedGraphClass.new(statType, isMaximized)
	local self = setmetatable({}, StatsAnnotatedGraphClass)

	self._statType = statType
	self._statMaxName = StatsUtils.StatMaxNames[statType]
	self._isMaximized = isMaximized == true

	self._values = {}
	self._average = 0
	self._target = 0
	self._axisMax = 1
	self._aggregator = nil
	self._listenerId = nil

	self._frame = Instance.new("Frame")
	self._frame.Name = "PS_AnnotatedGraph"
	self._frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	self._frame.ZIndex = StatsUtils.GraphZIndex or 2

	self._topLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	self._topLabel.Name = "PS_TopAxisLabel"
	self._topLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	self._topLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	self._topLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
	self._topLabel.TextSize = 10
	self._topLabel.Parent = self._frame

	self._bottomLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	self._bottomLabel.Name = "PS_BottomAxisLabel"
	self._bottomLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	self._bottomLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	self._bottomLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Bottom
	self._bottomLabel.TextSize = 10
	self._bottomLabel.Parent = self._frame

	StatsUtils.StyleTextWidget(self._topLabel)
	StatsUtils.StyleTextWidget(self._bottomLabel)

	self._graph = BarGraphClass.new(self._isMaximized)

	self:_layoutElements()
	self:_updateValuesAndRender()

	return self
end

function StatsAnnotatedGraphClass:SetZIndex(zIndex)
	self._frame.ZIndex = zIndex
	self._topLabel.ZIndex = zIndex
	self._bottomLabel.ZIndex = zIndex

	self._graph:SetZIndex(zIndex)
end

function StatsAnnotatedGraphClass:_layoutElements()
	local labelWidth = self._isMaximized and LABEL_WIDTH or 0

	self._topLabel.Visible = self._isMaximized
	self._bottomLabel.Visible = self._isMaximized

	local graphPosition = UDim2.new(0, MARGIN, 0, MARGIN)
	local graphSize = UDim2.new(
		1, -(2 * MARGIN + labelWidth),
		1, -2 * MARGIN
	)

	self._topLabel.Position = UDim2.new(
		1, -(MARGIN + labelWidth), 0, MARGIN
	)
	self._topLabel.Size = UDim2.new(
		0, labelWidth, 0.333, -2 * MARGIN
	)

	self._bottomLabel.Position = UDim2.new(
		1, -(MARGIN + labelWidth), 0.666, MARGIN
	)
	self._bottomLabel.Size = UDim2.new(
		0, labelWidth, 0.333, -2 * MARGIN
	)

	self._graph:PlaceInParent(self._frame, graphSize, graphPosition)
end

function StatsAnnotatedGraphClass:PlaceInParent(parent, size, position)
	self._frame.Position = position
	self._frame.Size = size
	self._frame.Parent = parent
end

function StatsAnnotatedGraphClass:SetStatsAggregator(aggregator)
	if self._aggregator and self._listenerId then
		self._aggregator:RemoveListener(self._listenerId)
	end

	self._listenerId = nil
	self._aggregator = aggregator

	self:_refreshVisibility()
end

function StatsAnnotatedGraphClass:_stopListening()
	if self._aggregator and self._listenerId then
		self._aggregator:RemoveListener(self._listenerId)
	end

	self._listenerId = nil
end

function StatsAnnotatedGraphClass:_startListening()
	if not self._aggregator or self._listenerId then
		return
	end

	self._listenerId = self._aggregator:AddListener(function()
		self:_updateValuesAndRender()
	end)
end

function StatsAnnotatedGraphClass:_shouldBeVisible()
	return self._frame.Visible
		and StatsUtils.PerformanceStatsShouldBeVisible()
end

function StatsAnnotatedGraphClass:SetVisible(visible)
	self._frame.Visible = visible == true
	self:_refreshVisibility()
end

function StatsAnnotatedGraphClass:_refreshVisibility()
	if self:_shouldBeVisible() then
		self:_startListening()
		self:_updateValuesAndRender()
	else
		self:_stopListening()
	end
end

function StatsAnnotatedGraphClass:OnPerformanceStatsShouldBeVisibleChanged()
	self:_refreshVisibility()
end

-- Choose a stable positive axis range. The original recursive routine
-- could approach zero indefinitely when the target was zero.
function StatsAnnotatedGraphClass:_calculateAxisMax()
	local maxValue = math.max(0, tonumber(self._target) or 0)

	for _, value in ipairs(self._values) do
		maxValue = math.max(maxValue, tonumber(value) or 0)
	end

	maxValue = math.max(maxValue * 1.1, 1)

	local magnitude = 10 ^ math.floor(math.log10(maxValue))
	local scaled = maxValue / magnitude
	local niceScale

	if scaled &lt;= 1 then
		niceScale = 1
	elseif scaled &lt;= 2 then
		niceScale = 2
	elseif scaled &lt;= 5 then
		niceScale = 5
	else
		niceScale = 10
	end

	self._axisMax = niceScale * magnitude
end

function StatsAnnotatedGraphClass:_render()
	self._graph:SetAxisMax(self._axisMax)
	self._graph:SetValues(self._values)
	self._graph:SetAverage(self._average)
	self._graph:SetTarget(self._target)
	self._graph:Render()

	self._topLabel.Text = string.format("%.2f", self._axisMax)
	self._bottomLabel.Text = string.format("%.2f", 0)
end

function StatsAnnotatedGraphClass:_updateValuesAndRender()
	self._values = {}
	self._average = 0
	self._target = 0

	if self._aggregator then
		self._values = self._aggregator:GetValues() or {}
		self._average = tonumber(self._aggregator:GetAverage()) or 0
		self._target = tonumber(self._aggregator:GetTarget()) or 0
	end

	self:_calculateAxisMax()

	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		self:_render()
	end)

	if not ok then
		warn("[StatsAnnotatedGraph] Rendering failed: " .. tostring(err))
	end
end

--==============================================================
-- DIAGNOSTICS
--==============================================================

local function log(message)
	print("[ChatSelector] " .. tostring(message))
end

local function warnScript(message)
	warn("[ChatSelector] " .. tostring(message))
end

local function traceback(message)
	if debug and debug.traceback then
		return debug.traceback(tostring(message), 2)
	end

	return tostring(message)
end

local unpackValues = table.unpack or unpack

--==============================================================
-- SIGNALS
--==============================================================

local function createSignal()
	local listeners = {}
	local signal = {}

	function signal:Connect(callback)
		assert(
			type(callback) == "function",
			"[ChatSelector] Signal callback must be a function."
		)

		local entry = {
			Callback = callback,
			Connected = true,
		}

		local connection = {}

		function connection:Disconnect()
			entry.Connected = false
		end

		connection.disconnect = connection.Disconnect

		table.insert(listeners, entry)

		return connection
	end

	signal.connect = signal.Connect

	function signal:Fire(...)
		local arguments = table.pack(...)

		for _, entry in ipairs(listeners) do
			if entry.Connected then
				local ok, err = xpcall(function()
					entry.Callback(
						unpackValues(arguments, 1, arguments.n)
					)
				end, traceback)

				if not ok then
					warnScript(
						"Signal callback failed: " .. tostring(err)
					)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	signal.fire = signal.Fire

	return signal
end

local function connectSignal(source, callback)
	if not source then
		return nil
	end

	local ok, method = pcall(function()
		return source.Connect or source.connect
	end)

	if not ok or type(method) ~= "function" then
		return nil
	end

	local connected, connection = pcall(function()
		return method(source, callback)
	end)

	if connected then
		return connection
	end

	return nil
end

local function fireSignal(signal, ...)
	if not signal then
		return
	end

	local ok, method = pcall(function()
		return signal.Fire or signal.fire
	end)

	if not ok or type(method) ~= "function" then
		return
	end

	local arguments = table.pack(...)

	local fired, err = pcall(function()
		method(signal, unpackValues(arguments, 1, arguments.n))
	end)

	if not fired then
		warnScript("Could not fire signal: " .. tostring(err))
	end
end

--==============================================================
-- CHAT SETTINGS
--==============================================================

local function readLegacyBoolean(propertyName, defaultValue)
	local ok, value = pcall(function()
		return Players[propertyName]
	end)

	if ok and type(value) == "boolean" then
		return value
	end

	return defaultValue
end

local ClassicChatEnabled =
	readLegacyBoolean("ClassicChat", true)

local BubbleChatEnabled =
	readLegacyBoolean("BubbleChat", false)

local backend = nil
local backendReady = false
local backendFailed = false

local state = {
	Visible = true,
}

local interface = {
	ChatBarFocusChanged = createSignal(),
	VisibilityStateChanged = createSignal(),
	MessagesChanged = createSignal(),
	BubbleChatOnlySet = createSignal(),
	ChatDisabled = createSignal(),

	IncomingMessage = createSignal(),
	OutgoingMessage = createSignal(),
	SpecialKeyPressed = createSignal(),
	TextChannelsChanged = createSignal(),
}

local backendConnections = {}

--==============================================================
-- BACKEND INVOCATION
--==============================================================

local function invokeBackend(methodName, ...)
	if not backend then
		return false, false, nil
	end

	local ok, method = pcall(function()
		return backend[methodName]
	end)

	if not ok or type(method) ~= "function" then
		return false, false, nil
	end

	local arguments = table.pack(...)

	local results = table.pack(xpcall(function()
		return method(
			backend,
			unpackValues(arguments, 1, arguments.n)
		)
	end, traceback))

	if not results[1] then
		warnScript(
			"NewChat."
				.. methodName
				.. " failed: "
				.. tostring(results[2])
		)

		return true, false, results[2]
	end

	return true, true, unpackValues(results, 2, results.n)
end

local function forwardBackendSignal(signalName)
	if not backend then
		return
	end

	local ok, source = pcall(function()
		return backend[signalName]
	end)

	if not ok or not source then
		return
	end

	local connection = connectSignal(source, function(...)
		if signalName == "VisibilityStateChanged" then
			local visible = ...

			if type(visible) == "boolean" then
				state.Visible = visible
			end
		end

		fireSignal(interface[signalName], ...)
	end)

	if connection then
		table.insert(backendConnections, connection)
	end
end

local function connectBackendSignals()
	for _, name in ipairs({
		"ChatBarFocusChanged",
		"VisibilityStateChanged",
		"MessagesChanged",
		"BubbleChatOnlySet",
		"ChatDisabled",
		"IncomingMessage",
		"OutgoingMessage",
		"SpecialKeyPressed",
		"TextChannelsChanged",
		}) do
		forwardBackendSignal(name)
	end
end

--==============================================================
-- PUBLIC VISIBILITY API
--==============================================================

local function queryBackendVisibility()
	local found, success, visible = invokeBackend("GetVisibility")
	if found and success and type(visible) == "boolean" then
		return visible, true
	end
	return state.Visible, false
end

local function queryLegacyGuiVisibility()
	local player = Players.LocalPlayer
	local playerGui = player and player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
	local chatGui = playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("Chat")
	if not chatGui or not chatGui:IsA("ScreenGui") then
		return nil
	end
	if not chatGui.Enabled then
		return false
	end
	local chatWindow = chatGui:FindFirstChild("ChatWindow")
	if chatWindow and chatWindow:IsA("GuiObject") then
		return chatWindow.Visible
	end
	return chatGui.Enabled
end

function interface:SetVisible(visible)
	if type(visible) ~= "boolean" then
		warnScript("SetVisible expects a boolean.")
		return false
	end

	-- Requests made during asynchronous startup are remembered and applied
	-- once NewChat returns its backend interface.
	if not backendReady or not backend then
		local changed = state.Visible ~= visible
		state.Visible = visible
		if changed then
			fireSignal(interface.VisibilityStateChanged, visible)
		end
		return true
	end

	local found, success, result = invokeBackend("SetVisible", visible)
	if not found or not success then
		warnScript("NewChat.SetVisible did not complete.")
		return false
	end

	local actual, hasActual = queryBackendVisibility()
	if hasActual then
		local changed = state.Visible ~= actual
		state.Visible = actual
		-- NewChat normally forwards this event synchronously. This fallback
		-- also supports older backends that expose no visibility signal.
		if changed then
			fireSignal(interface.VisibilityStateChanged, actual)
		end
		return actual == visible and result ~= false
	end

	state.Visible = visible
	return result ~= false
end

function interface:ToggleVisibility()
	-- Never issue an independent backend toggle followed by a SetVisible call.
	-- That sequence can close and immediately reopen the window.
	return self:SetVisible(not self:GetVisibility())
end

function interface:GetVisibility()
	local visible, hasActual = queryBackendVisibility()
	if hasActual then
		state.Visible = visible
	end

	-- The backend is authoritative normally. If the actual custom legacy GUI
	-- is hidden (e.g. a stale controller instance), report what is on screen.
	if backendReady then
		local guiVisible = queryLegacyGuiVisibility()
		if guiVisible ~= nil then
			state.Visible = guiVisible
		end
	end
	return state.Visible
end

function interface:FocusChatBar()
	local found, success, result = invokeBackend("FocusChatBar")
	if not found or not success then
		return false
	end
	-- Historical ChatMain implementations return nil on successful focus.
	return result ~= false
end

function interface:GetMessageCount()
	local found, success, count =
		invokeBackend("GetMessageCount")

	if found and success and type(count) == "number" then
		return count
	end

	return 0
end

function interface:TopbarEnabledChanged(...)
	return invokeBackend("TopbarEnabledChanged", ...)
end

function interface:IsFocused(useWasFocused)
	local found, success, focused =
		invokeBackend("IsFocused", useWasFocused)

	return found and success and type(focused) == "boolean"
		and focused
		or false
end

function interface:ClassicChatEnabled()
	local found, success, value =
		invokeBackend("ClassicChatEnabled")

	if found and success and type(value) == "boolean" then
		return value
	end

	return ClassicChatEnabled
end

function interface:IsBubbleChatOnly()
	local found, success, value =
		invokeBackend("IsBubbleChatOnly")

	if found and success and type(value) == "boolean" then
		return value
	end

	return BubbleChatEnabled and not ClassicChatEnabled
end

function interface:IsDisabled()
	local found, success, value =
		invokeBackend("IsDisabled")

	if found and success and type(value) == "boolean" then
		return value
	end

	return not (ClassicChatEnabled or BubbleChatEnabled)
end

function interface:SendMessage(text, channelName, metadata)
	local found, success, result, detail =
		invokeBackend(
			"SendMessage",
			text,
			channelName,
			metadata
		)

	if found then
		return success and result ~= false, detail or result
	end

	return false, "NewChat has not finished initializing."
end

function interface:DisplaySystemMessage(text, channelName, metadata)
	local found, success, result, detail =
		invokeBackend(
			"DisplaySystemMessage",
			text,
			channelName,
			metadata
		)

	if found then
		return success and result ~= false, detail or result
	end

	return false, "NewChat has not finished initializing."
end

--==============================================================
-- CORE API COMPATIBILITY
--==============================================================

local function tryRegisterSetCore(name, callback)
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		StarterGui:RegisterSetCore(name, callback)
	end)

	if not ok then
		warnScript(
			"RegisterSetCore("
				.. name
				.. ") is unavailable: "
				.. tostring(err)
		)
	end

	return ok
end

local function tryRegisterGetCore(name, callback)
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		StarterGui:RegisterGetCore(name, callback)
	end)

	if not ok then
		warnScript(
			"RegisterGetCore("
				.. name
				.. ") is unavailable: "
				.. tostring(err)
		)
	end

	return ok
end

tryRegisterSetCore("ChatMakeSystemMessage", function(data)
	local found, success = invokeBackend("SetCore", "ChatMakeSystemMessage", data)

	if not found or not success then
		warnScript(
			"ChatMakeSystemMessage could not be delivered to NewChat."
		)
	end
end)

tryRegisterSetCore("ChatActive", function(visible)
	interface:SetVisible(visible)
end)

tryRegisterGetCore("ChatActive", function()
	return interface:GetVisibility()
end)

tryRegisterGetCore("UseNewLuaChat", function()
	return true
end)

--==============================================================
-- SYSTEM MESSAGE DEFERRED DELIVERY
--==============================================================

local queuedSystemMessages = {}
local MAX_QUEUED_SYSTEM_MESSAGES = 100

local function queueSystemMessage(data)
	if #queuedSystemMessages &gt;= MAX_QUEUED_SYSTEM_MESSAGES then
		table.remove(queuedSystemMessages, 1)

		warnScript("System-message queue full; dropped its oldest entry.")
	end

	table.insert(queuedSystemMessages, data)
end

local function deliverSystemMessage(data)
	if not backendReady or not backend then
		queueSystemMessage(data)
		return
	end

	local found, success = invokeBackend(
		"SetCore",
		"ChatMakeSystemMessage",
		data
	)

	if not found or not success then
		queueSystemMessage(data)
	end
end

local function flushSystemMessages()
	if not backendReady then
		return
	end

	local pending = queuedSystemMessages
	queuedSystemMessages = {}

	for _, data in ipairs(pending) do
		deliverSystemMessage(data)
	end
end

--==============================================================
-- CHAT BACKEND STARTUP
--
-- IMPORTANT:
-- Run require(NewChat) without starting Modules.Chat.
-- NewChat must return its public interface even if the old
-- ChatScript coordinator has an initialization problem.
--==============================================================

local function initializeBackend()
	local newChatModule = Modules:FindFirstChild("NewChat")

	if not newChatModule or not newChatModule:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		backendFailed = true
		warnScript("NewChat ModuleScript is missing.")
		return
	end

	log("Starting NewChat backend: " .. newChatModule:GetFullName())

	local ok, result = xpcall(function()
		return require(newChatModule)
	end, traceback)

	if not ok then
		backendFailed = true

		warnScript(
			"NewChat module failed:\n" .. tostring(result)
		)

		return
	end

	if type(result) ~= "table" then
		backendFailed = true

		warnScript(
			"NewChat must return an API table; received "
				.. typeof(result)
		)

		return
	end

	backend = result
	backendReady = true

	connectBackendSignals()

	-- Preserve the latest requested visibility state.
	invokeBackend("SetVisible", state.Visible)

	flushSystemMessages()

	log("NewChat compatibility API initialized.")
	log("Modules.Chat was not required.")
end

-- Returning the selector interface does not wait for require(NewChat).
-- This keeps the rest of the CoreScripts startup from blocking.
task.spawn(initializeBackend)

--==============================================================
-- SETCORE MESSAGE QUEUE
--==============================================================

local originalSystemMessageHandler = function(data)
	if backendReady then
		deliverSystemMessage(data)
	else
		queueSystemMessage(data)
	end
end

-- Re-register after defining the deferred handler so messages received
-- before the asynchronous backend load are retained.
tryRegisterSetCore(
	"ChatMakeSystemMessage",
	originalSystemMessageHandler
)

--==============================================================
-- READ-ONLY NATIVE CHAT STATE
--==============================================================

local function refreshNativeChatState()
	local ok, enabled = pcall(function()
		return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat)
	end)

	if ok and type(enabled) == "boolean" then
		-- Observe native Chat state only. Never change it here.
		interface.NativeChatEnabled = enabled
	end
end

refreshNativeChatState()

local changedOK, changedSignal = pcall(function()
	return StarterGui.CoreGuiChangedSignal
end)

if changedOK and changedSignal then
	local connection = connectSignal(
		changedSignal,
		function(coreGuiType, enabled)
			if coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat
				or coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.All then

				interface.NativeChatEnabled = enabled == true
			end
		end
	)

	if connection then
		table.insert(backendConnections, connection)
	end
end

--==============================================================
-- STATUS
--==============================================================

function interface:GetInitializationStatus()
	if backendReady then
		return "Ready"
	elseif backendFailed then
		return "Failed"
	end

	return "Initializing"
end

log("Chat selector initialized.")
return interface

    Runtime:
        CoreGui.NewRobloxGui.Modules.NewChat

    Responsibilities:
        - Preserve the historical chat/topbar API.
        - Provide a direct registration method for custom chat UI.

        - Receive filtered messages through TextChatService.MessageReceived.
        - Forward UI commands using existing BindableEvents and

    Does not:
        - Create or render a chat GUI.
        - Copy, clone, move, or destroy modules.
        - Use StarterGui:RegisterSetCore/RegisterGetCore.
        - Use Players:Chat().
        - Use Players.ClassicChat or Players.BubbleChat.
        - Modify ChatWindowConfiguration or ChatInputBarConfiguration.
        - Force-enable or force-disable native CoreGui.
]]

-- ============================================================
-- SERVICES AND RUNTIME HIERARCHY
-- ============================================================

local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

assert(
	RunService:IsClient(),
	"[NewChat] This module must run on the client."
)

local Modules = script.Parent
local RobloxGui = Modules and Modules.Parent

assert(
	RobloxGui
		and RobloxGui.Name == "NewRobloxGui"
		and RobloxGui.Parent == CoreGui,
	"[NewChat] Expected CoreGui.NewRobloxGui.Modules.NewChat."
)

-- ============================================================
-- DIAGNOSTICS
-- ============================================================

local function log(message)
	print("[NewChat] " .. tostring(message))
end

local function warnChat(message)
	warn("[NewChat] " .. tostring(message))
end

local function traceback(message)
	if debug and debug.traceback then
		return debug.traceback(tostring(message), 2)
	end

	return tostring(message)
end

local unpackValues = table.unpack or unpack

-- ============================================================
-- SIGNAL IMPLEMENTATION
--
-- Provides both modern Connect/Fire and the historical
-- connect/fire spellings so existing modules can use the API.
-- ============================================================

local function createSignal()
	local listeners = {}
	local signal = {}

	function signal:Connect(callback)
		assert(
			type(callback) == "function",
			"Signal callback must be a function."
		)

		local entry = {
			Callback = callback,
			Connected = true,
		}

		local connection = {}

		function connection:Disconnect()
			entry.Connected = false
		end

		connection.disconnect = connection.Disconnect

		table.insert(listeners, entry)

		return connection
	end

	signal.connect = signal.Connect

	function signal:Fire(...)
		local arguments = table.pack(...)

		for _, entry in ipairs(listeners) do
			if entry.Connected then
				local success, err = xpcall(function()
					entry.Callback(
						unpackValues(arguments, 1, arguments.n)
					)
				end, traceback)

				if not success then
					warnChat(
						"Signal callback failed: " .. tostring(err)
					)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	signal.fire = signal.Fire

	return signal
end

local function fireSignal(signal, ...)
	if not signal then
		return
	end

	local callback = signal.Fire or signal.fire

	if type(callback) ~= "function" then
		return
	end

	local success, err = pcall(callback, signal, ...)

	if not success then
		warnChat("Could not fire signal: " .. tostring(err))
	end
end

-- ============================================================
-- TEXT CHANNEL DISCOVERY
-- ============================================================

local function getTextChannelsFolder()
	local folder = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels")

	if folder and folder:IsA("Folder") then
		return folder
	end

	return nil
end

local function getTextChannels()
	local channels = {}
	local seen = {}

	local function addChannel(instance)
		if instance:IsA("TextChannel") and not seen[instance] then
			seen[instance] = true
			table.insert(channels, instance)
		end
	end

	local folder = getTextChannelsFolder()

	if folder then
		for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
			addChannel(child)
		end
	end

	-- Also accept channels parented directly beneath TextChatService.
	for _, child in ipairs(TextChatService:GetChildren()) do
		addChannel(child)
	end

	return channels
end

local function findTextChannel(channelName)
	if typeof(channelName) == "Instance" then
		if channelName:IsA("TextChannel")
			and channelName:IsDescendantOf(TextChatService) then
			return channelName
		end

		return nil
	end

	local folder = getTextChannelsFolder()

	if type(channelName) == "string" then
		local channel = folder and folder:FindFirstChild(channelName)

		if channel and channel:IsA("TextChannel") then
			return channel
		end

		channel = TextChatService:FindFirstChild(channelName)

		if channel and channel:IsA("TextChannel") then
			return channel
		end

		return nil
	end

	-- Prefer the conventional public channel.
	local general = findTextChannel("RBXGeneral")

	if general then
		return general
	end

	-- Next, use the first available TextChannel.
	return getTextChannels()[1]
end

local function hasModernTextChatBackend()
	if #getTextChannels() &gt; 0 then
		return true
	end

	-- Default text channels may be created after startup.
	local success, createDefaults = pcall(function()
		return TextChatService.CreateDefaultTextChannels
	end)

	return success and createDefaults == true
end

local function getBubbleChatEnabled()
	local configuration =
		TextChatService:FindFirstChild("BubbleChatConfiguration")

	if not configuration then
		return false
	end

	local success, enabled = pcall(function()
		return configuration.Enabled
	end)

	return success and enabled == true
end

-- ============================================================
-- MODULE API AND STATE
-- ============================================================

local moduleApiTable = {}

local ChatWindowState = {
	Visible = true,
	MessageCount = 0,
	TopbarEnabled = true,
	CoreGuiEnabled = true,
	ChatBarDisabled = false,
}

local communicationsConnections = {
	ChatWindow = {},
	SetCore = {},
	GetCore = {},
}

local eventConnections = {}
local runtimeConnections = {}
local SetCoreCache = {}

local ChatTypesSet = false
local BubbleChatEnabled = getBubbleChatEnabled()
local MAX_SETCORE_QUEUE = 100

moduleApiTable.ChatBarFocusChanged = createSignal()
moduleApiTable.VisibilityStateChanged = createSignal()
moduleApiTable.MessagesChanged = createSignal()

moduleApiTable.BubbleChatOnlySet = createSignal()
moduleApiTable.ChatDisabled = createSignal()

-- Modern frontend-facing signals.
moduleApiTable.IncomingMessage = createSignal()
moduleApiTable.OutgoingMessage = createSignal()
moduleApiTable.SpecialKeyPressed = createSignal()
moduleApiTable.TextChannelsChanged = createSignal()

-- ============================================================
-- LEGACY CHAT-TYPE API
-- ============================================================

function moduleApiTable:ClassicChatEnabled()
	return hasModernTextChatBackend()
end

function moduleApiTable:IsBubbleChatOnly()
	return getBubbleChatEnabled()
		and not hasModernTextChatBackend()
end

function moduleApiTable:IsDisabled()
	return not hasModernTextChatBackend()
		and not getBubbleChatEnabled()
end

local function setInitialChatTypes(chatTypesTable)
	if ChatTypesSet then
		return
	end

	ChatTypesSet = true
	BubbleChatEnabled = getBubbleChatEnabled()

	-- On platforms where BubbleChatConfiguration isn't present,
	-- accept the old frontend's bubble-chat hint as a fallback.
	if not TextChatService:FindFirstChild("BubbleChatConfiguration")
		and type(chatTypesTable) == "table"
		and type(chatTypesTable.BubbleChatEnabled) == "boolean" then

		BubbleChatEnabled = chatTypesTable.BubbleChatEnabled
	end

	if not hasModernTextChatBackend() and not BubbleChatEnabled then
		fireSignal(moduleApiTable.ChatDisabled)
	elseif BubbleChatEnabled and not hasModernTextChatBackend() then
		fireSignal(moduleApiTable.BubbleChatOnlySet)
	end
end

-- ============================================================
-- BINDABLE LOOKUPS
-- ============================================================

local function findInCollectionByKeyAndType(collection, key, className)
	if type(collection) ~= "table" then
		return nil
	end

	local instance = collection[key]

	if typeof(instance) == "Instance"
		and instance:IsA(className) then
		return instance
	end

	return nil
end

local function dispatchEvent(eventName, ...)
	local event = findInCollectionByKeyAndType(
		communicationsConnections.ChatWindow,
		eventName,
		"BindableEvent"
	)

	if not event then
		return false
	end

	-- Luau does not allow ... to be referenced inside a nested,
	-- non-vararg function. Capture the varargs before pcall and
	-- unpack the captured array inside the callback instead.
	local arguments = table.pack(...)

	local success, err = pcall(function()
		event:Fire(unpackValues(arguments, 1, arguments.n))
	end)

	if not success then
		warnChat(
			"UI event "
				.. eventName
				.. " failed: "
				.. tostring(err)
		)

		return false
	end

	return true
end

local function attemptInvokeFunction(functionName, ...)
	local callback = findInCollectionByKeyAndType(
		communicationsConnections.ChatWindow,
		functionName,
		"BindableFunction"
	)

	if not callback then
		return false, nil
	end

	local arguments = table.pack(...)

	local results = table.pack(xpcall(function()
		return callback:Invoke(
			unpackValues(arguments, 1, arguments.n)
		)
	end, traceback))

	if not results[1] then
		warnChat(
			"UI query "
				.. functionName
				.. " failed: "
				.. tostring(results[2])
		)

		return false, nil
	end

	return true, unpackValues(results, 2, results.n)
end

local function disconnectAll(connections)
	for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do
		pcall(function()
			connection:Disconnect()
		end)
	end

	table.clear(connections)
end

-- ============================================================
-- HISTORICAL PUBLIC INTERFACE
-- ============================================================

function moduleApiTable:ToggleVisibility()
	local visible = not self:GetVisibility()

	ChatWindowState.Visible = visible

	if not dispatchEvent("ToggleVisibility") then
		fireSignal(self.VisibilityStateChanged, visible)
	end
end

function moduleApiTable:SetVisible(visible)
	if type(visible) ~= "boolean" then
		warnChat("SetVisible expects a boolean.")
		return
	end

	ChatWindowState.Visible = visible

	if not dispatchEvent("SetVisible", visible) then
		fireSignal(self.VisibilityStateChanged, visible)
	end
end

function moduleApiTable:FocusChatBar()
	dispatchEvent("FocusChatBar")
end

function moduleApiTable:GetVisibility()
	local success, value = attemptInvokeFunction("GetVisibility")

	if success and type(value) == "boolean" then
		ChatWindowState.Visible = value
	end

	return ChatWindowState.Visible
end

function moduleApiTable:GetMessageCount()
	local success, value = attemptInvokeFunction("GetMessageCount")

	if success and type(value) == "number" then
		return value
	end

	return ChatWindowState.MessageCount
end

function moduleApiTable:TopbarEnabledChanged(enabled)
	if type(enabled) == "boolean" then
		ChatWindowState.TopbarEnabled = enabled
	end

	dispatchEvent("TopbarEnabledChanged", ChatWindowState.TopbarEnabled)
end

function moduleApiTable:IsFocused(useWasFocused)
	local success, value =
		attemptInvokeFunction("IsFocused", useWasFocused)

	if success and type(value) == "boolean" then
		return value
	end

	return false
end

-- ============================================================
-- MODERN MESSAGE BACKEND
-- ============================================================

function moduleApiTable:SendMessage(text, channelName, metadata)
	if type(text) ~= "string" or text:match("^%s*$") then
		return false, "Message must be a non-empty string."
	end

	local channel = findTextChannel(channelName)

	if not channel then
		return false, "No TextChannel is currently available."
	end

	if metadata ~= nil and type(metadata) ~= "string" then
		return false, "Metadata must be a string or nil."
	end

	local success, result = pcall(function()
		return channel:SendAsync(text, metadata)
	end)

	if not success then
		warnChat("TextChannel:SendAsync failed: " .. tostring(result))
		return false, tostring(result)
	end

	return true, result
end

function moduleApiTable:DisplaySystemMessage(text, channelName, metadata)
	if type(text) ~= "string" or text == "" then
		return false, "System message must be a non-empty string."
	end

	local channel = findTextChannel(channelName)

	if not channel then
		return false, "No TextChannel is currently available."
	end

	local success, result = pcall(function()
		return channel:DisplaySystemMessage(text, metadata)
	end)

	if not success then
		warnChat(
			"TextChannel:DisplaySystemMessage failed: "
				.. tostring(result)
		)

		return false, tostring(result)
	end

	return true, result
end

function moduleApiTable:GetTextChannels()
	return getTextChannels()
end

function moduleApiTable:GetDefaultTextChannel()
	return findTextChannel()
end

-- ============================================================
-- SYSTEM MESSAGE COMPATIBILITY
-- ============================================================

function moduleApiTable:SetCore(setCoreName, data)
	if type(setCoreName) ~= "string" then
		return false, "SetCore name must be a string."
	end

	if setCoreName == "ChatActive" then
		self:SetVisible(data)
		return true
	end

	if setCoreName == "ChatBarDisabled" then
		if type(data) ~= "boolean" then
			return false, "ChatBarDisabled expects a boolean."
		end

		ChatWindowState.ChatBarDisabled = data
	end

	local event = findInCollectionByKeyAndType(
		communicationsConnections.SetCore,
		setCoreName,
		"BindableEvent"
	)

	if event then
		event:Fire(data)
		return true
	end

	if SetCoreCache[setCoreName] == nil then
		SetCoreCache[setCoreName] = {}
	end

	local queue = SetCoreCache[setCoreName]

	if #queue &gt;= MAX_SETCORE_QUEUE then
		table.remove(queue, 1)
	end

	table.insert(queue, data)

	return false, "No connected UI handler; value was queued."
end

function moduleApiTable:GetCore(getCoreName, ...)
	if type(getCoreName) ~= "string" then
		return nil
	end

	if getCoreName == "ChatActive" then
		return self:GetVisibility()
	elseif getCoreName == "UseNewLuaChat" then
		return hasModernTextChatBackend()
	elseif getCoreName == "ChatBarDisabled" then
		local success, result =
			attemptInvokeFunction(getCoreName, ...)

		if success then
			return result
		end

		return ChatWindowState.ChatBarDisabled
	end

	local callback = findInCollectionByKeyAndType(
		communicationsConnections.GetCore,
		getCoreName,
		"BindableFunction"
	)

	if callback then
		local arguments = table.pack(...)

		local success, result = pcall(function()
			return callback:Invoke(
				unpackValues(arguments, 1, arguments.n)
			)
		end)

		if success then
			return result
		end

		warnChat(
			"GetCore handler "
				.. getCoreName
				.. " failed: "
				.. tostring(result)
		)
	end

	return nil
end

-- ============================================================
-- DIRECT UI REGISTRATION
-- ============================================================

function moduleApiTable:RegisterCoreGuiConnections(containerTable)
	if type(containerTable) ~= "table" then
		warnChat("RegisterCoreGuiConnections expects a table.")
		return false
	end

	local chatWindow = containerTable.ChatWindow

	if type(chatWindow) ~= "table" then
		warnChat("Connection table must contain ChatWindow.")
		return false
	end

	local setCoreCollection = containerTable.SetCore
	local getCoreCollection = containerTable.GetCore

	if setCoreCollection ~= nil and type(setCoreCollection) ~= "table" then
		warnChat("SetCore must be a table when supplied.")
		return false
	end

	if getCoreCollection ~= nil and type(getCoreCollection) ~= "table" then
		warnChat("GetCore must be a table when supplied.")
		return false
	end

	-- Replacing a frontend must disconnect the previous frontend.
	disconnectAll(eventConnections)

	communicationsConnections.ChatWindow = {}
	communicationsConnections.SetCore = {}
	communicationsConnections.GetCore = {}

	local eventNames = {
		"ToggleVisibility",
		"SetVisible",
		"FocusChatBar",
		"TopbarEnabledChanged",
		"SpecialKeyPressed",
		"CoreGuiEnabled",
	}

	local functionNameList = {
		"GetVisibility",
		"GetMessageCount",
		"IsFocused",
	}

	for _, name in ipairs(eventNames) do
		communicationsConnections.ChatWindow[name] =
			findInCollectionByKeyAndType(
				chatWindow,
				name,
				"BindableEvent"
			)
	end

	for _, name in ipairs(functionNameList) do
		communicationsConnections.ChatWindow[name] =
			findInCollectionByKeyAndType(
				chatWindow,
				name,
				"BindableFunction"
			)
	end

	-- Let the existing custom UI tell us when focus or visibility
	-- changes. Actual chat transport remains TextChatService-owned.
	for _, name in ipairs({
		"ChatBarFocusChanged",
		"VisibilityStateChanged",
		}) do
		local bindable = findInCollectionByKeyAndType(
			chatWindow,
			name,
			"BindableEvent"
		)

		if bindable then
			local connection = bindable.Event:Connect(function(...)
				if name == "VisibilityStateChanged" then
					local visible = ...

					if type(visible) == "boolean" then
						ChatWindowState.Visible = visible
					end
				end

				fireSignal(moduleApiTable[name], ...)
			end)

			table.insert(eventConnections, connection)
		end
	end

	-- The UI posts message text here; the backend sends it through
	-- the selected TextChannel instead of Players:Chat().
	local messagePosted = findInCollectionByKeyAndType(
		chatWindow,
		"MessagePosted",
		"BindableEvent"
	)

	if messagePosted then
		local connection = messagePosted.Event:Connect(function(message)
			local text = message

			if type(message) == "table" then
				text = message.Text
			end

			task.spawn(function()
				local success, err = moduleApiTable:SendMessage(text)

				if not success then
					warnChat("Could not send posted message: " .. tostring(err))
				end
			end)
		end)

		table.insert(eventConnections, connection)
	end

	setInitialChatTypes(chatWindow.ChatTypes or {})

	-- Register SetCore BindableEvents and flush queued requests.
	if type(setCoreCollection) == "table" then
		local setCoreNames = {
			"ChatMakeSystemMessage",
			"ChatWindowPosition",
			"ChatWindowSize",
			"ChatBarDisabled",
		}

		for _, name in ipairs(setCoreNames) do
			local event = findInCollectionByKeyAndType(
				setCoreCollection,
				name,
				"BindableEvent"
			)

			if event then
				communicationsConnections.SetCore[name] = event

				local queue = SetCoreCache[name]

				if queue then
					for _, data in ipairs(queue) do
						local success, err = pcall(function()
							event:Fire(data)
						end)

						if not success then
							warnChat(
								"Could not replay "
									.. name
									.. ": "
									.. tostring(err)
							)
						end
					end

					SetCoreCache[name] = nil
				end
			end
		end
	end

	-- Register GetCore BindableFunctions.
	if type(getCoreCollection) == "table" then
		for _, name in ipairs({
			"ChatWindowPosition",
			"ChatWindowSize",
			"ChatBarDisabled",
			}) do
			communicationsConnections.GetCore[name] =
				findInCollectionByKeyAndType(
					getCoreCollection,
					name,
					"BindableFunction"
				)
		end
	end

	-- Inform the frontend of current state.
	dispatchEvent("SetVisible", ChatWindowState.Visible)
	dispatchEvent("TopbarEnabledChanged", ChatWindowState.TopbarEnabled)
	dispatchEvent("CoreGuiEnabled", ChatWindowState.CoreGuiEnabled)

	log("Custom chat frontend registered.")
	return true
end

moduleApiTable.RegisterFrontend =
	moduleApiTable.RegisterCoreGuiConnections

-- ============================================================
-- NATIVE COREGUI STATE MONITORING
--
-- Read-only observation. This does not alter any CoreGui flags.
-- ============================================================

do
	local success, enabled = pcall(function()
		return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat)
	end)

	if success and type(enabled) == "boolean" then
		ChatWindowState.CoreGuiEnabled = enabled
	end

	local signalSuccess, changedSignal = pcall(function()
		return StarterGui.CoreGuiChangedSignal
	end)

	if signalSuccess and changedSignal then
		local connectSuccess, connection = pcall(function()
			return changedSignal:Connect(function(coreGuiType, enabledNow)
				if coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.All
					or coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat then

					ChatWindowState.CoreGuiEnabled = enabledNow == true

					dispatchEvent(
						"CoreGuiEnabled",
						ChatWindowState.CoreGuiEnabled
					)
				end
			end)
		end)

		if connectSuccess and connection then
			table.insert(runtimeConnections, connection)
		end
	end
end

-- Physical '/' is owned exclusively by the matching Topbar ContextActionService binding.
-- This module does not observe/re-dispatch the key because InputBegan cannot Sink it.

-- ============================================================
-- MODERN INCOMING MESSAGE EVENTS
-- ============================================================

do
	local success, connection = pcall(function()
		return TextChatService.MessageReceived:Connect(function(message)
			ChatWindowState.MessageCount += 1

			fireSignal(moduleApiTable.IncomingMessage, message)

			fireSignal(
				moduleApiTable.MessagesChanged,
				message,
				ChatWindowState.MessageCount
			)
		end)
	end)

	if success and connection then
		table.insert(runtimeConnections, connection)
	else
		warnChat(
			"Could not connect TextChatService.MessageReceived. "
				.. "Incoming messages will not reach this bridge."
		)
	end
end

-- Optional outgoing-message signal for custom renderers.
do
	local success, connection = pcall(function()
		return TextChatService.SendingMessage:Connect(function(message)
			fireSignal(moduleApiTable.OutgoingMessage, message)
		end)
	end)

	if success and connection then
		table.insert(runtimeConnections, connection)
	end
end

-- ============================================================
-- TEXT CHANNEL LIFECYCLE
-- ============================================================

local function onChannelsChanged()
	fireSignal(moduleApiTable.TextChannelsChanged, getTextChannels())
end

local function connectTextChannelsFolder(folder)
	if not folder or not folder:IsA("Folder") then
		return
	end

	table.insert(
		runtimeConnections,
		folder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child:IsA("TextChannel") then
				onChannelsChanged()
			end
		end)
	)

	table.insert(
		runtimeConnections,
		folder.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
			if child:IsA("TextChannel") then
				onChannelsChanged()
			end
		end)
	)
end

do
	connectTextChannelsFolder(getTextChannelsFolder())

	table.insert(
		runtimeConnections,
		TextChatService.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child:IsA("TextChannel") then
				onChannelsChanged()
			elseif child.Name == "TextChannels" and child:IsA("Folder") then
				connectTextChannelsFolder(child)
				onChannelsChanged()
			end
		end)
	)
end

-- ============================================================
-- CLEANUP
-- ============================================================

function moduleApiTable:Disconnect()
	disconnectAll(eventConnections)
	disconnectAll(runtimeConnections)

	communicationsConnections.ChatWindow = {}
	communicationsConnections.SetCore = {}
	communicationsConnections.GetCore = {}

	log("Disconnected chat bridge connections.")
end

			.. "requires TextChatService channels; it does not "
			.. "fall back to legacy Players:Chat()."
	)
end

log("Modern TextChatService bridge initialized.")

return moduleApiTable

local function LoadLibrary(name)
	local root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
	local folder = root and root:FindFirstChild("LoadLibrary")
	local moduleScript = folder and folder:FindFirstChild(name)

	if not moduleScript or not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		return nil
	end

	local ok, result = pcall(require, moduleScript)
	if ok then
		return result
	end

	warn("[PlayerlistModule] Could not load " .. tostring(name) .. ": " .. tostring(result))
	return nil
end

	task.wait(0.1)
end
assert(Players.LocalPlayer, "PlayerlistModule: LocalPlayer was not available within 20 seconds")

local function waitForChildBounded(parent, childName, timeout)
	local found = parent and parent:FindFirstChild(childName)
	if found then return found end
	if not parent then return nil end
	local ok, result = pcall(function() return parent:WaitForChild(childName, timeout or 10) end)
	if ok then return result end
	return nil
end

-- Follower cache
local FollowRelationships = {}
local FollowRequestInProgress = false
local LastFollowRequestTime = 0
local FOLLOW_REFRESH_INTERVAL = 15
local FOLLOWING_EXISTS_URL = "https://friends.roblox.com/v1/user/following-exists"

-- Friendship cache.
-- Friendship is queried through the built-in player API. No executor HTTP
-- bridge is used to determine whether players are friends or have requests.
local FriendStatusCache = {}
local FriendStatusRefreshInProgress = false
local LastFriendStatusRefresh = 0
local FRIEND_STATUS_REFRESH_INTERVAL = 30
local onFriendshipChanged

-- Helper functions
local function clamp(value, minimum, maximum)
	return math.max(minimum, math.min(maximum, value))
end

local function getFriendStatus(selectedPlayer)
	if not selectedPlayer or selectedPlayer == Player then
		return Enum.FriendStatus.NotFriend
	end

	local cachedStatus = FriendStatusCache[tostring(selectedPlayer.UserId)]
	if cachedStatus ~= nil then
		return cachedStatus
	end

	return Enum.FriendStatus.Unknown
end

-- The single source of friendship status: Roblox's built-in Player API.
local function readBuiltInFriendStatus(otherPlayer)
	if not otherPlayer or otherPlayer == Player then
		return Enum.FriendStatus.NotFriend
	end

	local success, status = pcall(function()
		return Player:GetFriendStatus(otherPlayer)
	end)

	if not success then
		return nil
	end

	if status == Enum.FriendStatus.Unknown
		or status == Enum.FriendStatus.NotFriend
		or status == Enum.FriendStatus.Friend
		or status == Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestSent
		or status == Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestReceived then
		return status
	end

	return nil
end

local function cacheFriendStatus(otherPlayer, status)
	if not otherPlayer or otherPlayer == Player or status == nil then
		return
	end

	local key = tostring(otherPlayer.UserId)
	local previousStatus = FriendStatusCache[key]

	FriendStatusCache[key] = status

	if previousStatus ~= status and onFriendshipChanged then
		onFriendshipChanged(otherPlayer, status)
	end
end

local function getFollowerStatus(selectedPlayer)
	if not IsFollowersEnabled or not selectedPlayer or selectedPlayer == Player then
		return nil
	end

	if selectedPlayer.UserId &lt;= 0 or Player.UserId &lt;= 0 then
		return nil
	end

	local relationship = FollowRelationships[tostring(selectedPlayer.UserId)]
	if not relationship then
		return nil
	end

	if relationship.IsFollowing and relationship.IsFollowed then
		return FOLLOWER_STATUS.MUTUAL
	elseif relationship.IsFollowed then
		return FOLLOWER_STATUS.FOLLOWER
	elseif relationship.IsFollowing then
		return FOLLOWER_STATUS.FOLLOWING
	end

	return nil
end

local function getFollowerStatusIcon(status)
	if status == FOLLOWER_STATUS.MUTUAL then
		return MUTUAL_FOLLOWING_ICON
	elseif status == FOLLOWER_STATUS.FOLLOWING then
		return FOLLOWING_ICON
	elseif status == FOLLOWER_STATUS.FOLLOWER then
		return FOLLOWER_ICON
	end

	return nil
end

local function getFriendStatusIcon(status)
	if status == Enum.FriendStatus.Unknown or status == Enum.FriendStatus.NotFriend then
		return nil
	elseif status == Enum.FriendStatus.Friend then
		return FRIEND_ICON
	elseif status == Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestSent then
		return FRIEND_REQUEST_ICON
	elseif status == Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestReceived then
		return FRIEND_RECEIVED_ICON
	end

	return nil
end

local function getAdminIcon(player)
	local userId = tostring(player.UserId)

	if ADMINS[userId] then
		return nil
	end

	local success, result = pcall(function()
		return player:IsInGroup(1200769)
	end)

	if not success then
		warn("[PlayerlistModule] getAdminIcon failed: " .. tostring(result))
		return nil
	end

	if result then
		return ADMIN_ICON
	end

	return nil
end

local function setAvatarIconAsync(player, iconImage)
	local useSubdomains = false

	pcall(function()
		useSubdomains = settings():GetFFlag("UseNewSubdomainsInCoreScripts")
	end)

	local thumbsUrl = BaseUrl
	if useSubdomains and AssetGameUrl then
		thumbsUrl = AssetGameUrl
	end

	local thumbnailLoader = nil

	pcall(function()
		thumbnailLoader = require(NewRobloxGui.Modules.ThumbnailLoader)
	end)

	local loaded = false

	if thumbnailLoader then
		pcall(function()
			local loader = thumbnailLoader:Create(
				iconImage,
				player.UserId,
				thumbnailLoader.Sizes.Small,
				thumbnailLoader.AssetType.Avatar,
				true
			)
			loaded = loader:LoadAsync(false, true, nil)
		end)
	end

	if not loaded then
		iconImage.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Shell/Icons/DefaultProfileIcon.png"
	end
end

local function getMembershipIcon(player)
	if isTenFootInterface then
		return ""
	end

	local isBlocked = false
	if blockingUtility then
		pcall(function() isBlocked = blockingUtility:IsPlayerBlockedByUserId(player.UserId) == true end)
	end
	if isBlocked then
		return BLOCKED_ICON
	end

	local userId = tostring(player.UserId)
	local membershipType = player.MembershipType

	if ADMINS[userId] then
		return ADMINS[userId]
	elseif player.UserId == game.CreatorId and game.CreatorType == Enum.CreatorType.User then
		return PLACE_OWNER_ICON
	elseif membershipType == Enum.MembershipType.None then
		return ""
	elseif membershipType == Enum.MembershipType.BuildersClub then
		return BC_ICON
	elseif membershipType == Enum.MembershipType.TurboBuildersClub then
		return TBC_ICON
	elseif membershipType == Enum.MembershipType.OutrageousBuildersClub then
		return OBC_ICON
	end

	return ""
end

local function isValidStat(object)
	return object:IsA("StringValue")
		or object:IsA("IntValue")
		or object:IsA("BoolValue")
		or object:IsA("NumberValue")
		or object:IsA("DoubleConstrainedValue")
		or object:IsA("IntConstrainedValue")
end

local function sortPlayerEntries(a, b)
	if a.PrimaryStat == b.PrimaryStat then
		return a.Player.Name:upper() &lt; b.Player.Name:upper()
	end

	if not a.PrimaryStat then
		return false
	end

	if not b.PrimaryStat then
		return true
	end

	return a.PrimaryStat &gt; b.PrimaryStat
end

local function sortLeaderStats(a, b)
	if a.IsPrimary ~= b.IsPrimary then
		return a.IsPrimary
	end

	if a.Priority == b.Priority then
		return a.AddId &lt; b.AddId
	end

	if typeof(a.Priority) == "Instance" then
		return a.Priority.Value &lt; b.Priority.Value
	end

	return a.Priority &lt; b.Priority
end

local function sortTeams(a, b)
	if a.TeamScore == b.TeamScore then
		return a.Id &lt; b.Id
	end

	if not a.TeamScore then
		return false
	end

	if not b.TeamScore then
		return true
	end

	return a.TeamScore &lt; b.TeamScore
end

	end

	frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	frame.ZIndex = isTenFootInterface and 2 or 1

	bg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	bg.Size = UDim2.new(0, NameEntrySizeX, 0, sizeYOffset)

end

		background.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		background.Image = CHARACTER_BACKGROUND_IMAGE

		background.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -33, 0.5, -33)
