	else
		icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
	end

	icon.Position = UDim2.new(0.01, xOffset, 0.5, -icon.Size.Y.Offset / 2)
	icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	icon.Image = image
	icon.BorderSizePixel = 0
	icon.Parent = parent

	return icon
end

local function getScoreValue(object)
	if object:IsA("DoubleConstrainedValue") or object:IsA("IntConstrainedValue") then
		return object.ConstrainedValue
	elseif object:IsA("BoolValue") then
		return object.Value and 1 or 0
	end

	return object.Value
end

local THIN_CHARS = "[^%[iIl%.,']"

local function strWidth(value)
	return string.len(value)
	- math.floor(string.len(string.gsub(value, THIN_CHARS, "")) / 2)
end

local function formatNumber(value)
	local _, _, minusSign, int, fraction = tostring(value):find("([-]?)(%d+)([.]?%d*)")
	if not int then
		return tostring(value)
	end

	int = int:reverse():gsub("%d%d%d", "%1,")
	return minusSign .. int:reverse():gsub("^,", "") .. fraction
end

local function formatStatString(value)
	value = tostring(value)
	local numberValue = tonumber(value)

	if numberValue then
		value = formatNumber(numberValue)
	end

	if strWidth(value) &lt;= MAX_STR_LEN then
		return value
	end

	return string.sub(value, 1, MAX_STR_LEN - 3) .. "..."
end

-- Resizing
local LastMaxScrollSize = 0

local function setScrollListSize()
	local teamSize = #TeamEntries * TeamEntrySizeY
	local playerSize = #PlayerEntries * PlayerEntrySizeY
	local spacing = #PlayerEntries * ENTRY_PAD + #TeamEntries * ENTRY_PAD
	local canvasSize = teamSize + playerSize + spacing

	if #TeamEntries &gt; 0 and NeutralTeam and IsShowingNeutralFrame then
		canvasSize = canvasSize + TeamEntrySizeY + ENTRY_PAD
	end

	ScrollList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, canvasSize)

	local newSize = math.min(canvasSize, Container.AbsoluteSize.Y)

	if ScrollList.Size.Y.Offset == LastMaxScrollSize then
		if isTenFootInterface then
			ScrollList.Size = UDim2.new(1, 20, 0, newSize)
		else
			ScrollList.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, newSize)
		end
	end

	LastMaxScrollSize = newSize
end

-- Entry positioning
local function setPlayerEntryPositions()
	local position = 0

	for i = 1, #PlayerEntries do
		local entry = PlayerEntries[i]

		if entry.Frame ~= MyPlayerEntryTopFrame then
			entry.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(
				0,
				isTenFootInterface and 10 or 0,
				0,
				position
			)
			position = position + PlayerEntrySizeY + TILE_SPACING
		end
	end
end

local function setTeamEntryPositions()
	local teams = {}

	for _, teamEntry in ipairs(TeamEntries) do
		teams[tostring(teamEntry.Team.TeamColor)] = {}
	end

	if NeutralTeam then
		teams.Neutral = {}
	end

	for _, entry in ipairs(PlayerEntries) do
		if entry.Frame ~= MyPlayerEntryTopFrame then
			local player = entry.Player

			if player.Neutral then
				teams.Neutral = teams.Neutral or {}
				table.insert(teams.Neutral, entry)
			elseif teams[tostring(player.TeamColor)] then
				table.insert(teams[tostring(player.TeamColor)], entry)
			else
				teams.Neutral = teams.Neutral or {}
				table.insert(teams.Neutral, entry)
			end
		end
	end

	local position = 0

	for _, teamEntry in ipairs(TeamEntries) do
		local team = teamEntry.Team
		teamEntry.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, isTenFootInterface and 10 or 0, 0, position)
		position = position + TeamEntrySizeY + TILE_SPACING

		for _, entry in ipairs(teams[tostring(team.TeamColor)] or {}) do
			entry.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, isTenFootInterface and 10 or 0, 0, position)
			position = position + PlayerEntrySizeY + TILE_SPACING
		end
	end

	if NeutralTeam then
		NeutralTeam.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, isTenFootInterface and 10 or 0, 0, position)
		position = position + TeamEntrySizeY + TILE_SPACING

		local neutralPlayers = teams.Neutral or {}
		IsShowingNeutralFrame = #neutralPlayers &gt; 0

		for _, entry in ipairs(neutralPlayers) do
			entry.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, isTenFootInterface and 10 or 0, 0, position)
			position = position + PlayerEntrySizeY + TILE_SPACING
		end
	end
end

local function setEntryPositions()
	table.sort(PlayerEntries, sortPlayerEntries)

	if #TeamEntries &gt; 0 then
		setTeamEntryPositions()
	else
		setPlayerEntryPositions()
	end
end

local function updateSocialIcon(newIcon, bgFrame)
	if not bgFrame then
		return
	end

	local socialIcon = bgFrame:FindFirstChild("SocialIcon")
	local nameFrame = bgFrame:FindFirstChild("PlayerName")
	local offset = 19

	if socialIcon then
		if newIcon then
			socialIcon.Image = newIcon
		else
			if nameFrame then
				local newSize = nameFrame.Size.X.Offset + socialIcon.Size.X.Offset + 2
				nameFrame.Size = UDim2.new(-0.01, newSize, 0.5, 0)
				nameFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.01, offset, 0.245, 0)
			end
			socialIcon:Destroy()
		end
	elseif newIcon then
		socialIcon = createImageIcon(newIcon, "SocialIcon", offset, bgFrame)
		offset = offset + socialIcon.Size.X.Offset + 2

		if nameFrame then
			local newSize = bgFrame.Size.X.Offset - offset
			nameFrame.Size = UDim2.new(-0.01, newSize, 0.5, 0)
			nameFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.01, offset, 0.245, 0)
		end
	end
end

-- Friendship icons take priority over follower icons.
local function updateAllFollowerIcons()
	for _, entry in ipairs(PlayerEntries) do
		local otherPlayer = entry.Player

		if otherPlayer ~= Player and otherPlayer.UserId &gt; 0 then
			local friendStatus = getFriendStatus(otherPlayer)
			local friendIcon = getFriendStatusIcon(friendStatus)
			local icon = friendIcon

			if not friendIcon and friendStatus ~= Enum.FriendStatus.Friend then
				icon = getFollowerStatusIcon(getFollowerStatus(otherPlayer))
			end

			local bg = entry.Frame:FindFirstChild("BGFrame")
			if bg then
				updateSocialIcon(icon, bg)
			end
		end
	end
end

-- Follower relationships are retrieved directly from Roblox. The cookie, if
-- configured by the user in their local executor environment, is sent only to
-- friends.roblox.com over HTTPS. No third-party bridge or storage is involved.
local ExecutorRequest = nil
local ExecutorRequestName = nil
local ExecutorCsrfToken = nil

local function findExecutorRequest()
	local scopes = {}
	local seen = {}
	local function add(scope)
		if type(scope) == "table" and not seen[scope] then
			seen[scope] = true
			table.insert(scopes, scope)
		end
	end
	if type(getgenv) == "function" then
		local ok, scope = pcall(getgenv)
		if ok then add(scope) end
	end
	if type(getfenv) == "function" then
		local ok, scope = pcall(getfenv, 0)
		if ok then add(scope) end
	end
	add(_G)

	local candidates = {
		{ "request", function(scope) return scope.request end },
		{ "http_request", function(scope) return scope.http_request end },
		{ "httprequest", function(scope) return scope.httprequest end },
		{ "http.request", function(scope) return type(scope.http) == "table" and scope.http.request end },
		{ "syn.request", function(scope) return type(scope.syn) == "table" and scope.syn.request end },
		{ "fluxus.request", function(scope) return type(scope.fluxus) == "table" and scope.fluxus.request end },
		{ "krnl.request", function(scope) return type(scope.krnl) == "table" and scope.krnl.request end },
	}
	for _, scope in ipairs(scopes) do
		for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
			local ok, callback = pcall(candidate[2], scope)
			if ok and type(callback) == "function" then
				return callback, candidate[1], scopes
			end
		end
	end
	return nil, nil, scopes
end

local executorScopes
ExecutorRequest, ExecutorRequestName, executorScopes = findExecutorRequest()

local function getExecutorCookieHeader()
	for _, scope in ipairs(executorScopes or {}) do
		local value = scope.YekuRobloxCookie or scope.ROBLOSECURITY or scope.RobloxCookie
		if type(value) == "string" and value ~= "" then
			value = value:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
			value = value:gsub("^Cookie:%s*", "")
			if value:match("^%.ROBLOSECURITY=") then return value end
			return ".ROBLOSECURITY=" .. value
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function getHeaderValue(headers, wanted)
	if type(headers) ~= "table" then return nil end
	wanted = string.lower(wanted)
	for name, value in pairs(headers) do
		if string.lower(tostring(name)) == wanted then return tostring(value) end
	end
	return nil
end

local function normalizeFollowingResponse(response)
	if type(response) == "string" then
		return { StatusCode = 200, Body = response, Headers = {}, Success = true }
	end
	if type(response) ~= "table" then
		error("executor request returned " .. typeof(response) .. " instead of an HTTP response")
	end
	local success = response.Success
	if success == nil then success = response.success end
	return {
		StatusCode = tonumber(response.StatusCode or response.statusCode or response.status_code or response.status),
		Body = response.Body or response.body or "",
		Headers = response.Headers or response.headers or {},
		Success = success,
	}
end

local function requestRobloxFollowingExists(targetIds)
	if type(ExecutorRequest) ~= "function" then
		return nil, "no compatible executor HTTP request API is available"
	end
	local cookie = getExecutorCookieHeader()
	if not cookie then
		return nil, "no local .ROBLOSECURITY cookie is configured in the executor environment"
	end

	local headers = {
		["Accept"] = "application/json",
		["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
		["Cookie"] = cookie,
	}
	if ExecutorCsrfToken then headers["X-CSRF-TOKEN"] = ExecutorCsrfToken end

	local options = {
		Url = FOLLOWING_EXISTS_URL,
		Method = "POST",
		Headers = headers,
		Body = HttpService:JSONEncode({ targetUserIds = targetIds }),
		Timeout = 20,
	}
	local ok, raw = pcall(ExecutorRequest, options)
	if not ok then return nil, tostring(ExecutorRequestName or "executor request") .. " failed: " .. tostring(raw) end
	local response = normalizeFollowingResponse(raw)
	local token = getHeaderValue(response.Headers, "x-csrf-token")
	if token and token ~= "" then ExecutorCsrfToken = token end

	if response.StatusCode == 403 and ExecutorCsrfToken then
		local retryHeaders = {}
		for key, value in pairs(headers) do retryHeaders[key] = value end
		retryHeaders["X-CSRF-TOKEN"] = ExecutorCsrfToken
		options.Headers = retryHeaders
		local retryOK, retryRaw = pcall(ExecutorRequest, options)
		if retryOK then
			response = normalizeFollowingResponse(retryRaw)
			local retryToken = getHeaderValue(response.Headers, "x-csrf-token")
			if retryToken and retryToken ~= "" then ExecutorCsrfToken = retryToken end
		else
			return nil, tostring(ExecutorRequestName or "executor request") .. " retry failed: " .. tostring(retryRaw)
		end
	end

	local status = tonumber(response.StatusCode) or 0
	if status &lt; 200 or status &gt;= 300 or response.Success == false then
		return nil, "Roblox following-exists returned HTTP " .. tostring(status)
	end
	local decodeOK, payload = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(response.Body or "") end)
	if not decodeOK or type(payload) ~= "table" or type(payload.followings) ~= "table" then
		return nil, "Roblox following-exists returned an invalid response"
	end
	return payload.followings, nil
end

local function refreshFollowRelationships(force)
	if not IsFollowersEnabled or FollowRequestInProgress then return end
	local now = os.clock()
	if not force and now - LastFollowRequestTime &lt; FOLLOW_REFRESH_INTERVAL then return end

	local targetIds = {}
	for _, otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if otherPlayer ~= Player and otherPlayer.UserId &gt; 0 then
			table.insert(targetIds, otherPlayer.UserId)
		end
	end
	if #targetIds == 0 then
		FollowRelationships = {}
		LastFollowRequestTime = now
		updateAllFollowerIcons()
		return
	end

	FollowRequestInProgress = true
	LastFollowRequestTime = now
	local callOK, rows, requestError = pcall(requestRobloxFollowingExists, targetIds)
	if not callOK then
		requestError = rows
		rows = nil
	end
	if not rows then
		FollowRequestInProgress = false
		-- Preserve the last valid cache. A temporary API/cookie failure must not
		-- make every player look unfollowed.
		warn("[PlayerlistModule] Roblox following lookup failed: " .. tostring(requestError))
		return
	end

	local relationships = {}
	for _, id in ipairs(targetIds) do
		relationships[tostring(id)] = { IsFollowing = false, IsFollowed = false }
	end
	for _, row in ipairs(rows) do
		local key = tostring(row.userId or row.UserId or "")
		local relationship = relationships[key]
		if relationship then
			relationship.IsFollowing = row.isFollowing == true
			relationship.IsFollowed = row.isFollowed == true
		end
	end

	FollowRelationships = relationships
	FollowRequestInProgress = false
	updateAllFollowerIcons()
end


local playerDropDownLoading = false
local playerDropDownLoadFailed = false
local onFollowerStatusChanged
local popupHidden
local blockStatusChanged

local function showSelectedPlayerPopup()
	local selectedFrame = LastSelectedFrame
	local selectedPlayer = LastSelectedPlayer
	if not playerDropDown or not selectedFrame or not selectedPlayer then return end
	if selectedFrame.Parent == nil or selectedPlayer.Parent ~= Players then
		if popupHidden then popupHidden() end
		return
	end

	local ok, popup = pcall(function()
		return playerDropDown:CreatePopup(selectedPlayer)
	end)
	if not ok or not popup or typeof(popup) ~= "Instance" then
		warn("[PlayerlistModule] Could not create player dropdown: " .. tostring(popup))
		if ScrollList then ScrollList.ScrollingEnabled = true end
		return
	end
	if LastSelectedFrame ~= selectedFrame or LastSelectedPlayer ~= selectedPlayer then
		pcall(function() popup:Destroy() end)
		return
	end

	popup.Parent = PopupClipFrame
	local rowY = selectedFrame.Position.Y.Offset - ScrollList.CanvasPosition.Y
	local clipHeight = PopupClipFrame.AbsoluteSize.Y
	if clipHeight &lt;= 0 then clipHeight = Container.AbsoluteSize.Y end
	local popupHeight = popup.AbsoluteSize.Y
	if popupHeight &lt;= 0 then popupHeight = popup.Size.Y.Offset end
	local popupY = math.clamp(rowY, 0, math.max(0, clipHeight - popupHeight))
	popup.Position = UDim2.new(1, 1, 0, popupY)
	popup:TweenPosition(
		UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, popupY),
		Enum.EasingDirection.InOut,
		Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
		TWEEN_TIME,
		true
	)
end

local function ensurePlayerDropDownAsync()
	if playerDropDown then return true end
	if playerDropDownLoading or playerDropDownLoadFailed then return false end
	playerDropDownLoading = true
	task.spawn(function()
		local dropdownScript = Modules:FindFirstChild("PlayerDropDown")
		if not dropdownScript then
			dropdownScript = Modules:WaitForChild("PlayerDropDown", 15)
		end
		if not dropdownScript or not dropdownScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
			playerDropDownLoading = false
			playerDropDownLoadFailed = true
			warn("[PlayerlistModule] PlayerDropDown was not available after the deferred lookup.")
			if LastSelectedFrame and popupHidden then popupHidden() end
			return
		end

		local requireOK, loadedModule = pcall(require, dropdownScript)
		if not requireOK or type(loadedModule) ~= "table" then
			playerDropDownLoading = false
			playerDropDownLoadFailed = true
			warn("[PlayerlistModule] Deferred PlayerDropDown require failed: " .. tostring(loadedModule))
			if LastSelectedFrame and popupHidden then popupHidden() end
			return
		end

		playerDropDownModule = loadedModule
		local utilityOK, utility = pcall(function()
			return loadedModule:CreateBlockingUtility()
		end)
		if utilityOK then blockingUtility = utility end
		local dropdownOK, dropdown = pcall(function()
			return loadedModule:CreatePlayerDropDown()
		end)
		if not dropdownOK or not dropdown then
			playerDropDownLoading = false
			playerDropDownLoadFailed = true
			warn("[PlayerlistModule] Deferred dropdown creation failed: " .. tostring(dropdown))
			if LastSelectedFrame and popupHidden then popupHidden() end
			return
		end

		playerDropDown = dropdown
		playerDropDownLoading = false
		if not isTenFootInterface and onFollowerStatusChanged then
			pcall(function() playerDropDownModule.FollowerStatusChanged:Connect(onFollowerStatusChanged) end)
		end
		if popupHidden then
			pcall(function() playerDropDown.HiddenSignal:Connect(popupHidden) end)
		end
		if blockingUtility and blockStatusChanged then
			pcall(function() blockingUtility:GetBlockedStatusChangedEvent():Connect(blockStatusChanged) end)
		end
		-- Refresh membership marks once block-list state is available. The
		-- initial roster never waits for PlayerDropDown or the block-list API.
		for _, entry in ipairs(PlayerEntries) do
			local bg = entry.Frame and entry.Frame:FindFirstChild("BGFrame")
			local icon = bg and bg:FindFirstChild("MembershipIcon")
			if icon then pcall(function() icon.Image = getMembershipIcon(entry.Player) end) end
		end
		showSelectedPlayerPopup()
	end)
	return false
end

onFollowerStatusChanged = function()
	if not IsFollowersEnabled then return end
	-- Refresh immediately after Follow/Unfollow, then once per second for five seconds.
	task.spawn(function()
		for attempt = 1, 5 do
			refreshFollowRelationships(true)
			if attempt &lt; 5 then task.wait(1) end
		end
	end)
	if not LastSelectedFrame or not LastSelectedPlayer then return end

	local friendStatus = getFriendStatus(LastSelectedPlayer)
	local friendIcon = getFriendStatusIcon(friendStatus)
	if friendStatus == Enum.FriendStatus.Friend or friendIcon then
		local bg = LastSelectedFrame:FindFirstChild("BGFrame")
		if bg then
			updateSocialIcon(friendIcon, bg)
		end
		return
	end

	local bg = LastSelectedFrame:FindFirstChild("BGFrame")
	local icon = getFollowerStatusIcon(getFollowerStatus(LastSelectedPlayer))
	if bg then
		updateSocialIcon(icon, bg)
	end
end

popupHidden = function()
	if LastSelectedFrame then
		for _, child in ipairs(LastSelectedFrame:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("TextButton") or child:IsA("Frame") then
				child.BackgroundColor3 = BG_COLOR
			end
		end
	end

	ScrollList.ScrollingEnabled = true
	LastSelectedFrame = nil
	LastSelectedPlayer = nil
end

local function onEntryFrameSelected(selectedFrame, selectedPlayer)
	if selectedPlayer == Player or selectedPlayer.UserId &lt;= 1 or Player.UserId &lt;= 1 then
		return
	end

	if LastSelectedFrame == selectedFrame then
		if playerDropDown then pcall(function() playerDropDown:Hide() end) end
		LastSelectedFrame = nil
		LastSelectedPlayer = nil
		if ScrollList then ScrollList.ScrollingEnabled = true end
		return
	end

	if LastSelectedFrame then
		for _, child in ipairs(LastSelectedFrame:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("TextButton") or child:IsA("Frame") then
				child.BackgroundColor3 = BG_COLOR
			end
		end
	end

	LastSelectedFrame = selectedFrame
	LastSelectedPlayer = selectedPlayer

	for _, child in ipairs(selectedFrame:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("TextButton") or child:IsA("Frame") then
			child.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 1, 1)
		end
	end

	ScrollList.ScrollingEnabled = false

	if not playerDropDown then
		ensurePlayerDropDownAsync()
		return
	end
	showSelectedPlayerPopup()
end

onFriendshipChanged = function(otherPlayer, newFriendStatus)
	local entryToUpdate = nil

	for _, entry in ipairs(PlayerEntries) do
		if entry.Player == otherPlayer then
			entryToUpdate = entry
			break
		end
	end

	if not entryToUpdate then
		return
	end

	local icon = getFriendStatusIcon(newFriendStatus)
	local bg = entryToUpdate.Frame:FindFirstChild("BGFrame")

	if bg then
		if not icon and newFriendStatus ~= Enum.FriendStatus.Friend
			and not IsServerFollowers and IsFollowersEnabled then
			icon = getFollowerStatusIcon(getFollowerStatus(otherPlayer))
		end

		updateSocialIcon(icon, bg)
	end

	if LastSelectedPlayer == otherPlayer then
		onFollowerStatusChanged()
	end
end

-- Immediate native friendship-status updates, where the event is available.
-- Some legacy client versions do not expose this event, so it is guarded.
pcall(function()
	Player.FriendStatusChanged:Connect(function(otherPlayer, newFriendStatus)
		cacheFriendStatus(otherPlayer, newFriendStatus)
	end)
end)

-- Refresh friendship state through Player:GetFriendStatus().
-- This function performs no HTTP requests and does not require an executor
-- HTTP bridge.
local function refreshFriendStatuses(force)
	if FriendStatusRefreshInProgress then
		return
	end

	local now = os.clock()
	if not force and now - LastFriendStatusRefresh &lt; FRIEND_STATUS_REFRESH_INTERVAL then
		return
	end

	local targets = {}

	for _, otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if otherPlayer ~= Player and otherPlayer.UserId &gt; 0 then
			table.insert(targets, otherPlayer)
		end
	end

	LastFriendStatusRefresh = now

	if #targets == 0 then
		return
	end

	FriendStatusRefreshInProgress = true

	task.spawn(function()
		for _, otherPlayer in ipairs(targets) do
			if otherPlayer.Parent == Players then
				local status = readBuiltInFriendStatus(otherPlayer)
				if status ~= nil then
					cacheFriendStatus(otherPlayer, status)
				end
			end
		end

		FriendStatusRefreshInProgress = false
	end)
end

local function setFollowRelationshipsView(relationshipTable)
	if not relationshipTable then
		return
	end

	for _, entry in ipairs(PlayerEntries) do
		local otherPlayer = entry.Player
		local key = tostring(otherPlayer.UserId)
		local friendStatus = getFriendStatus(otherPlayer)
		local icon = getFriendStatusIcon(friendStatus)

		if not icon and friendStatus ~= Enum.FriendStatus.Friend then
			local relationship = relationshipTable[key]
			if relationship then
				if relationship.IsMutual == true then
					icon = MUTUAL_FOLLOWING_ICON
				elseif relationship.IsFollowing == true then
					icon = FOLLOWING_ICON
				elseif relationship.IsFollower == true then
					icon = FOLLOWER_ICON
				end
			end
		end

		local bg = entry.Frame:FindFirstChild("BGFrame")
		if bg then
			updateSocialIcon(icon, bg)
		end
	end
end

local function getFollowRelationships()
	if RemoteFunc_GetFollowRelationships then
		local success, result = pcall(function()
			return RemoteFunc_GetFollowRelationships:InvokeServer()
		end)

		if success then
			return result
		end
	end

	return nil
end

-- Team scores
local function updateAllTeamScores()
	local teamScores = {}

	for _, entry in ipairs(PlayerEntries) do
		local player = entry.Player
		local leaderstats = player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
		local team = player.Neutral and "Neutral" or tostring(player.TeamColor)
		local isInvalidColor = true

		if team ~= "Neutral" then
			for _, teamEntry in ipairs(TeamEntries) do
				if team == tostring(teamEntry.Team.TeamColor) then
					isInvalidColor = false
					break
				end
			end
		end

		if isInvalidColor then
			team = "Neutral"
		end

		teamScores[team] = teamScores[team] or {}

		if entry.Frame ~= MyPlayerEntryTopFrame and leaderstats then
			for _, stat in ipairs(GameStats) do
				local object = leaderstats:FindFirstChild(stat.Name)

				if object and not object:IsA("StringValue") then
					teamScores[team][stat.Name] = (teamScores[team][stat.Name] or 0)
						+ getScoreValue(object)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function updateTeamFrame(frame, stats, visible)
		if not frame then
			return
		end

		if visible ~= nil then
			frame.Visible = visible
		end

		for _, stat in ipairs(GameStats) do
			local statFrame = frame:FindFirstChild(stat.Name)
			local statText = statFrame and statFrame:FindFirstChild("StatText")

			if statText then
				statText.Text = stats and stats[stat.Name] ~= nil
					and formatStatString(tostring(stats[stat.Name]))
					or ""
			end
		end
	end

	for _, teamEntry in ipairs(TeamEntries) do
		local team = teamEntry.Team
		local stats = teamScores[tostring(team.TeamColor)]
		updateTeamFrame(teamEntry.Frame, stats, nil)
	end

	if NeutralTeam then
		local stats = teamScores.Neutral
		updateTeamFrame(NeutralTeam.Frame, stats, stats ~= nil)
	end
end

local function updateTeamEntry(entry)
	local frame = entry.Frame
	local team = entry.Team
	local offset = NameEntrySizeX

	for _, stat in ipairs(GameStats) do
		local statFrame = frame:FindFirstChild(stat.Name)

		if not statFrame then
			statFrame = createStatFrame(offset, frame, stat.Name)
			statFrame.BackgroundColor3 = team.TeamColor.Color
			createStatText(statFrame, "", false, true)
		end

		statFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, offset + TILE_SPACING, 0, 0)
		offset = offset + statFrame.Size.X.Offset + TILE_SPACING
	end
end

local function updatePrimaryStats(statName)
	for _, entry in ipairs(PlayerEntries) do
		local leaderstats = entry.Player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
		entry.PrimaryStat = nil

		if leaderstats then
			local object = leaderstats:FindFirstChild(statName)
			if object then
				entry.PrimaryStat = getScoreValue(object)
			end
		end
	end
end

local updateLeaderstatFrames

local function initializeStatText(stat, statObject, entry, statFrame, index, isTopStat)
	local player = entry.Player
	local value = getScoreValue(statObject)

	if GameStats[1] and statObject.Name == GameStats[1].Name then
		entry.PrimaryStat = value
	end

	local statText = createStatText(statFrame, formatStatString(tostring(value)), isTopStat)

	if player == Player then
		stat.Text = statText.Text
	end

	statObject.Changed:Connect(function()
		local newValue = getScoreValue(statObject)
		statText.Text = formatStatString(tostring(newValue))

		if GameStats[1] and statObject.Name == GameStats[1].Name then
			entry.PrimaryStat = newValue
		end

		if player == Player then
			stat.Text = statText.Text
			Playerlist.OnStatChanged:Fire(stat.Name, stat.Text)
		end

		updateAllTeamScores()
		setEntryPositions()
	end)

	statObject.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == "IsPrimary" then
			if GameStats[1] then
				GameStats[1].IsPrimary = false
			end

			stat.IsPrimary = true
			updatePrimaryStats(stat.Name)

			if updateLeaderstatFrames then
				updateLeaderstatFrames()
			end

			Playerlist.OnLeaderstatsChanged:Fire(GameStats)
		end
	end)
end

updateLeaderstatFrames = function()
	table.sort(GameStats, sortLeaderStats)

	if #TeamEntries &gt; 0 then
		for _, entry in ipairs(TeamEntries) do
			updateTeamEntry(entry)
		end

		if NeutralTeam then
			updateTeamEntry(NeutralTeam)
		end
	end

	for _, entry in ipairs(PlayerEntries) do
		local player = entry.Player
		local mainFrame = entry.Frame
		local offset = NameEntrySizeX
		local leaderstats = player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
		local isTopStat = entry.Frame == MyPlayerEntryTopFrame

		for _, stat in ipairs(GameStats) do
			local statObject = leaderstats and leaderstats:FindFirstChild(stat.Name)
			local statFrame = mainFrame:FindFirstChild(stat.Name)

			if not statFrame then
				statFrame = createStatFrame(offset, mainFrame, stat.Name, isTopStat)
			end

			if statObject and not statFrame:FindFirstChild("StatText") then
				initializeStatText(stat, statObject, entry, statFrame, nil, isTopStat)
			end

			statFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, offset + TILE_SPACING, 0, 0)
			offset = offset + statFrame.Size.X.Offset + TILE_SPACING
		end

		if entry.Frame ~= MyPlayerEntryTopFrame then
			if isTenFootInterface then
				Container.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -offset / 2, 0, 110)
				Container.Size = UDim2.new(0, offset, 0.8, 0)
			else
				Container.Position = UDim2.new(1, -offset, 0, 2)
				Container.Size = UDim2.new(0, offset, 0.5, 0)
			end

			MinContainerSize = UDim2.new(0, offset, 0.5, 0)
		end
	end

	updateAllTeamScores()
	setEntryPositions()
	Playerlist.OnLeaderstatsChanged:Fire(GameStats)
end

local function addNewStats(leaderstats)
	for _, stat in ipairs(leaderstats:GetChildren()) do
		if isValidStat(stat) and #GameStats &lt; MAX_LEADERSTATS then
			local exists = false

			for _, existing in ipairs(GameStats) do
				if existing.Name == stat.Name then
					exists = true
					break
				end
			end

			if not exists then
				local newStat = {
					Name = stat.Name,
					Text = "-",
					Priority = 0,
					IsPrimary = false,
					AddId = StatAddId,
				}

				StatAddId = StatAddId + 1

				local priority = stat:FindFirstChild("Priority")
				if priority then
					newStat.Priority = priority
				end

				local primary = stat:FindFirstChild("IsPrimary")
				if primary then
					newStat.IsPrimary = true
				end

				table.insert(GameStats, newStat)
				table.sort(GameStats, sortLeaderStats)
			end
		end
	end
end

local function removeStatFrameFromEntry(stat, frame)
	local statFrame = frame:FindFirstChild(stat.Name)
	if statFrame then
		statFrame:Destroy()
	end
end

local function doesStatExist(stat)
	for _, entry in ipairs(PlayerEntries) do
		local player = entry.Player
		local leaderstats = player and player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")

		if leaderstats and leaderstats:FindFirstChild(stat.Name) then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

local function onStatRemoved(oldStat, entry)
	if not isValidStat(oldStat) then
		return
	end

	removeStatFrameFromEntry(oldStat, entry.Frame)

	local stillExists = doesStatExist(oldStat)
	local removeIndex = nil

	for i, stat in ipairs(GameStats) do
		if stat.Name == oldStat.Name then
			removeIndex = i
			break
		end
	end

	if stillExists then
		if removeIndex and entry.Player == Player then
			GameStats[removeIndex].Text = "-"
			Playerlist.OnStatChanged:Fire(GameStats[removeIndex].Name, "-")
		end
	else
		for _, playerEntry in ipairs(PlayerEntries) do
			removeStatFrameFromEntry(oldStat, playerEntry.Frame)
		end

		for _, teamEntry in ipairs(TeamEntries) do
			removeStatFrameFromEntry(oldStat, teamEntry.Frame)
		end

		if removeIndex then
			table.remove(GameStats, removeIndex)
		end
	end

	if GameStats[1] then
		updatePrimaryStats(GameStats[1].Name)
	end

	updateLeaderstatFrames()
end

local function onStatAdded(leaderstats, entry)
	leaderstats.ChildAdded:Connect(function(newStat)
		if isValidStat(newStat) then
			addNewStats(newStat.Parent)
			updateLeaderstatFrames()
		end
	end)

	leaderstats.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
		onStatRemoved(child, entry)
	end)

	addNewStats(leaderstats)
	updateLeaderstatFrames()
end

local function setLeaderStats(entry)
	local player = entry.Player
	local leaderstats = player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")

	if leaderstats then
		onStatAdded(leaderstats, entry)
	end

	player.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == "leaderstats" then
			onStatAdded(child, entry)
		end
	end)

	player.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == "leaderstats" then
			for _, stat in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do
				onStatRemoved(stat, entry)
			end

			updateLeaderstatFrames()
		end
	end)
end

-- Player entries
local offsetSize = isTenFootInterface and 32 or 18

local function createPlayerEntry(player, isTopStat)
	local entry = {}
	local containerFrame, bgFrame = createEntryFrame(player.Name, PlayerEntrySizeY, isTopStat)

	bgFrame.Active = true

	if not isTenFootInterface then
		bgFrame.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			onEntryFrameSelected(containerFrame, player)
		end)
	end

	local currentXOffset = 1
	local membershipIconImage = getMembershipIcon(player)
	local membershipIcon = nil

	if membershipIconImage then
		membershipIcon = createImageIcon(membershipIconImage, "MembershipIcon", currentXOffset, bgFrame)
		currentXOffset = currentXOffset + membershipIcon.Size.X.Offset + 2
	else
		currentXOffset = currentXOffset + offsetSize
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		if isTenFootInterface and membershipIcon then
			setAvatarIconAsync(player, membershipIcon)
		end
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		local success, isOwner = pcall(function()
			return player:GetRankInGroup(game.CreatorId) == 255
		end)

		if success and game.CreatorType == Enum.CreatorType.Group and isOwner then
			membershipIconImage = PLACE_OWNER_ICON

			if not membershipIcon then
				membershipIcon = createImageIcon(membershipIconImage, "MembershipIcon", 1, bgFrame)
			else
				membershipIcon.Image = membershipIconImage
			end
		end

		local adminIcon = getAdminIcon(player)

		if adminIcon then
			if not membershipIcon then
				membershipIcon = createImageIcon(adminIcon, "MembershipIcon", 1, bgFrame)
			else
				membershipIcon.Image = adminIcon
			end
		end
	end)

	local playerName = createEntryNameText(
		"PlayerName",
		player.Name,
		bgFrame.Size.X.Offset - currentXOffset,
		currentXOffset
	)

	playerName.Parent = bgFrame
	entry.Player = player
	entry.Frame = containerFrame

	if isTenFootInterface then
		local shadow = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		shadow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		shadow.Name = "Shadow"
		shadow.Image = SHADOW_IMAGE
		shadow.Position = UDim2.new(0, -SHADOW_SLICE_SIZE, 0, 0)
		shadow.Size = UDim2.new(1, SHADOW_SLICE_SIZE * 2, 1, SHADOW_SLICE_SIZE)
		shadow.ScaleType = "Slice"
		shadow.SliceCenter = SHADOW_SLICE_RECT
		shadow.Parent = bgFrame
	end

	if isTopStat then
		playerName.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	end

	return entry
end

local function createTeamEntry(team)
	local entry = {
		Team = team,
		TeamScore = 0,
	}

	local containerFrame, bgFrame = createEntryFrame(team.Name, TeamEntrySizeY)
	bgFrame.BackgroundColor3 = team.TeamColor.Color

	local teamName = createEntryNameText("TeamName", team.Name, bgFrame.AbsoluteSize.X, 1)
	teamName.Parent = bgFrame

	entry.Frame = containerFrame

	if isTenFootInterface then
		local shadow = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		shadow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		shadow.Name = "Shadow"
		shadow.Image = SHADOW_IMAGE
		shadow.Position = UDim2.new(0, -SHADOW_SLICE_SIZE, 0, 0)
		shadow.Size = UDim2.new(1, SHADOW_SLICE_SIZE * 2, 1, SHADOW_SLICE_SIZE)
		shadow.ScaleType = "Slice"
		shadow.SliceCenter = SHADOW_SLICE_RECT
		shadow.Parent = bgFrame
	end

	team.Changed:Connect(function(property)
		if property == "Name" then
			teamName.Text = team.Name
		elseif property == "TeamColor" then
			for _, child in ipairs(containerFrame:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("GuiObject") then
					child.BackgroundColor3 = team.TeamColor.Color
				end
			end

			setTeamEntryPositions()
			updateAllTeamScores()
			setEntryPositions()
			setScrollListSize()
		end
	end)

	return entry
end

local function createNeutralTeam()
	if NeutralTeam then
		return
	end

	local team = Instance.new("Team")
	team.Name = "Neutral"
	team.TeamColor = BrickColor.new("White")

	NeutralTeam = createTeamEntry(team)
	NeutralTeam.Frame.Parent = ScrollList
end

local function setupEntry(player, entry, isTopStat)
	setLeaderStats(entry)

	if isTopStat then
		entry.Frame.Parent = Container
	else
		entry.Frame.Parent = ScrollList
	end

	table.insert(PlayerEntries, entry)

	if not isTopStat then
		setScrollListSize()
	end

	updateLeaderstatFrames()

	player.Changed:Connect(function(property)
		if #TeamEntries &gt; 0 and (property == "Neutral" or property == "TeamColor") then
			setTeamEntryPositions()
			updateAllTeamScores()
			setEntryPositions()
			setScrollListSize()
		end
	end)
end

local function insertPlayerEntry(player)
	setupEntry(player, createPlayerEntry(player), false)

	if player == Player and isTenFootInterface then
		local localEntry = createPlayerEntry(player, true)
		MyPlayerEntryTopFrame = localEntry.Frame
		MyPlayerEntryTopFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		MyPlayerEntryTopFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
		setupEntry(player, localEntry, true)
	end

	-- Populate the native friendship-status cache as each player joins.
	if player ~= Player then
		task.defer(function()
			if player.Parent == Players then
				local status = readBuiltInFriendStatus(player)
				if status ~= nil then
					cacheFriendStatus(player, status)
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

local function removePlayerEntry(player)
	for i = #PlayerEntries, 1, -1 do
		if PlayerEntries[i].Player == player then
			PlayerEntries[i].Frame:Destroy()
			table.remove(PlayerEntries, i)
		end
	end

	setEntryPositions()
	setScrollListSize()
end

-- Teams
local function onTeamAdded(team)
	for i = #TeamEntries, 1, -1 do
		if TeamEntries[i].Team.TeamColor == team.TeamColor then
			TeamEntries[i].Frame:Destroy()
			table.remove(TeamEntries, i)
			break
		end
	end

	local entry = createTeamEntry(team)
	entry.Id = TeamAddId
	TeamAddId = TeamAddId + 1

	if not NeutralTeam then
		createNeutralTeam()
	end

	table.insert(TeamEntries, entry)
	table.sort(TeamEntries, sortTeams)

	setTeamEntryPositions()
	updateLeaderstatFrames()
	setScrollListSize()
	entry.Frame.Parent = ScrollList
end

local function onTeamRemoved(removedTeam)
	for i = #TeamEntries, 1, -1 do
		if TeamEntries[i].Team.Name == removedTeam.Name then
			TeamEntries[i].Frame:Destroy()
			table.remove(TeamEntries, i)
			break
		end
	end

	if #TeamEntries == 0 and NeutralTeam then
		NeutralTeam.Frame:Destroy()
		NeutralTeam.Team:Destroy()
		NeutralTeam = nil
		IsShowingNeutralFrame = false
	end

	setEntryPositions()
	updateLeaderstatFrames()
	setScrollListSize()
end

local function initializeTeams(teams)
	for _, team in ipairs(teams:GetTeams()) do
		onTeamAdded(team)
	end

	teams.ChildAdded:Connect(function(team)
		if team:IsA("Team") then
			onTeamAdded(team)
		end
	end)

	teams.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(team)
		if team:IsA("Team") then
			onTeamRemoved(team)
		end
	end)
end

if TeamsService then
	initializeTeams(TeamsService)
end

game.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
	if child:IsA("Teams") then
		initializeTeams(child)
	end
end)

-- Add/remove players
Players.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
	if child:IsA("Player") then
		insertPlayerEntry(child)

		task.spawn(function()
			-- Respect refresh throttles; do not repeat full roster/network refreshes
			-- once per PlayerAdded event (which becomes quadratic during joins).
			refreshFriendStatuses(false)
			refreshFollowRelationships(false)
		end)
	end
end)

for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
	insertPlayerEntry(player)
end

-- Prewarm PlayerDropDown independently after the first roster rows exist.
-- The actual require/CreatePlayerDropDown work runs inside task.spawn, so it
-- cannot hold PlayerlistModule's return value or Topbar's menu/backpack startup.
-- This also means the first player-row click normally finds the popup ready.
task.delay(0.15, function()
	if not playerDropDown and not playerDropDownLoading and not playerDropDownLoadFailed then
		ensurePlayerDropDownAsync()
	end
end)

Players.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
	if child:IsA("Player") then
		FollowRelationships[tostring(child.UserId)] = nil
		FriendStatusCache[tostring(child.UserId)] = nil

		if LastSelectedPlayer == child then
			if playerDropDown then pcall(function() playerDropDown:Hide() end) end
			if popupHidden then popupHidden() end
		end

		removePlayerEntry(child)
	end
end)

-- New server followers
if IsServerFollowers and not isTenFootInterface then
	task.spawn(function()
		local storage = game:GetService("RobloxReplicatedStorage")

		RemoveEvent_OnFollowRelationshipChanged = storage:FindFirstChild("FollowRelationshipChanged")
			or waitForChildBounded(storage, "FollowRelationshipChanged", 8)
		RemoteFunc_GetFollowRelationships = storage:FindFirstChild("GetFollowRelationships")
			or waitForChildBounded(storage, "GetFollowRelationships", 8)

		if RemoveEvent_OnFollowRelationshipChanged and RemoveEvent_OnFollowRelationshipChanged:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
			RemoveEvent_OnFollowRelationshipChanged.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(result)
				setFollowRelationshipsView(result)
			end)
		else
			warn("[PlayerlistModule] Optional FollowRelationshipChanged RemoteEvent is unavailable; follower UI will remain empty.")
		end

		if RemoteFunc_GetFollowRelationships and RemoteFunc_GetFollowRelationships:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
			setFollowRelationshipsView(getFollowRelationships())
		end
	end)
end

-- Resize and canvas clamping
local function clampCanvasPosition()
	local maximum = ScrollList.CanvasSize.Y.Offset - ScrollList.Size.Y.Offset

	if maximum &gt;= 0 and ScrollList.CanvasPosition.Y &gt; maximum then
		ScrollList.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, maximum)
	end
end

local function resizePlayerList()
	setScrollListSize()
	clampCanvasPosition()
end

NewRobloxGui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
	task.spawn(resizePlayerList)
end)

-- Close player dropdown when clicking elsewhere.
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
	local inputType = input.UserInputType

	if inputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or inputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
		if LastSelectedFrame and playerDropDown then
			playerDropDown:Hide()
		end
	end
end)

-- Block status
blockStatusChanged = function(userId, isBlocked)
	if userId &lt; 0 then
		return
	end

	for _, entry in ipairs(PlayerEntries) do
		if entry.Player.UserId == userId then
			local bg = entry.Frame:FindFirstChild("BGFrame")
			local membershipIcon = bg and bg:FindFirstChild("MembershipIcon")

			if membershipIcon then
				membershipIcon.Image = getMembershipIcon(entry.Player)
			end

			return
		end
	end
end

-- Connected lazily when PlayerDropDown is first needed.

-- Native friendship refresh. No ExecutorHttpBridge is involved.
task.spawn(function()
	refreshFriendStatuses(true)

	while task.wait(FRIEND_STATUS_REFRESH_INTERVAL) do
		refreshFriendStatuses(true)
	end
end)

-- Follower refresh remains separate.
if IsFollowersEnabled then
	task.spawn(function()
		refreshFollowRelationships(true)

		while task.wait(FOLLOW_REFRESH_INTERVAL) do
			refreshFollowRelationships(true)
		end
	end)
end

--[[
    VISIBILITY CONTROL

    Tab is handled by UserInputService.InputBegan, not by a Tab
    ContextActionService binding that another CoreScript may override.
]]

local isOpen = not isTenFootInterface
local topStat = nil

local coreGuiAllEnabled = true
local playerListSettingEnabled = true
local lastObservedPlayerListEnabled = nil
local lastEffectivePlayerListEnabled = nil

local closeListFunc

local function isGamepadInput(inputType)
	return inputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1
		or inputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad2
		or inputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad3
		or inputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad4
end

local function readPlayerListEnabled()
	local success, enabled = pcall(function()
		return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.PlayerList)
	end)

	if success and type(enabled) == "boolean" then
		return enabled
	end

	return nil
end

local function readAllCoreGuiEnabled()
	local success, enabled = pcall(function()
		return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.All)
	end)

	if success and type(enabled) == "boolean" then
		return enabled
	end

	return nil
end

local function setVisible(requestedVisible, fromTemp)
	local shouldShow =
		requestedVisible == true
		and playerlistCoreGuiEnabled
		and not IsSmallScreenDevice
		and next(TempHideKeys) == nil

	Container.Visible = shouldShow

	if not shouldShow then
		ContextActionService:UnbindAction("CloseList")
		ContextActionService:UnbindAction("StopAction")

		if GuiService.SelectedObject
			and GuiService.SelectedObject:IsDescendantOf(Container) then
			GuiService.SelectedObject = nil
		end

		pcall(function()
			UserInputService.OverrideMouseIconBehavior =
				Enum.OverrideMouseIconBehavior.None
		end)

		return
	end

	if fromTemp then
		return
	end

	if not isGamepadInput(UserInputService:GetLastInputType()) then
		return
	end

	local selectedButton = nil

	for _, descendant in ipairs(ScrollList:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendant:IsA("TextButton") then
			selectedButton = descendant
			break
		end
	end

	if selectedButton then
		GuiService.SelectedObject = selectedButton
	elseif isTenFootInterface then
		GuiService.SelectedObject = ScrollList
	end

	pcall(function()
		UserInputService.OverrideMouseIconBehavior =
			Enum.OverrideMouseIconBehavior.ForceHide
	end)

	ContextActionService:BindAction(
		"StopAction",
		function()
			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
		end,
		false,
		Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1
	)

	ContextActionService:BindAction(
		"CloseList",
		closeListFunc,
		false,
		Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB,
		Enum.KeyCode.ButtonStart
	)
end

closeListFunc = function(actionName, inputState)
	if inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
	end

	isOpen = false
	setVisible(false)

	pcall(function()
		GuiService:SetMenuIsOpen(false)
	end)

	return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
end

-- Shared public API.
Playerlist.Open = function()
	isOpen = true
	setVisible(true)
end

Playerlist.Close = function()
	isOpen = false
	setVisible(false)
end

Playerlist.SetVisible = function(visible)
	if type(visible) ~= "boolean" then
		return
	end

	isOpen = visible
	setVisible(visible)
end

Playerlist.ToggleVisibility = function(actionName, inputState, inputObject)
	if inputState ~= nil and inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
		return
	end

	if IsSmallScreenDevice or not playerlistCoreGuiEnabled then
		return
	end

	isOpen = not isOpen
	setVisible(isOpen)
end

Playerlist.IsOpen = function()
	return isOpen
end

Playerlist.HideTemp = function(self, key, hidden)
	if hidden then
		TempHideKeys[key] = true
	else
		TempHideKeys[key] = nil
	end

	-- Preserve isOpen, even when multiple systems temporarily hide the list.
	setVisible(isOpen, next(TempHideKeys) ~= nil)

	if next(TempHideKeys) == nil then
		setVisible(isOpen)
	end
end

if isTenFootInterface then
	pcall(function()
		topStat = TenFootInterface:SetupTopStat()
	end)
end

local function applyPlayerListEnabledState(forceOpen)
	local enabled = coreGuiAllEnabled
		and playerListSettingEnabled
		and topbarEnabled

	playerlistCoreGuiEnabled = enabled

	if forceOpen and enabled then
		isOpen = true
	end

	setVisible(isOpen)

	if isTenFootInterface and topStat then
		pcall(function()
			topStat:SetTopStatEnabled(enabled)
		end)
	end

	-- Do not bind Tab through ContextActionService.
	ContextActionService:UnbindAction("RbxPlayerListToggle")

	lastEffectivePlayerListEnabled = enabled
end

local function onCoreGuiChanged(coreGuiType, enabled)
	if coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.All then
		if type(enabled) == "boolean" then
			coreGuiAllEnabled = enabled
		else
			local current = readAllCoreGuiEnabled()
			if current ~= nil then
				coreGuiAllEnabled = current
			end
		end

		if coreGuiAllEnabled then
			local individual = readPlayerListEnabled()

			if individual ~= nil then
				playerListSettingEnabled = individual
				lastObservedPlayerListEnabled = individual
			end
		end

		applyPlayerListEnabledState(false)
		return
	end

	if coreGuiType ~= Enum.CoreGuiType.PlayerList then
		return
	end

	if type(enabled) ~= "boolean" then
		enabled = readPlayerListEnabled()
	end

	if type(enabled) ~= "boolean" then
		return
	end

	playerListSettingEnabled = enabled
	lastObservedPlayerListEnabled = enabled

	applyPlayerListEnabledState(false)
end

Playerlist.TopbarEnabledChanged = function(enabled)
	topbarEnabled = enabled == true

	local individual = readPlayerListEnabled()

	if individual ~= nil and coreGuiAllEnabled then
		playerListSettingEnabled = individual
	end

	applyPlayerListEnabledState(false)
end

local initialAllEnabled = readAllCoreGuiEnabled()
if initialAllEnabled ~= nil then
	coreGuiAllEnabled = initialAllEnabled
end

local initialPlayerListEnabled = readPlayerListEnabled()
if initialPlayerListEnabled ~= nil then
	playerListSettingEnabled = initialPlayerListEnabled
	lastObservedPlayerListEnabled = initialPlayerListEnabled
end

applyPlayerListEnabledState(false)

local signalConnected = pcall(function()
	StarterGui.CoreGuiChangedSignal:Connect(onCoreGuiChanged)
end)

if not signalConnected then
	pcall(function()
		StarterGui.CoreGuiChangedSignal:connect(onCoreGuiChanged)
	end)
end

-- Direct Tab handling. Ignore gameProcessed because legacy CoreScripts may
-- consume Tab first, but do not toggle while a text box is focused.
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Tab then
		return
	end

	if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
		return
	end

	Playerlist.ToggleVisibility(
		"KeyboardTab",
		Enum.UserInputState.Begin,
		input
	)
end)

-- Topbar integration. Recognized legacy button names are connected once.
local hookedTopbarButtons = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

local function normalizedName(name)
	return string.lower(string.gsub(tostring(name or ""), "[^%w]", ""))
end

local function isSupportedTopbarTree(instance)
	local current = instance

	while current and current ~= CoreGui do
		if current == Container or current:IsDescendantOf(Container) then
			return false
		end

		local name = normalizedName(current.Name)

		if name == "robloxgui" then
			return false
		end

		if name == "topbarapp"
			or name == "topbar"
			or name == "newrobloxgui" then
			return true
		end

		current = current.Parent
	end

	return false
end

local function isPlayerListControl(instance)
	local current = instance

	while current and current ~= CoreGui do
		local name = normalizedName(current.Name)

		if string.find(name, "playerlist", 1, true)
			or string.find(name, "leaderboard", 1, true) then
			return true
		end

		if name == "playersbutton"
			or name == "playersicon"
			or name == "playerstab" then
			return true
		end

		if name == "topbarapp" or name == "topbar" then
			break
		end

		current = current.Parent
	end

	return false
end

local function hookTopbarButton(instance)
	if not instance or not instance:IsA("GuiButton") then
		return
	end

	if hookedTopbarButtons[instance] then
		return
	end

	if not isSupportedTopbarTree(instance) then
		return
	end

	if not isPlayerListControl(instance) then
		return
	end

	local connected = pcall(function()
		instance.Activated:Connect(function()
			Playerlist.ToggleVisibility(
				"TopbarButton",
				Enum.UserInputState.Begin,
				nil
			)
		end)
	end)

	if connected then
		hookedTopbarButtons[instance] = true
	else
		warn("[PlayerlistModule] Could not connect " .. instance:GetFullName())
	end
end

local function scanTopbar(root)
	if not root then
		return
	end

	hookTopbarButton(root)

	for _, instance in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
		hookTopbarButton(instance)
	end
end

scanTopbar(CoreGui:FindFirstChild("TopBarApp"))
scanTopbar(CoreGui:FindFirstChild("TopBar"))
scanTopbar(CoreGui:FindFirstChild("Topbar"))
scanTopbar(CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui"))

CoreGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(instance)
	task.defer(function()
		if not instance.Parent then
			return
		end

		hookTopbarButton(instance)

		local name = normalizedName(instance.Name)

		if name == "topbarapp"
			or name == "topbar"
			or name == "newrobloxgui" then
			scanTopbar(instance)
		end
	end)
end)

-- Fallback monitor. Never reset isOpen just because the polling loop ran.
task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		task.wait(0.5)

		local currentAll = readAllCoreGuiEnabled()

		if currentAll ~= nil and currentAll ~= coreGuiAllEnabled then
			coreGuiAllEnabled = currentAll

			if currentAll then
				local individual = readPlayerListEnabled()

				if individual ~= nil then
					playerListSettingEnabled = individual
					lastObservedPlayerListEnabled = individual
				end
			end

			applyPlayerListEnabledState(false)
		end

		if coreGuiAllEnabled then
			local individual = readPlayerListEnabled()

			if individual ~= nil
				and individual ~= lastObservedPlayerListEnabled then
				playerListSettingEnabled = individual
				lastObservedPlayerListEnabled = individual
				applyPlayerListEnabledState(false)
			elseif lastEffectivePlayerListEnabled
				~= (coreGuiAllEnabled and playerListSettingEnabled and topbarEnabled) then
				applyPlayerListEnabledState(false)
			end
		elseif lastEffectivePlayerListEnabled ~= false then
			applyPlayerListEnabledState(false)
		end
	end
end)

resizePlayerList()

local function findRobloxGuiRoot()
	local node = script
	while node do
		if node.Name == "NewRobloxGui"
			and node:IsA("ScreenGui")
			and node.Parent == CoreGui then
			return node
		end
		node = node.Parent
	end

	return CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
		or CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
end

local RobloxGui = findRobloxGuiRoot()
assert(RobloxGui, "[BackpackScript] CoreGui.NewRobloxGui is missing")

local Modules = RobloxGui:WaitForChild("Modules", 15)
assert(Modules, "[BackpackScript] Modules folder is missing")

local function requireModule(parent, name)
	local instance = parent and parent:FindFirstChild(name)
	assert(
		instance and instance:IsA("ModuleScript"),
		"[BackpackScript] Missing ModuleScript "
			.. tostring(name)
			.. " under "
			.. (parent and parent:GetFullName() or "&lt;missing parent&gt;")
	)
	return require(instance)
end

local SettingsModules = Modules:FindFirstChild("Settings")
local Utility = requireModule(SettingsModules, "Utility")
local GameTranslator = requireModule(Modules, "GameTranslator")

local IsTenFootInterface = false
do
	local tenFootModule = Modules:FindFirstChild("TenFootInterface")
	if tenFootModule and tenFootModule:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return require(tenFootModule):IsEnabled()
		end)
		if ok then
			IsTenFootInterface = result == true
		else
			warn("[BackpackScript] TenFootInterface failed: " .. tostring(result))
		end
	end
end

-- The historical UI does not require these engine FastFlags.
-- Use the compatible, non-FormatByKey translation path.
local FFlagBackpackScriptUseFormatByKey = false
local FFlagCoreScriptTranslateGameText2 = false

local function translateGameText(instance, text)
	text = tostring(text or "")
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return GameTranslator:TranslateGameText(instance, text)
	end)
	return ok and type(result) == "string" and result or text
end

local function translateAndRegister(instance, source, text)
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		GameTranslator:TranslateAndRegister(instance, source, text)
	end)
	if not ok then
		warn("[BackpackScript] Translation registration failed: " .. tostring(err))
	end
end

local function isCoreGuiEnabled(coreGuiType)
	local ok, enabled = pcall(function()
		return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(coreGuiType)
	end)
	return ok and enabled == true
end
