local function EvaluateBackpackPanelVisibility(enabled)
	return enabled
		and isCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack)
		and TopbarEnabled
		and VRService.VREnabled
end

local function ShowVRBackpackPopup()
	if BackpackPanel and EvaluateBackpackPanelVisibility(true) then
		pcall(function()
			BackpackPanel:ForceShowForSeconds(2)
		end)
	end
end

local function NewGui(className, objectName)
	local gui = Instance.new(className)
	gui.Name = tostring(objectName or className)
	gui.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
	gui.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	gui.BorderColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
	gui.BorderSizePixel = 0
	gui.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)

	if gui:IsA("TextLabel") or gui:IsA("TextButton") or gui:IsA("TextBox") then
		gui.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		gui.Text = ""
		gui.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
		gui.TextSize = IsTenFootInterface and 36 or 14
		gui.TextWrapped = true

		if gui:IsA("TextButton") then
			gui.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
			gui.BorderSizePixel = 1
		end
	end

	return gui
end

local function FindLowestEmpty()
	for i = 1, NumberOfHotbarSlots do
		local slot = Slots[i]
		if slot and not slot.Tool then
			return slot
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function isInventoryEmpty()
	for i = NumberOfHotbarSlots + 1, #Slots do
		local slot = Slots[i]
		if slot and slot.Tool then
			return false
		end
	end
	return true
end

local function UseGazeSelection()
	return VRService.VREnabled
end

local function UpdateScrollingFrameCanvasSize()
	if not ScrollingFrame or not UIGridFrame then
		return
	end

	local cellWidth = ICON_SIZE + ICON_BUFFER
	local countX = math.max(
		1,
		math.floor(ScrollingFrame.AbsoluteSize.X / cellWidth)
	)

	local itemCount = 0
	for _, child in ipairs(UIGridFrame:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("GuiObject") and child ~= UIGridLayout then
			itemCount += 1
		end
	end

	local maxRow = math.ceil(itemCount / countX)
	ScrollingFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(
		0, 0, 0, maxRow * cellWidth + ICON_BUFFER
	)
end

local function AdjustHotbarFrames()
	if not InventoryFrame or not OpenInventoryButton then
		return
	end

	local inventoryOpen = InventoryFrame.Visible
	local visualTotal = inventoryOpen and NumberOfHotbarSlots or FullHotbarSlots
	local visualIndex = 0
	local hotbarIsVisible = visualTotal &gt;= 1

	for i = 1, NumberOfHotbarSlots do
		local slot = Slots[i]
		if slot and slot.Frame then
			if slot.Tool or inventoryOpen then
				visualIndex += 1
				slot:Readjust(visualIndex, math.max(1, visualTotal))
				slot.Frame.Visible = true
			else
				slot.Frame.Visible = false
			end
		end
	end

	OpenInventoryButton.Visible = not inventoryOpen
		and (hotbarIsVisible or not isInventoryEmpty())

	OpenInventoryButton.Position = UDim2.new(
		0.5, -15, 1, hotbarIsVisible and -110 or -50
	)
end

local function AdjustInventoryFrames()
	for i = NumberOfHotbarSlots + 1, #Slots do
		local slot = Slots[i]
		if slot and slot.Frame then
			slot.Frame.LayoutOrder = slot.Index
			slot.Frame.Visible = slot.Tool ~= nil
		end
	end
	UpdateScrollingFrameCanvasSize()
end

local function UpdateBackpackLayout()
	if not HotbarFrame or not InventoryFrame or not ScrollingFrame then
		return
	end

	HotbarFrame.Size = UDim2.new(
		0, ICON_BUFFER + NumberOfHotbarSlots * (ICON_SIZE + ICON_BUFFER),
		0, ICON_BUFFER + ICON_SIZE + ICON_BUFFER
	)

	HotbarFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
		0.5, -HotbarFrame.Size.X.Offset / 2,
		1, -HotbarFrame.Size.Y.Offset
	)

	InventoryFrame.Size = UDim2.new(
		0, HotbarFrame.Size.X.Offset,
		0,
		HotbarFrame.Size.Y.Offset * NumberOfInventoryRows
			+ INVENTORY_HEADER_SIZE
			+ (IsVR and 2 * INVENTORY_ARROWS_BUFFER_VR or 0)
	)

	InventoryFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
		0.5, -InventoryFrame.Size.X.Offset / 2,
		1, HotbarFrame.Position.Y.Offset - InventoryFrame.Size.Y.Offset
	)

	ScrollingFrame.Size = UDim2.new(
		1, ScrollingFrame.ScrollBarThickness + 1,
		1, -INVENTORY_HEADER_SIZE
		- (IsVR and 2 * INVENTORY_ARROWS_BUFFER_VR or 0)
	)

	ScrollingFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
		0, 0, 0,
		INVENTORY_HEADER_SIZE + (IsVR and INVENTORY_ARROWS_BUFFER_VR or 0)
	)

	AdjustHotbarFrames()
	AdjustInventoryFrames()
end

local function Clamp(low, high, num)
	return math.min(high, math.max(low, num))
end

local function CheckBounds(guiObject, x, y)
	local pos = guiObject.AbsolutePosition
	local size = guiObject.AbsoluteSize
	return x &gt; pos.X and x &lt;= pos.X + size.X
		and y &gt; pos.Y and y &lt;= pos.Y + size.Y
end

local function GetOffset(guiObject, point)
	local center = guiObject.AbsolutePosition + guiObject.AbsoluteSize / 2
	return (center - point).Magnitude
end

local function DisableActiveHopper()
	if not ActiveHopper then
		return
	end

	pcall(function()
		ActiveHopper:ToggleSelect()
	end)

	local slot = SlotsByTool[ActiveHopper]
	if slot then
		slot:UpdateEquipView()
	end

	ActiveHopper = nil
end

local function UnequipAllTools()
	if Humanoid then
		pcall(function()
			Humanoid:UnequipTools()
		end)
	end

	if ActiveHopper then
		DisableActiveHopper()
	end
end

local function EquipNewTool(tool)
	if not tool or not Character then
		return
	end

	UnequipAllTools()

	if tool:IsA("HopperBin") then
		pcall(function()
			tool:ToggleSelect()
		end)

		local slot = SlotsByTool[tool]
		if slot then
			slot:UpdateEquipView()
		end

		ActiveHopper = tool
	else
		-- Preserve the historical backpack's direct-parent equip behavior.
		tool.Parent = Character
	end
end

local function IsEquipped(tool)
	return tool and (
		(tool:IsA("HopperBin") and tool.Active)
			or tool.Parent == Character
	)
end

local function MakeSlot(parent, index)
	index = index or (#Slots + 1)

	local slot = {
		Tool = nil,
		Index = index,
		Frame = nil,
	}

	local LocalizedName = ""
	local LocalizedToolTip = ""
	local SlotFrame
	local FakeSlotFrame
	local ToolIcon
	local ToolName
	local ToolChangeConn
	local HighlightFrame
	local SelectionObj
	local ToolTip
	local SlotNumber

	local function UpdateSlotFading()
		if not SlotFrame then
			return
		end

		if VRService.VREnabled and BackpackPanel then
			local panelTransparency = BackpackPanel.transparency or 0
			local finalTransparency = panelTransparency
				+ SLOT_FADE_LOCKED
			- panelTransparency * SLOT_FADE_LOCKED

			SlotFrame.BackgroundTransparency = finalTransparency
			SlotFrame.TextTransparency = finalTransparency

			if ToolIcon then
				ToolIcon.ImageTransparency =
					InventoryFrame.Visible and 0 or panelTransparency
			end

			if HighlightFrame then
				for _, child in ipairs(HighlightFrame:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("GuiObject") then
						child.BackgroundTransparency = finalTransparency
					end
				end
			end

			SlotFrame.SelectionImageObject = SelectionObj
		else
			SlotFrame.SelectionImageObject = nil
			SlotFrame.BackgroundTransparency =
				SlotFrame.Draggable and 0 or SLOT_FADE_LOCKED
		end

		SlotFrame.BackgroundColor3 = SlotFrame.Draggable
			and SLOT_DRAGGABLE_COLOR or BACKGROUND_COLOR
	end

	function slot:Readjust(visualIndex, visualTotal)
		local centered = HotbarFrame.Size.X.Offset / 2
		local sizePlus = ICON_BUFFER + ICON_SIZE
		local midpoint = visualTotal / 2 + 0.5
		local factor = visualIndex - midpoint

		SlotFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
			0, centered - ICON_SIZE / 2 + sizePlus * factor,
			0, ICON_BUFFER
		)
	end

	function slot:Fill(tool)
		if not tool then
			return self:Clear()
		end

		self.Tool = tool

		local function assignToolData()
			if not ToolIcon or not ToolName then
				return
			end

			local icon = tool.TextureId or ""
			ToolIcon.Image = icon

			LocalizedName = translateGameText(tool, tool.Name)
			ToolName.Text = icon == "" and LocalizedName or ""
			ToolName.Visible = icon == ""

			if ToolTip and tool:IsA("Tool") then
				LocalizedToolTip = translateGameText(tool, tool.ToolTip)
				ToolTip.Text = LocalizedToolTip
				local width = ToolTip.TextBounds.X + TOOLTIP_BUFFER
				ToolTip.Size = UDim2.new(0, width, 0, TOOLTIP_HEIGHT)
				ToolTip.Position = UDim2.new(
					0.5, -width / 2, 0, TOOLTIP_OFFSET
				)
			end
		end

		assignToolData()

		if ToolChangeConn then
			ToolChangeConn:Disconnect()
			ToolChangeConn = nil
		end

		ToolChangeConn = tool.Changed:Connect(function(property)
			if property == "TextureId"
				or property == "Name"
				or property == "ToolTip" then
				assignToolData()
			end
		end)

		local hotbarSlot = self.Index &lt;= NumberOfHotbarSlots

		if (not hotbarSlot or InventoryFrame.Visible)
			and not UserInputService.VREnabled then
			SlotFrame.Draggable = true
		end

		self:UpdateEquipView()

		if hotbarSlot then
			FullHotbarSlots += 1

			if WholeThingEnabled
				and FullHotbarSlots &gt;= 1
				and not GamepadActionsBound then
				GamepadActionsBound = true
				pcall(function()
					ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(
						"RBXHotbarEquip",
						changeToolFunc,
						false,
						Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL1,
						Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR1
					)
				end)
			end
		end

		SlotsByTool[tool] = self
		LowestEmptySlot = FindLowestEmpty()
	end

	function slot:Clear()
		if not self.Tool then
			return
		end

		if ToolChangeConn then
			ToolChangeConn:Disconnect()
			ToolChangeConn = nil
		end

		ToolIcon.Image = ""
		ToolName.Text = ""

		if ToolTip then
			ToolTip.Text = ""
			ToolTip.Visible = false
		end

		SlotFrame.Draggable = false
		self:UpdateEquipView(true)

		if self.Index &lt;= NumberOfHotbarSlots then
			FullHotbarSlots = math.max(0, FullHotbarSlots - 1)

			if FullHotbarSlots &lt; 1 then
				GamepadActionsBound = false
				pcall(function()
					ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction("RBXHotbarEquip")
				end)
			end
		end

		SlotsByTool[self.Tool] = nil
		self.Tool = nil
		LowestEmptySlot = FindLowestEmpty()
	end

	function slot:UpdateEquipView(unequippedOverride)
		if not unequippedOverride and IsEquipped(self.Tool) then
			lastEquippedSlot = slot

			if not HighlightFrame then
				HighlightFrame = NewGui("Frame", "Equipped")
				HighlightFrame.ZIndex = SlotFrame.ZIndex

				local t = SLOT_EQUIP_THICKNESS
				local edges = {
					{t, 1, 0, 0},
					{1 - 2 * t, t, t, 0},
					{t, 1, 1 - t, 0},
					{1 - 2 * t, t, t, 1 - t},
				}

				for _, data in ipairs(edges) do
					local edge = NewGui("Frame", "Edge")
					edge.BackgroundTransparency = 0
					edge.BackgroundColor3 = SLOT_EQUIP_COLOR
					edge.Size = UDim2.new(data[1], 0, data[2], 0)
					edge.Position = UDim2.new(data[3], 0, data[4], 0)
					edge.ZIndex = HighlightFrame.ZIndex
					edge.Parent = HighlightFrame
				end
			end

			HighlightFrame.Parent = SlotFrame
		elseif HighlightFrame then
			HighlightFrame.Parent = nil
		end

		UpdateSlotFading()
	end

	function slot:IsEquipped()
		return IsEquipped(self.Tool)
	end

	function slot:Delete()
		if SlotFrame then
			SlotFrame:Destroy()
		end

		table.remove(Slots, self.Index)

		for i = self.Index, #Slots do
			Slots[i]:SlideBack()
		end

		UpdateScrollingFrameCanvasSize()
		LowestEmptySlot = FindLowestEmpty()
	end

	function slot:Swap(targetSlot)
		if not targetSlot or targetSlot == self then
			return
		end

		local myTool = self.Tool
		local otherTool = targetSlot.Tool

		self:Clear()
		targetSlot:Clear()

		if otherTool then
			self:Fill(otherTool)
		end

		if myTool then
			targetSlot:Fill(myTool)
		end
	end

	function slot:SlideBack()
		self.Index -= 1
		SlotFrame.Name = tostring(self.Index)
		SlotFrame.LayoutOrder = self.Index
	end

	function slot:TurnNumber(on)
		if SlotNumber then
			SlotNumber.Visible = on
		end
	end

	function slot:SetClickability(on)
		if self.Tool then
			SlotFrame.Draggable = not on and not UserInputService.VREnabled
			UpdateSlotFading()
		end
	end

	function slot:CheckTerms(terms)
		local hits = 0
		local function checkText(str, term)
			str = tostring(str or ""):lower()
			term = tostring(term or ""):lower()
			local _, count = str:find(term, 1, true)
			if count then
				hits += 1
			end
		end

		if self.Tool then
			for term in pairs(terms) do
				checkText(LocalizedName, term)
				if self.Tool:IsA("Tool") then
					checkText(LocalizedToolTip, term)
				end
			end
		end

		return hits
	end

	function slot:Select()
		local tool = self.Tool
		if not tool then
			return
		end

		if IsEquipped(tool) then
			UnequipAllTools()
		elseif tool.Parent == Backpack then
			EquipNewTool(tool)
		end
	end

	SlotFrame = NewGui("TextButton", tostring(index))
	SlotFrame.BackgroundColor3 = BACKGROUND_COLOR
	SlotFrame.BorderColor3 = SLOT_BORDER_COLOR
	SlotFrame.Text = ""
	SlotFrame.AutoButtonColor = false
	SlotFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	SlotFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, ICON_SIZE, 0, ICON_SIZE)
	SlotFrame.Active = true
	SlotFrame.Draggable = false
	SlotFrame.BackgroundTransparency = SLOT_FADE_LOCKED
	SlotFrame.Activated:Connect(function()
		if changeSlot then
			changeSlot(slot)
		end
	end)
	slot.Frame = SlotFrame

	local selectionClipper = NewGui("Frame", "SelectionObjectClipper")
	selectionClipper.Visible = false
	selectionClipper.Parent = SlotFrame

	SelectionObj = NewGui("ImageLabel", "Selector")
	SelectionObj.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	SelectionObj.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Keyboard/key_selection_9slice.png"
	SelectionObj.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
	SelectionObj.SliceCenter = Rect.new(12, 12, 52, 52)
	SelectionObj.Parent = selectionClipper

	ToolIcon = NewGui("ImageLabel", "Icon")
	ToolIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 0.8, 0)
	ToolIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0.1, 0, 0.1, 0)
	ToolIcon.Parent = SlotFrame

	ToolName = NewGui("TextLabel", "ToolName")
	ToolName.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 1, -2)
	ToolName.Position = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 1)
	ToolName.Parent = SlotFrame

	SlotFrame.LayoutOrder = slot.Index

	if index &lt;= NumberOfHotbarSlots then
		ToolTip = NewGui("TextLabel", "ToolTip")
		ToolTip.TextWrapped = false
		ToolTip.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
		ToolTip.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0.4, 0.4, 0.4)
		ToolTip.BackgroundTransparency = 0
		ToolTip.Visible = false
		ToolTip.Parent = SlotFrame

		SlotFrame.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
			if ToolTip.Text ~= "" then
				ToolTip.Visible = true
			end
		end)

		SlotFrame.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
			ToolTip.Visible = false
		end)

		function slot:MoveToInventory()
			if self.Index &gt; NumberOfHotbarSlots then
				return
			end

			local tool = self.Tool
			if not tool then
				return
			end

			self:Clear()
			local newSlot = MakeSlot(UIGridFrame)
			newSlot:Fill(tool)

			if IsEquipped(tool) then
				UnequipAllTools()
			end

			if ViewingSearchResults then
				newSlot.Frame.Visible = false
				newSlot.Frame.Parent = InventoryFrame
			end
		end

		if index &lt; 10 or index == NumberOfHotbarSlots then
			local slotNum = index &lt; 10 and index or 0
			SlotNumber = NewGui("TextLabel", "Number")
			SlotNumber.Text = tostring(slotNum)
			SlotNumber.Size = UDim2.new(0.15, 0, 0.15, 0)
			SlotNumber.Visible = false
			SlotNumber.Parent = SlotFrame
			HotkeyFns[ZERO_KEY_VALUE + slotNum] = slot.Select
		end
	end

	-- Drag and drop.
	do
		local startPoint = SlotFrame.Position
		local lastUpTime = 0
		local startParent

		SlotFrame.DragBegin:Connect(function(dragPoint)
			Dragging[SlotFrame] = true
			startPoint = dragPoint
			SlotFrame.BorderSizePixel = 2
			SlotFrame.ZIndex = 2
			ToolIcon.ZIndex = 2
			ToolName.ZIndex = 2

			if SlotNumber then
				SlotNumber.ZIndex = 2
			end

			if HighlightFrame then
				HighlightFrame.ZIndex = 2
				for _, child in ipairs(HighlightFrame:GetChildren()) do
					child.ZIndex = 2
				end
			end

			startParent = SlotFrame.Parent

			if startParent == UIGridFrame and InventoryFrame then
				SlotFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
					0, SlotFrame.AbsolutePosition.X - InventoryFrame.AbsolutePosition.X,
					0, SlotFrame.AbsolutePosition.Y - InventoryFrame.AbsolutePosition.Y
				)
				SlotFrame.Parent = InventoryFrame

				FakeSlotFrame = NewGui("Frame", "FakeSlot")
				FakeSlotFrame.LayoutOrder = SlotFrame.LayoutOrder
				FakeSlotFrame.Size = SlotFrame.Size
				FakeSlotFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				FakeSlotFrame.Parent = UIGridFrame
			end
		end)

		SlotFrame.DragStopped:Connect(function(x, y)
			if FakeSlotFrame then
				FakeSlotFrame:Destroy()
				FakeSlotFrame = nil
			end

			local now = tick()
			SlotFrame.Position = startPoint
			SlotFrame.Parent = startParent
			SlotFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
			SlotFrame.ZIndex = 1
			ToolIcon.ZIndex = 1
			ToolName.ZIndex = 1

			if SlotNumber then
				SlotNumber.ZIndex = 1
			end

			if HighlightFrame then
				HighlightFrame.ZIndex = 1
				for _, child in ipairs(HighlightFrame:GetChildren()) do
					child.ZIndex = 1
				end
			end

			Dragging[SlotFrame] = nil

			if not slot.Tool then
				return
			end

			if CheckBounds(InventoryFrame, x, y) then
				if slot.Index &lt;= NumberOfHotbarSlots then
					slot:MoveToInventory()
				elseif now - lastUpTime &lt; DOUBLE_CLICK_TIME and LowestEmptySlot then
					local tool = slot.Tool
					slot:Clear()
					LowestEmptySlot:Fill(tool)
					slot:Delete()
					now = 0
				end
			elseif CheckBounds(HotbarFrame, x, y) then
				local closestDistance = math.huge
				local closestSlot

				for i = 1, NumberOfHotbarSlots do
					local candidate = Slots[i]
					if candidate and candidate.Frame then
						local distance = GetOffset(candidate.Frame, Vector2.new(x, y))
						if distance &lt; closestDistance then
							closestDistance = distance
							closestSlot = candidate
						end
					end
				end

				if closestSlot and closestSlot ~= slot then
					slot:Swap(closestSlot)

					if slot.Index &gt; NumberOfHotbarSlots and not slot.Tool then
						slot:Delete()
					elseif slot.Index &gt; NumberOfHotbarSlots and ViewingSearchResults then
						slot.Frame.Visible = false
						slot.Frame.Parent = InventoryFrame
					end

					if closestSlot.Index &gt; NumberOfHotbarSlots and not closestSlot.Tool then
						closestSlot:Delete()
					end
				end
			elseif slot.Index &lt;= NumberOfHotbarSlots then
				slot:MoveToInventory()
			end

			lastUpTime = now
		end)
	end

	SlotFrame.Parent = parent
	Slots[index] = slot

	if index &gt; NumberOfHotbarSlots then
		UpdateScrollingFrameCanvasSize()
		if InventoryFrame.Visible and not ViewingSearchResults then
			local offset = ScrollingFrame.CanvasSize.Y.Offset
			- ScrollingFrame.AbsoluteSize.Y
			ScrollingFrame.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, offset))
		end
	end

	return slot
end

local function SetNumberOfHotbarSlots(numSlots)
	if NumberOfHotbarSlots == numSlots then
		return
	end

	local previousSlots = NumberOfHotbarSlots

	if previousSlots &gt; numSlots then
		for i = previousSlots, numSlots + 1, -1 do
			local slot = Slots[i]
			if slot then
				if slot.Tool then
					slot:MoveToInventory()
				end
				if Slots[i] then
					Slots[i]:Delete()
				end
			end
		end
	else
		for i = previousSlots + 1, numSlots do
			NumberOfHotbarSlots = i
			local oldSlot = Slots[i]

			if oldSlot then
				local tool = oldSlot.Tool
				local newSlot = MakeSlot(HotbarFrame, i)
				if tool then
					oldSlot:Clear()
					newSlot:Fill(tool)
				end
			else
				local slot = MakeSlot(HotbarFrame, i)
				slot.Frame.Visible = false
			end
		end
	end

	NumberOfHotbarSlots = numSlots
	FullHotbarSlots = 0

	for i = 1, NumberOfHotbarSlots do
		if Slots[i] and Slots[i].Tool then
			FullHotbarSlots += 1
		end
	end

	LowestEmptySlot = FindLowestEmpty()
	UpdateBackpackLayout()
end

local function OnChildAdded(child)
	if not child:IsA("Tool") and not child:IsA("HopperBin") then
		if child:IsA("Humanoid") and child.Parent == Character then
			Humanoid = child
		end
		return
	end

	local tool = child

	if tool.Parent == Character then
		ShowVRBackpackPopup()
		TimeOfLastToolChange = tick()
	end

	if ActiveHopper and tool.Parent == Character then
		DisableActiveHopper()
	end

	if not StarterToolFound and tool.Parent == Character and not SlotsByTool[tool] then
		local starterGear = Player and Player:FindFirstChild("StarterGear")

		if starterGear and starterGear:FindFirstChild(tool.Name) then
			StarterToolFound = true
			local slot = LowestEmptySlot or MakeSlot(UIGridFrame)

			for i = slot.Index, 1, -1 do
				local current = Slots[i]
				if i &gt; 1 then
					local previous = Slots[i - 1]
					if previous and current then
						previous:Swap(current)
					end
				elseif current then
					current:Fill(tool)
				end
			end

			for _, characterChild in ipairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
				if characterChild:IsA("Tool") and characterChild ~= tool then
					characterChild.Parent = Backpack
				end
			end

			AdjustHotbarFrames()
			return
		end
	end

	local slot = SlotsByTool[tool]

	if slot then
		slot:UpdateEquipView()
	else
		slot = LowestEmptySlot or MakeSlot(UIGridFrame)
		slot:Fill(tool)

		if slot.Index &lt;= NumberOfHotbarSlots and not InventoryFrame.Visible then
			AdjustHotbarFrames()
		end

		if tool:IsA("HopperBin") and tool.Active then
			UnequipAllTools()
			ActiveHopper = tool
		end
	end
end

local function OnChildRemoved(child)
	if not child:IsA("Tool") and not child:IsA("HopperBin") then
		return
	end

	ShowVRBackpackPopup()
	TimeOfLastToolChange = tick()

	if child.Parent == Character or child.Parent == Backpack then
		return
	end

	local slot = SlotsByTool[child]
	if slot then
		slot:Clear()

		if slot.Index &gt; NumberOfHotbarSlots then
			slot:Delete()
		elseif not InventoryFrame.Visible then
			AdjustHotbarFrames()
		end
	end

	if child == ActiveHopper then
		ActiveHopper = nil
	end
end

local function OnCharacterAdded(character)
	for i = #Slots, 1, -1 do
		local slot = Slots[i]
		if slot then
			if slot.Tool then
				slot:Clear()
			end
			if i &gt; NumberOfHotbarSlots then
				slot:Delete()
			end
		end
	end

	ActiveHopper = nil

	for _, connection in ipairs(CharConns) do
		pcall(function()
			connection:Disconnect()
		end)
	end
	table.clear(CharConns)

	Character = character
	Humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

	table.insert(CharConns, character.ChildRemoved:Connect(OnChildRemoved))
	table.insert(CharConns, character.ChildAdded:Connect(OnChildAdded))

	for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
		OnChildAdded(child)
	end

	Backpack = Player:WaitForChild("Backpack")
	table.insert(CharConns, Backpack.ChildRemoved:Connect(OnChildRemoved))
	table.insert(CharConns, Backpack.ChildAdded:Connect(OnChildAdded))

	for _, child in ipairs(Backpack:GetChildren()) do
		OnChildAdded(child)
	end

	AdjustHotbarFrames()
	AdjustInventoryFrames()
end

local function OnInputBegan(input, isProcessed)
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard
		and not TextBoxFocused
		and (WholeThingEnabled or input.KeyCode.Value == DROP_HOTKEY_VALUE) then
		local callback = HotkeyFns[input.KeyCode.Value]
		if callback then
			callback(isProcessed)
		end
	end
end

local function OnUISChanged(property)
	if property == "KeyboardEnabled" or property == "VREnabled" then
		local on = UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled and not UserInputService.VREnabled

		for i = 1, NumberOfHotbarSlots do
			local slot = Slots[i]
			if slot then
				slot:TurnNumber(on)
			end
		end
	end
end

local lastChangeToolInputObject
local lastChangeToolInputTime
local maxEquipDeltaTime = 0.06
local noOpFunc = function() end
local selectDirection = Vector2.new(0, 0)

unbindAllGamepadEquipActions = function()
	pcall(function()
		ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction("RBXBackpackHasGamepadFocus")
		ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction("RBXCloseInventory")
	end)
end

local function setHotbarVisibility(visible, isInventoryScreen)
	for i = 1, NumberOfHotbarSlots do
		local slot = Slots[i]
		if slot and slot.Frame and (isInventoryScreen or slot.Tool) then
			slot.Frame.Visible = visible
		end
	end
end

local function getInputDirection(inputObject)
	local modifier = inputObject.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End and -1 or 1

	if inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Thumbstick1 then
		local position = inputObject.Position
		local magnitude = position.Magnitude

		if magnitude &gt; 0.98 then
			selectDirection = Vector2.new(
				position.X / magnitude,
				-position.Y / magnitude
			)
		else
			selectDirection = Vector2.new(0, 0)
		end
	elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.DPadLeft then
		selectDirection = Vector2.new(selectDirection.X - modifier, selectDirection.Y)
	elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.DPadRight then
		selectDirection = Vector2.new(selectDirection.X + modifier, selectDirection.Y)
	elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.DPadUp then
		selectDirection = Vector2.new(selectDirection.X, selectDirection.Y - modifier)
	elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.DPadDown then
		selectDirection = Vector2.new(selectDirection.X, selectDirection.Y + modifier)
	else
		selectDirection = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	end

	return selectDirection
end

local function selectToolExperiment(actionName, inputState, inputObject)
	local direction = getInputDirection(inputObject)
	if direction == Vector2.new(0, 0) then
		return
	end

	local angle = math.atan2(direction.Y, direction.X) - math.atan2(-1, 0)
	if angle &lt; 0 then
		angle += math.pi * 2
	end

	local index = math.floor(angle / (math.pi * 0.25) + 1.5)
	if index &gt; NumberOfHotbarSlots then
		index = 1
	end

	local slot = Slots[index]
	if slot and slot.Tool and not slot:IsEquipped() then
		slot:Select()
	else
		UnequipAllTools()
	end
end

changeToolFunc = function(actionName, inputState, inputObject)
	if inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
		return
	end

	if lastChangeToolInputObject then
		local opposite = (
			lastChangeToolInputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR1
				and inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL1
		) or (
			lastChangeToolInputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL1
				and inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR1
		)

		if opposite and tick() - lastChangeToolInputTime &lt;= maxEquipDeltaTime then
			UnequipAllTools()
			lastChangeToolInputObject = inputObject
			lastChangeToolInputTime = tick()
			return
		end
	end

	lastChangeToolInputObject = inputObject
	lastChangeToolInputTime = tick()

	task.delay(maxEquipDeltaTime, function()
		if lastChangeToolInputObject ~= inputObject then
			return
		end

		local direction = inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL1 and -1 or 1

		for i = 1, NumberOfHotbarSlots do
			local slot = Slots[i]
			if slot and slot:IsEquipped() then
				local targetIndex = i + direction

				if targetIndex &gt; NumberOfHotbarSlots then
					targetIndex = 1
					UnequipAllTools()
					lastEquippedSlot = nil
					return
				elseif targetIndex &lt; 1 then
					targetIndex = NumberOfHotbarSlots
					UnequipAllTools()
					lastEquippedSlot = nil
					return
				end

				for _ = 1, NumberOfHotbarSlots do
					local target = Slots[targetIndex]
					if target and target.Tool then
						target:Select()
						return
					end

					targetIndex += direction
					if targetIndex &gt; NumberOfHotbarSlots then
						targetIndex = 1
					elseif targetIndex &lt; 1 then
						targetIndex = NumberOfHotbarSlots
					end
				end

				return
			end
		end

		if lastEquippedSlot and lastEquippedSlot.Tool then
			lastEquippedSlot:Select()
			return
		end

		local startIndex = direction == -1 and NumberOfHotbarSlots or 1
		local endIndex = direction == -1 and 1 or NumberOfHotbarSlots

		for i = startIndex, endIndex, direction do
			if Slots[i] and Slots[i].Tool then
				Slots[i]:Select()
				return
			end
		end
	end)
end

getGamepadSwapSlot = function()
	for i = 1, #Slots do
		local slot = Slots[i]
		if slot and slot.Frame and slot.Frame.BorderSizePixel &gt; 0 then
			return slot
		end
	end
	return nil
end

changeSlot = function(slot)
	if not slot or not slot.Frame then
		return
	end

	local swapInVr = not VRService.VREnabled or InventoryFrame.Visible

	if slot.Frame == GuiService.SelectedCoreObject and swapInVr then
		local selectedSlot = getGamepadSwapSlot()

		if selectedSlot then
			selectedSlot.Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0

			if selectedSlot ~= slot then
				slot:Swap(selectedSlot)

				if VRInventorySelector and VRInventorySelector.SelectionImageObject then
					VRInventorySelector.SelectionImageObject.Visible = false
				end

				if slot.Index &gt; NumberOfHotbarSlots and not slot.Tool then
					slot:Delete()
				end

				if selectedSlot.Index &gt; NumberOfHotbarSlots and not selectedSlot.Tool then
					selectedSlot:Delete()
				end
			end
		else
			local startSize = slot.Frame.Size
			local startPosition = slot.Frame.Position

			slot.Frame:TweenSizeAndPosition(
				startSize + UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10),
				startPosition - UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 5),
				Enum.EasingDirection.Out,
				Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
				0.1,
				true,
				function()
					if slot.Frame and slot.Frame.Parent then
						slot.Frame:TweenSizeAndPosition(
							startSize,
							startPosition,
							Enum.EasingDirection.In,
							Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
							0.1,
							true
						)
					end
				end
			)

			slot.Frame.BorderSizePixel = 3

			if VRInventorySelector and VRInventorySelector.SelectionImageObject then
				VRInventorySelector.SelectionImageObject.Visible = true
			end
		end
	else
		slot:Select()

		if VRInventorySelector and VRInventorySelector.SelectionImageObject then
			VRInventorySelector.SelectionImageObject.Visible = false
		end
	end
end

vrMoveSlotToInventory = function()
	if not VRService.VREnabled then
		return
	end

	local slot = getGamepadSwapSlot()
	if slot and slot.Tool then
		slot.Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
		slot:MoveToInventory()

		if VRInventorySelector and VRInventorySelector.SelectionImageObject then
			VRInventorySelector.SelectionImageObject.Visible = false
		end
	end
end

enableGamepadInventoryControl = function()
	local function goBackOneLevel(_, inputState)
		if inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
			return
		end

		local selected = getGamepadSwapSlot()
		if selected then
			selected.Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
		elseif InventoryFrame.Visible then
			BackpackScript.OpenClose()
			task.spawn(function()
				pcall(function()
					GuiService:SetMenuIsOpen(false)
				end)
			end)
		end
	end

	pcall(function()
		ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(
			"RBXBackpackHasGamepadFocus",
			noOpFunc,
			false,
			Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1
		)

		ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(
			"RBXCloseInventory",
			goBackOneLevel,
			false,
			Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB,
			Enum.KeyCode.ButtonStart
		)
	end)

	if not UseGazeSelection() and HotbarFrame then
		pcall(function()
			GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = HotbarFrame:FindFirstChild("1")
		end)
	end
end

disableGamepadInventoryControl = function()
	unbindAllGamepadEquipActions()

	for i = 1, NumberOfHotbarSlots do
		local slot = Slots[i]
		if slot and slot.Frame then
			slot.Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
		end
	end

	pcall(function()
		if GuiService.SelectedCoreObject
			and GuiService.SelectedCoreObject:IsDescendantOf(MainFrame) then
			GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = nil
		end
	end)
end

local function bindBackpackHotbarAction()
	if WholeThingEnabled and not GamepadActionsBound then
		GamepadActionsBound = true
		pcall(function()
			ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(
				"RBXHotbarEquip",
				changeToolFunc,
				false,
				Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL1,
				Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR1
			)
		end)
	end
end

local function unbindBackpackHotbarAction()
	disableGamepadInventoryControl()
	GamepadActionsBound = false
	pcall(function()
		ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction("RBXHotbarEquip")
	end)
end

gamepadDisconnected = function()
	GamepadEnabled = false
	disableGamepadInventoryControl()
end

gamepadConnected = function()
	GamepadEnabled = true

	pcall(function()
		GuiService:AddSelectionParent("RBXBackpackSelection", MainFrame)
	end)

	if FullHotbarSlots &gt;= 1 then
		bindBackpackHotbarAction()
	end

	if InventoryFrame.Visible then
		enableGamepadInventoryControl()
	end
end

local function OnCoreGuiChanged(coreGuiType, enabled)
	if coreGuiType ~= Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack
		and coreGuiType ~= Enum.CoreGuiType.All then
		return
	end

	WholeThingEnabled = enabled == true and TopbarEnabled
	MainFrame.Visible = WholeThingEnabled

	for _, keyString in ipairs(HotkeyStrings) do
		pcall(function()
			if WholeThingEnabled then
				GuiService:AddKey(keyString)
			else
				GuiService:RemoveKey(keyString)
			end
		end)
	end

	if WholeThingEnabled then
		if FullHotbarSlots &gt;= 1 then
			bindBackpackHotbarAction()
		end
	else
		unbindBackpackHotbarAction()
	end
end

local function MakeVRRoundButton(name, image)
	local button = NewGui("ImageButton", name)
	button.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 40)
	button.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Keyboard/close_button_background.png"

	local icon = NewGui("ImageLabel", "Icon")
	icon.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
	icon.Position = UDim2.new(0.25, 0, 0.25, 0)
	icon.Image = image
	icon.Parent = button

	local selection = NewGui("ImageLabel", "Selection")
	selection.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0.9, 0)
	selection.Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.05, 0)
	selection.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Keyboard/close_button_selection.png"
	button.SelectionImageObject = selection

	return button, icon, selection
end

	local maxY = math.max(
		0,
		ScrollingFrame.CanvasSize.Y.Offset - ScrollingFrame.AbsoluteSize.Y
	)
	ScrollUpInventoryButton.Visible = ScrollingFrame.CanvasPosition.Y &gt; 0
	ScrollDownInventoryButton.Visible = ScrollingFrame.CanvasPosition.Y &lt; maxY
end)

local gamepadHintsFrame = Utility:Create("Frame")({
	Name = "GamepadHintsFrame",
	Size = UDim2.new(0, HotbarFrame.Size.X.Offset, 0, IsTenFootInterface and 95 or 60),
	BackgroundTransparency = 1,
	Visible = false,
	Parent = MainFrame,
})

local function addGamepadHint(hintImage, hintImageLarge, hintText)
	local hintFrame = Utility:Create("Frame")({
		Name = "HintFrame",
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -5),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Parent = gamepadHintsFrame,
	})

	Utility:Create("ImageLabel")({
		Name = "HintImage",
		Size = IsTenFootInterface and UDim2.new(0, 90, 0, 90)
			or UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 60),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Image = IsTenFootInterface and hintImageLarge or hintImage,
		Parent = hintFrame,
	})

	local label = Utility:Create("TextLabel")({
		Name = "HintText",
		Position = UDim2.new(0, IsTenFootInterface and 100 or 70, 0, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -(IsTenFootInterface and 100 or 70), 1, 0),
		Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold,
		TextSize = IsTenFootInterface and 36 or 24,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = hintText,
		TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextWrapped = true,
		Parent = hintFrame,
	})

	local constraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
	constraint.MaxTextSize = label.TextSize
	constraint.Parent = label
end

	"rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Help/XButtonDark.png",
	"rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Help/XButtonDark@2x.png",
	"Remove From Hotbar"
)

	"rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Help/BButtonDark@2x.png",
	"Close Backpack"
)

local function resizeGamepadHintsFrame()
	gamepadHintsFrame.Size = UDim2.new(
		HotbarFrame.Size.X.Scale,
		HotbarFrame.Size.X.Offset,
		0,
		IsTenFootInterface and 95 or 60
	)

	gamepadHintsFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
		HotbarFrame.Position.X.Scale,
		HotbarFrame.Position.X.Offset,
		InventoryFrame.Position.Y.Scale,
		InventoryFrame.Position.Y.Offset - gamepadHintsFrame.Size.Y.Offset
	)

	local hints = gamepadHintsFrame:GetChildren()
	local spaceTaken = 0

	for _, hint in ipairs(hints) do
		if hint:IsA("GuiObject") and hint:FindFirstChild("HintText") then
			local hintText = hint.HintText
			hint.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -5)
			hint.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
			spaceTaken += hintText.Position.X.Offset + hintText.TextBounds.X
		end
	end

	local hintCount = #hints
	if hintCount &lt;= 1 then
		return
	end

	local spaceBetween = (gamepadHintsFrame.AbsoluteSize.X - spaceTaken) / (hintCount - 1)

	for i, hint in ipairs(hints) do
		if hint:IsA("GuiObject") and hint:FindFirstChild("HintText") then
			if i &gt; 1 then
				local previous = hints[i - 1]
				hint.Position = UDim2.new(
					0,
					previous.Position.X.Offset + previous.Size.X.Offset + spaceBetween,
					0,
					0
				)
			end

			hint.Size = UDim2.new(
				0,
				hint.HintText.Position.X.Offset + hint.HintText.TextBounds.X,
				1,
				-5
			)
		end
	end
end

-- Search controls.
do
	local searchFrame = NewGui("Frame", "Search")
	searchFrame.BackgroundColor3 = SEARCH_BACKGROUND_COLOR
	searchFrame.BackgroundTransparency = SEARCH_BACKGROUND_FADE
	searchFrame.Size = UDim2.new(
		0, SEARCH_WIDTH - SEARCH_BUFFER * 2,
		0, INVENTORY_HEADER_SIZE - SEARCH_BUFFER * 2
	)
	searchFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
		1, -searchFrame.Size.X.Offset - SEARCH_BUFFER,
		0, SEARCH_BUFFER
	)
	searchFrame.Parent = InventoryFrame

	local searchBox = NewGui("TextBox", "TextBox")
	searchBox.Text = SEARCH_TEXT
	searchBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
	searchBox.TextSize = 24
	searchBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	searchBox.Size = searchFrame.Size - UDim2.new(0, SEARCH_TEXT_OFFSET_FROMLEFT, 0, 0)
	searchBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, SEARCH_TEXT_OFFSET_FROMLEFT, 0, 0)
	searchBox.Parent = searchFrame

	local xButton = NewGui("TextButton", "X")
	xButton.Text = "x"
	xButton.TextColor3 = SLOT_EQUIP_COLOR
	xButton.TextSize = 24
	xButton.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Bottom
	xButton.BackgroundColor3 = SEARCH_BACKGROUND_COLOR
	xButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0
	xButton.Size = UDim2.new(
		0, searchFrame.Size.Y.Offset - SEARCH_BUFFER * 2,
		0, searchFrame.Size.Y.Offset - SEARCH_BUFFER * 2
	)
	xButton.Position = UDim2.new(
		1, -xButton.Size.X.Offset - SEARCH_BUFFER * 2,
		0.5, -xButton.Size.Y.Offset / 2
	)
	xButton.ZIndex = 0
	xButton.Visible = false
	xButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
	xButton.Parent = searchFrame

	local function search()
		local terms = {}
		for word in searchBox.Text:gmatch("%S+") do
			terms[word:lower()] = true
		end

		local matches = {}
		for i = NumberOfHotbarSlots + 1, #Slots do
			local slot = Slots[i]
			if slot then
				table.insert(matches, {slot, slot:CheckTerms(terms)})
				slot.Frame.Visible = false
				slot.Frame.Parent = InventoryFrame
			end
		end

		table.sort(matches, function(a, b)
			return a[2] &gt; b[2]
		end)

		ViewingSearchResults = true
		local hitCount = 0

		for _, data in ipairs(matches) do
			local slot, hits = data[1], data[2]
			if hits &gt; 0 then
				slot.Frame.Visible = true
				slot.Frame.Parent = UIGridFrame
				slot.Frame.LayoutOrder = NumberOfHotbarSlots + hitCount
				hitCount += 1
			end
		end

		ScrollingFrame.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
		UpdateScrollingFrameCanvasSize()
		xButton.ZIndex = 3
	end

	local function clearResults()
		if xButton.ZIndex &gt; 0 then
			ViewingSearchResults = false

			for i = NumberOfHotbarSlots + 1, #Slots do
				local slot = Slots[i]
				if slot then
					slot.Frame.LayoutOrder = slot.Index
					slot.Frame.Parent = UIGridFrame
					slot.Frame.Visible = slot.Tool ~= nil
				end
			end

			xButton.ZIndex = 0
		end

		UpdateScrollingFrameCanvasSize()
	end

	local function reset()
		clearResults()
		searchBox.Text = SEARCH_TEXT
	end

	searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		local text = searchBox.Text

		if text == "" or text == SEARCH_TEXT then
			clearResults()
		else
			search()
		end

		xButton.Visible = text ~= "" and text ~= SEARCH_TEXT
	end)

	searchBox.Focused:Connect(function()
		if searchBox.Text == SEARCH_TEXT then
			searchBox.Text = ""
		end
	end)

	xButton.Activated:Connect(reset)

	searchBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
		if enterPressed then
			search()
		elseif searchBox.Text == "" then
			searchBox.Text = SEARCH_TEXT
		end
	end)

	BackpackScript.StateChanged.Event:Connect(function(isNowOpen)
		xButton.Modal = isNowOpen
		if not isNowOpen then
			reset()
		end
	end)

	HotkeyFns[Enum.KeyCode.Escape.Value] = function(isProcessed)
		if isProcessed then
			reset()
		elseif InventoryFrame.Visible and BackpackScript.OpenClose then
			BackpackScript.OpenClose()
		end
	end

	local function detectGamepad(lastInputType)
		searchFrame.Visible = not (
			lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1
				and not UserInputService.VREnabled
		)
	end

	UserInputService.LastInputTypeChanged:Connect(detectGamepad)
end

-- Inventory open/close.
do
	local function removeHotbarSlot(_, state)
		if state ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin or not GuiService.SelectedCoreObject then
			return
		end

		for i = 1, NumberOfHotbarSlots do
			local slot = Slots[i]
			if slot and slot.Frame == GuiService.SelectedCoreObject and slot.Tool then
				slot:MoveToInventory()
				return
			end
		end
	end

	local function openClose()
		if not next(Dragging) then
			InventoryFrame.Visible = not InventoryFrame.Visible
			local isNowOpen = InventoryFrame.Visible

			AdjustHotbarFrames()
			HotbarFrame.Active = not isNowOpen

			for i = 1, NumberOfHotbarSlots do
				local slot = Slots[i]
				if slot then
					slot:SetClickability(not isNowOpen)
				end
			end
		end

		if InventoryFrame.Visible then
			if GamepadEnabled then
				if GAMEPAD_INPUT_TYPES[UserInputService:GetLastInputType()] then
					resizeGamepadHintsFrame()
					gamepadHintsFrame.Visible = not UserInputService.VREnabled
				end
				enableGamepadInventoryControl()
			end

			if BackpackPanel and VRService.VREnabled then
				pcall(function()
					BackpackPanel:SetVisible(true)
					BackpackPanel:RequestPositionUpdate()
				end)
			end
		else
			if GamepadEnabled then
				gamepadHintsFrame.Visible = false
			end
			disableGamepadInventoryControl()
		end

		if InventoryFrame.Visible then
			pcall(function()
				ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(
					"RBXRemoveSlot",
					removeHotbarSlot,
					false,
					Enum.KeyCode.ButtonX
				)
			end)
		else
			pcall(function()
				ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction("RBXRemoveSlot")
			end)
		end

		BackpackScript.IsOpen = InventoryFrame.Visible
		BackpackScript.StateChanged:Fire(InventoryFrame.Visible)
	end

	HotkeyFns[ARROW_HOTKEY] = openClose
	BackpackScript.OpenClose = openClose
end

-- Player and character initialization.
while not Player do
	Players.PlayerAdded:Wait()
	Player = Players.LocalPlayer
end

Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(OnCharacterAdded)
if Player.Character then
	OnCharacterAdded(Player.Character)
end

for i = 0, 9 do
	table.insert(HotkeyStrings, tostring(i))
end
table.insert(HotkeyStrings, ARROW_HOTKEY_STRING)

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(OnInputBegan)
UserInputService.TextBoxFocused:Connect(function()
	TextBoxFocused = true
end)
UserInputService.TextBoxFocusReleased:Connect(function()
	TextBoxFocused = false
end)

HotkeyFns[DROP_HOTKEY_VALUE] = function()
	if ActiveHopper then
		UnequipAllTools()
	end
end

UserInputService.Changed:Connect(OnUISChanged)
OnUISChanged("KeyboardEnabled")

if UserInputService:GetGamepadConnected(Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1) then
	gamepadConnected()
end

UserInputService.GamepadConnected:Connect(function(gamepadEnum)
	if gamepadEnum == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 then
		gamepadConnected()
	end
end)

UserInputService.GamepadDisconnected:Connect(function(gamepadEnum)
	if gamepadEnum == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 then
		gamepadDisconnected()
	end
end)

function BackpackScript:TopbarEnabledChanged(enabled)
	TopbarEnabled = enabled == true
	OnCoreGuiChanged(
		Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack,
		isCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack)
	)
end

local coreGuiChangedSignal
pcall(function()
	coreGuiChangedSignal = StarterGui.CoreGuiChangedSignal
end)

if coreGuiChangedSignal then
	coreGuiChangedSignal:Connect(OnCoreGuiChanged)
end

local backpackType = Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack
OnCoreGuiChanged(backpackType, isCoreGuiEnabled(backpackType))

-- VR is optional. Desktop initialization must not fail because the old
-- Panel3D or VRHub modules are unavailable.
local VRConnections = {}

local function disconnectVRConnections()
	for _, connection in ipairs(VRConnections) do
		pcall(function()
			connection:Disconnect()
		end)
	end
	table.clear(VRConnections)
end

local function safeVRRequire(name)
	local vrFolder = Modules:FindFirstChild("VR")
	local moduleScript = vrFolder and vrFolder:FindFirstChild(name)

	if not moduleScript or not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		return nil, "missing Modules.VR." .. name
	end

	local ok, result = pcall(require, moduleScript)
	if not ok then
		return nil, tostring(result)
	end

	return result
end

local function OnVREnabled()
	disconnectVRConnections()

	IsVR = VRService.VREnabled
	VRInventorySelector.Visible = IsVR

	if not IsVR then
		if BackpackPanel then
			pcall(function()
				BackpackPanel:SetVisible(false)
				BackpackPanel:LinkTo(nil)
			end)
			BackpackPanel = nil
		end

		MainFrame.Parent = RobloxGui
		OpenInventoryButton.Parent = nil
		CloseInventoryButton.Parent = nil
		ScrollUpInventoryButton.Parent = nil
		ScrollDownInventoryButton.Parent = nil
		ScrollingFrame.ScrollingEnabled = true

		for _, slot in ipairs(Slots) do
			slot:SetClickability(true)
		end

		NumberOfInventoryRows = IS_PHONE and INVENTORY_ROWS_MINI or INVENTORY_ROWS_FULL
		SetNumberOfHotbarSlots(IS_PHONE and HOTBAR_SLOTS_MINI or HOTBAR_SLOTS_FULL)
		return
	end

	local Panel3D, panelErr = safeVRRequire("Panel3D")
	if not Panel3D then
		warn("[BackpackScript] VR panel unavailable; desktop backpack remains active: "
			.. tostring(panelErr))
		return
	end

	local VRHub, hubErr = safeVRRequire("VRHub")
	if not VRHub then
		warn("[BackpackScript] VRHub unavailable: " .. tostring(hubErr))
		return
	end

	local panel
	local panelOk, panelResult = pcall(function()
		VRHub:RegisterModule(BackpackScript)
		return Panel3D.Get(BackpackScript.ModuleName)
	end)

	if panelOk then
		panel = panelResult
	else
		warn("[BackpackScript] Could not create VR backpack panel: "
			.. tostring(panelResult))
		return
	end

	if not panel then
		warn("[BackpackScript] Panel3D.Get returned no backpack panel")
		return
	end

	BackpackPanel = panel

	local slotsToStuds = (ICON_SIZE + ICON_BUFFER) / VR_PANEL_RESOLUTION
	local inventoryOpenStudSize = Vector2.new(6.25, 7.2) * slotsToStuds
	local inventoryClosedStudSize = Vector2.new(6.25, 2) * slotsToStuds
	local inventoryOpenPanelCF = CFrame.new(0, 0.6, 0)
	local inventoryClosedPanelCF = CFrame.new(0, -1, 0)

	pcall(function()
		BackpackPanel:ResizeStuds(
			inventoryClosedStudSize.X,
			inventoryClosedStudSize.Y,
			VR_PANEL_RESOLUTION
		)
		BackpackPanel:SetType(
			Panel3D.Type.Standard,
			{CFrame = inventoryClosedPanelCF}
		)
		BackpackPanel:RequestPositionUpdate()
	end)

	function BackpackPanel:CalculateTransparency()
		if InventoryFrame.Visible then
			return 0
		end

		local elapsed = tick() - TimeOfLastToolChange
		local transparency = math.clamp(elapsed / VR_FADE_TIME, 0, 1)

		if transparency &gt;= 1 and self:IsVisible() and not InventoryFrame.Visible then
			self:SetVisible(false)
		end

		return transparency
	end

	function BackpackPanel:PreUpdate()
		self.localCF = InventoryFrame.Visible
			and inventoryOpenPanelCF
			or inventoryClosedPanelCF
	end

	function BackpackPanel:OnUpdate()
		if not InventoryFrame.Visible then
			self:ResizeStuds(
				(FullHotbarSlots + 0.25) * slotsToStuds,
				inventoryClosedStudSize.Y,
				VR_PANEL_RESOLUTION
			)
		end

		for _, slot in ipairs(Slots) do
			slot:UpdateEquipView()
		end

		OpenInventoryButton.ImageTransparency = self.transparency
		CloseInventoryButton.ImageTransparency = self.transparency
	end

	MainFrame.Parent = BackpackPanel:GetGUI()
	OpenInventoryButton.Parent = MainFrame
	CloseInventoryButton.Parent = InventoryFrame
	ScrollUpInventoryButton.Parent = InventoryFrame
	ScrollDownInventoryButton.Parent = InventoryFrame
	ScrollingFrame.ScrollingEnabled = false

	table.insert(VRConnections, BackpackScript.StateChanged.Event:Connect(function(isNowOpen)
		if isNowOpen then
			pcall(function()
				VRHub:FireModuleOpened(BackpackScript.ModuleName)
				BackpackPanel:ResizeStuds(
					inventoryOpenStudSize.X,
					inventoryOpenStudSize.Y,
					VR_PANEL_RESOLUTION
				)
				BackpackPanel:SetCanFade(false)
			end)
		else
			pcall(function()
				VRHub:FireModuleClosed(BackpackScript.ModuleName)
				BackpackPanel:ResizeStuds(
					inventoryClosedStudSize.X,
					inventoryClosedStudSize.Y,
					VR_PANEL_RESOLUTION
				)
				BackpackPanel:SetCanFade(true)
			end)
		end
	end))

	if VRHub.ModuleOpened and VRHub.ModuleOpened.Event then
		table.insert(VRConnections, VRHub.ModuleOpened.Event:Connect(function(moduleName)
			if moduleName == BackpackScript.ModuleName then
				return
			end

			local ok, openedModule = pcall(function()
				return VRHub:GetModule(moduleName)
			end)

			if ok and openedModule and openedModule.VRIsExclusive then
				pcall(function()
					BackpackPanel:SetVisible(EvaluateBackpackPanelVisibility(false))
				end)

				if InventoryFrame.Visible and BackpackScript.OpenClose then
					BackpackScript.OpenClose()
				end
			end
		end))
	end

	if VRHub.ModuleClosed and VRHub.ModuleClosed.Event then
		table.insert(VRConnections, VRHub.ModuleClosed.Event:Connect(function(moduleName)
			if moduleName ~= BackpackScript.ModuleName then
				pcall(function()
					BackpackPanel:SetVisible(EvaluateBackpackPanelVisibility(true))
				end)
			end
		end))
	end

	for _, slot in ipairs(Slots) do
		slot:SetClickability(false)
	end

	NumberOfInventoryRows = INVENTORY_ROWS_VR
	SetNumberOfHotbarSlots(HOTBAR_SLOTS_VR)
end

VRService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("VREnabled"):Connect(OnVREnabled)
OnVREnabled()

print("[BackpackScript] Backpack v5.1 initialized under " .. RobloxGui:GetFullName())

return BackpackScript</ProtectedString>

                                <BinaryString name="Tags" />
                            </Properties>
                        </Item>
                        <Item class="ModuleScript" referent="RBX530">

                                <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: SocialUtil.lua
    Yeku compatibility patch: safe thumbnail retries and timeout cleanup.
]]

local SocialUtil = {}
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local THUMBNAIL_SIZE_MAP = {
	[Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size48x48] = 48,
	[Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size180x180] = 180,
	[Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size420x420] = 420,
	[Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size60x60] = 60,
	[Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100] = 100,
	[Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size150x150] = 150,
	[Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size352x352] = 352,
}

local THUMBNAIL_FALLBACK_URLS = {
	[Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot] = "https://www.roblox.com/headshot-thumbnail/image?width=%d&amp;height=%d&amp;format=png&amp;userId=%d",
	[Enum.ThumbnailType.AvatarBust] = "https://www.roblox.com/bust-thumbnail/image?width=%d&amp;height=%d&amp;format=png&amp;userId=%d",
	[Enum.ThumbnailType.AvatarThumbnail] = "https://www.roblox.com/avatar-thumbnail/image?width=%d&amp;height=%d&amp;format=png&amp;userId=%d",
}

local GET_PLAYER_IMAGE_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT = 5
local DEFAULT_THUMBNAIL_SIZE = Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100
local DEFAULT_THUMBNAIL_TYPE = Enum.ThumbnailType.AvatarThumbnail
local GET_USER_THUMBNAIL_ASYNC_RETRY_TIME = 1

pcall(function()
	local value = tonumber(settings():GetFVariable("GetUserThumbnailAsyncRetryTime"))
	if value and value &gt; 0 then GET_USER_THUMBNAIL_ASYNC_RETRY_TIME = value end
end)
pcall(function()
	local value = tonumber(settings():GetFVariable("GetPlayerImageDefaultTimeout"))
	if value and value &gt; 0 then GET_PLAYER_IMAGE_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT = value end
end)

function SocialUtil.GetFallbackPlayerImageUrl(userId, thumbnailSize, thumbnailType)
	local sizeNumber = THUMBNAIL_SIZE_MAP[thumbnailSize] or THUMBNAIL_SIZE_MAP[DEFAULT_THUMBNAIL_SIZE]
	local template = THUMBNAIL_FALLBACK_URLS[thumbnailType] or THUMBNAIL_FALLBACK_URLS[DEFAULT_THUMBNAIL_TYPE]
	return template:format(sizeNumber, sizeNumber, tonumber(userId) or 0)
end

function SocialUtil.GetPlayerImage(userId, thumbnailSize, thumbnailType, timeOut)
	userId = tonumber(userId)
	if not userId then return "" end
	thumbnailSize = thumbnailSize or DEFAULT_THUMBNAIL_SIZE
	thumbnailType = thumbnailType or DEFAULT_THUMBNAIL_TYPE
	timeOut = tonumber(timeOut) or GET_PLAYER_IMAGE_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT
	timeOut = math.max(0, timeOut)

	local fallback = SocialUtil.GetFallbackPlayerImageUrl(userId, thumbnailSize, thumbnailType)
	local finished = false
	local imageUrl = fallback
	local signal = Instance.new("BindableEvent")

	task.delay(timeOut, function()
		if not finished then
			finished = true
			signal:Fire()
		end
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		repeat
			if finished then break end
			local ok, url, isFinal = pcall(function()
				return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(userId, thumbnailType, thumbnailSize)
			end)
			if ok and type(url) == "string" and url ~= "" then
				imageUrl = url
				if isFinal then
					finished = true
					signal:Fire()
					break
				end
			end
			task.wait(GET_USER_THUMBNAIL_ASYNC_RETRY_TIME)
		until finished
	end)

	if not finished then signal.Event:Wait() end
	pcall(function() signal:Destroy() end)
	return imageUrl or fallback
end

return SocialUtil
</ProtectedString>
                                <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-434</int64>
                                <BinaryString name="Tags" />
                            </Properties>
                        </Item>
                        <Item class="ModuleScript" referent="RBX532">
                            <Properties>
                                <BinaryString name="AttributesSerialize" />
                                <SecurityCapabilities name="Capabilities">0</SecurityCapabilities>
                                <bool name="DefinesCapabilities">false</bool>

                                </ContentId>
                                <string name="Name">RobloxTranslator</string>
                                <string name="ScriptGuid">{49A9C676-91EA-4918-8ECB-6F0D76E6B231}</string>
                                <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: RobloxTranslator.lua
    Yeku CoreScript localization compatibility layer.

    The modern client may expose more than one CoreScript LocalizationTable.
    A GuiBase2d can select only one RootLocalizationTable, so this module merges
    the tables (all locale Values, not just the current locale) into one local
    table and attaches it to CoreGui ScreenGuis. Key lookup and source lookup
    therefore see the complete language set supplied by Roblox CoreScripts.

    Dynamic player names, player/chat messages, chat bubbles and editable input
    are deliberately excluded from automatic localization.
]]

local LocalizationService = game:GetService("LocalizationService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

local RobloxTranslator = {}
local cachedLocale = nil
local cachedTranslator = nil
local cachedTable = nil
local sourceTablesCache = nil
local attachedRoots = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local localizedTextObjects = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

local function getExperienceLocaleId()
    -- Yeku 2015-2017 intentionally ships an English-only interface.
    return "en-us"
end

local function getLocalizationTables()
    local result = {}
    local seen = {}
    local ok, tables = pcall(function()
        return LocalizationService:GetCorescriptLocalizations()
    end)
    if ok then
        for _, tableObject in pairs(tables or {}) do
            if typeof(tableObject) == "Instance" and tableObject:IsA("LocalizationTable")
                and not seen[tableObject] then
                seen[tableObject] = true
                table.insert(result, tableObject)
            end
        end
    end

    -- Compatibility with clients that expose the legacy CoreGui child.
    local legacyTable = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("CoreScriptLocalization")
    if legacyTable and legacyTable:IsA("LocalizationTable") and not seen[legacyTable] then
        table.insert(result, legacyTable)
    end
    return result
end

local function copyValues(values)
    local result = {}
    if type(values) == "table" then
        for localeId, translation in pairs(values) do
            if type(localeId) == "string" and type(translation) == "string" then
                result[localeId] = translation
            end
        end
    end
    return result
end

local function mergeLocalizationTables(sourceTables)
    if #sourceTables == 0 then
        return nil
    end
    if #sourceTables == 1 then
        return sourceTables[1]
    end

    local existing = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("YekuCoreScriptLocalizationMerged")
    local merged = existing
    if not merged or not merged:IsA("LocalizationTable") then
        local createOK, created = pcall(function()
            return Instance.new("LocalizationTable")
        end)
        if not createOK or not created then
            return sourceTables[1]
        end
        merged = created
        merged.Name = "YekuCoreScriptLocalizationMerged"
    end
    pcall(function()
        merged.SourceLocaleId = sourceTables[1].SourceLocaleId
    end)

    local entries = {}
    local byIdentity = {}
    local readableTableCount = 0
    for _, sourceTable in ipairs(sourceTables) do
        local ok, sourceEntries = pcall(function()
            return sourceTable:GetEntries()
        end)
        if ok and type(sourceEntries) == "table" then
            readableTableCount += 1
            for _, sourceEntry in ipairs(sourceEntries) do
                if type(sourceEntry) == "table" then
                    local key = type(sourceEntry.Key) == "string" and sourceEntry.Key or ""
                    local source = type(sourceEntry.Source) == "string" and sourceEntry.Source or ""
                    local context = type(sourceEntry.Context) == "string" and sourceEntry.Context or ""
                    if key ~= "" or source ~= "" then
                        local identity
                        if key ~= "" then
                            identity = "K\0" .. key
                        else
                            identity = "S\0" .. source .. "\0" .. context
                        end
                        local target = byIdentity[identity]
                        if not target then
                            target = {
                                Key = key,
                                Source = source,
                                Context = context,
                                Values = {},
                            }
                            if type(sourceEntry.Example) == "string" then
                                target.Example = sourceEntry.Example
                            end
                            byIdentity[identity] = target
                            table.insert(entries, target)
                        end
                        -- Keep the first translation on conflicts; fill in any
                        -- missing locales from later Roblox CoreScript tables.
                        for localeId, translation in pairs(copyValues(sourceEntry.Values)) do
                            if target.Values[localeId] == nil or target.Values[localeId] == "" then
                                target.Values[localeId] = translation
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local setOK = false
    if readableTableCount &gt; 0 then
        setOK = pcall(function()
            merged:SetEntries(entries)
        end)
    end
    if setOK then
        if merged.Parent ~= CoreGui then
            merged.Parent = CoreGui
        end
        return merged
    end

    if not existing then
        merged:Destroy()
    end
    return sourceTables[1]
end

local function getCoreScriptLocalizationTable()
    if cachedTable and cachedTable.Parent then
        return cachedTable
    end
    if not sourceTablesCache or #sourceTablesCache == 0 then
        sourceTablesCache = getLocalizationTables()
    end
    cachedTable = mergeLocalizationTables(sourceTablesCache)
    return cachedTable
end

local function getTranslator()
    local localeId = getExperienceLocaleId()
    if cachedTranslator and cachedLocale == localeId then
        return cachedTranslator
    end
    cachedLocale = localeId
    cachedTranslator = nil

    local localizationTable = getCoreScriptLocalizationTable()
    if localizationTable then
        local ok, translator = pcall(function()
            return localizationTable:GetTranslator(localeId)
        end)
        if ok and translator then
            cachedTranslator = translator
        end
    end

    -- If merging is unavailable on a particular client, retain the ordered
    -- native tables as a key-lookup fallback rather than silently losing them.
    if not cachedTranslator then
        for _, tableObject in ipairs(sourceTablesCache or getLocalizationTables()) do
            local ok, translator = pcall(function()
                return tableObject:GetTranslator(localeId)
            end)
            if ok and translator then
                cachedTranslator = translator
                break
            end
        end
    end
    return cachedTranslator
end

local function isDynamicText(instance)
    local skipNames = {
        RobloxLoadingGui = true,
        Chat = true,
        ChatBar = true,
        ChatMessage = true,
        SystemChatMessage = true,
        LegacyChatMessage = true,
        MessageText = true,
        SpeakerName = true,
        ChannelName = true,
        ChatTag = true,
        PlayerName = true,
        Username = true,
        UserName = true,
        DisplayName = true,
        MessageContainer = true,
        ChatWindow = true,
        ChatWindowScroll = true,
        ChatModeText = true,
        BubbleChat = true,
        BubbleChatBillboard = true,
        BubbleText = true,
        TeamName = true,
        StatText = true,
        StatName = true,
        ColumnName = true,
        ColumnValue = true,
        TextBox = true,
    }
    local current = instance
    while current and current ~= CoreGui do
        if skipNames[current.Name] then
            return true
        end
        current = current.Parent
    end
    return false
end

local function translateStaticSource(_context, sourceText)
    -- This year pack intentionally keeps 2015-2017 CoreGui copy in English.
    return sourceText
end

local function applyLocalizedText(instance, info)
    if not instance or not instance.Parent or not info then
        return
    end
    local sourceText = info.sourceText
    if type(sourceText) ~= "string" or sourceText == "" then
        return
    end
    local translated = translateStaticSource(instance, sourceText)
    info.lastRendered = translated
    if instance.Text ~= translated then
        info.applying = true
        instance.Text = translated
        info.applying = false
    end
end

local function configureTextObject(instance)
    if not instance or not instance:IsA("GuiBase2d") then
        return
    end
    if isDynamicText(instance) or instance:IsA("TextBox") then
        pcall(function() instance.AutoLocalize = false end)
        return
    end
    if not (instance:IsA("TextLabel") or instance:IsA("TextButton")) then
        return
    end

    -- Roblox AutoLocalize uses RobloxLocaleId, which can differ from the
    -- player's experience-language Player.LocaleId. We localize this object
    -- explicitly with the player-locale Translator instead.
    pcall(function() instance.AutoLocalize = false end)

    local info = localizedTextObjects[instance]
    if not info then
        info = {
            sourceText = instance.Text,
            lastRendered = nil,
            applying = false,
            connection = nil,
        }
        localizedTextObjects[instance] = info
        info.connection = instance:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            if info.applying then
                return
            end
            local currentText = instance.Text
            if currentText == info.lastRendered then
                return
            end
            info.sourceText = currentText
            applyLocalizedText(instance, info)
        end)
    elseif instance.Text ~= info.lastRendered then
        info.sourceText = instance.Text
    end
    applyLocalizedText(instance, info)
end

local function refreshAllLocalizedText()
    for instance, info in pairs(localizedTextObjects) do
        if instance and instance.Parent then
            applyLocalizedText(instance, info)
        else
            localizedTextObjects[instance] = nil
        end
    end
end
local function attachScreenGui(screenGui)
    if not screenGui or not screenGui:IsA("ScreenGui") or attachedRoots[screenGui] then
        return
    end

    -- Do not attach Roblox locale tables to 2015-2017 CoreGui. These packs
    -- intentionally keep the complete legacy interface in English.
    pcall(function() screenGui.RootLocalizationTable = nil end)
    pcall(function() screenGui.AutoLocalize = false end)

    for _, descendant in ipairs(screenGui:GetDescendants()) do
        configureTextObject(descendant)
    end
    screenGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(configureTextObject)
    attachedRoots[screenGui] = true
end

function RobloxTranslator:AttachCoreGui()
    for _, descendant in ipairs(CoreGui:GetDescendants()) do
        if descendant:IsA("ScreenGui") then
            attachScreenGui(descendant)
        end
    end
end

function RobloxTranslator:GetLocalizationTable()
    return getCoreScriptLocalizationTable()
end

function RobloxTranslator:GetLocalizationTables()
    return sourceTablesCache or getLocalizationTables()
end

function RobloxTranslator:GetTranslator()
    return getTranslator()
end

local function getEnglishSourceForKey(key)
    key = tostring(key or "")
    for _, localizationTable in ipairs(sourceTablesCache or getLocalizationTables()) do
        local ok, entries = pcall(function() return localizationTable:GetEntries() end)
        if ok and type(entries) == "table" then
            for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
                if type(entry) == "table" and entry.Key == key
                    and type(entry.Source) == "string" and entry.Source ~= "" then
                    return entry.Source
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return key
end

function RobloxTranslator:FormatByKey(key, args, fallback)
    local result = fallback ~= nil and tostring(fallback) or getEnglishSourceForKey(key)
    if type(args) == "table" then
        for name, value in pairs(args) do
            local token = "{" .. tostring(name) .. "}"
            result = string.gsub(result, token, function() return tostring(value) end)
        end
    end
    return result
end

function RobloxTranslator:TranslateText(_context, sourceText, fallback)
    if type(sourceText) ~= "string" then return fallback or sourceText end
    return fallback or sourceText
end

local function waitForLocalPlayer()
    if Players.LocalPlayer == nil then
        pcall(function()
            Players:GetPropertyChangedSignal("LocalPlayer"):Wait()
        end)
    end
end
waitForLocalPlayer()
RobloxTranslator:AttachCoreGui()

CoreGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
    if descendant:IsA("ScreenGui") then
        task.defer(attachScreenGui, descendant)
    elseif descendant:IsA("LocalizationTable") then
        sourceTablesCache = nil
        cachedTable = nil
        cachedTranslator = nil
        task.defer(function()
            refreshAllLocalizedText()
            RobloxTranslator:AttachCoreGui()
        end)
    else
        configureTextObject(descendant)
    end
end)

local function refreshExperienceLocale()
    -- Legacy 2015-2017 UI remains English regardless of Player.LocaleId.
    cachedLocale = nil
    cachedTranslator = nil
end

local playerLocaleConnection = nil
local function watchExperienceLocale()
    if playerLocaleConnection then
        playerLocaleConnection:Disconnect()
        playerLocaleConnection = nil
    end
    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    if localPlayer then
        pcall(function()
            playerLocaleConnection = localPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("LocaleId"):Connect(refreshExperienceLocale)
        end)
    end
end

watchExperienceLocale()
pcall(function()
    Players:GetPropertyChangedSignal("LocalPlayer"):Connect(function()
        watchExperienceLocale()
        refreshExperienceLocale()
    end)
end)
-- Fallback only for startup before LocalPlayer exists; Player.LocaleId is
-- authoritative once it is available.
pcall(function()
    LocalizationService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("RobloxLocaleId"):Connect(function()
        if not Players.LocalPlayer then
            refreshExperienceLocale()
        end
    end)
end)
return RobloxTranslator

    Filename: PromptCreator.lua

local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local ContextActionService = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")
